// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/lightning/bech32.js
var CHARSET = "qpzry9x8gf2tvdw0s3jn54khce6mua7l";
var GENERATOR = [996825010, 642813549, 513874426, 1027748829, 705979059];
function polymod(values) {
  let chk = 1;
  for (const value of values) {
    const top = chk >>> 25;
    chk = (chk & 33554431) << 5 ^ value;
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++)
      if (top >>> i & 1)
        chk ^= GENERATOR[i];
  }
  return chk >>> 0;
}
function expandHrp(hrp) {
  const high = [];
  const low = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < hrp.length; i++) {
    const code = hrp.charCodeAt(i);
    high.push(code >> 5);
    low.push(code & 31);
  }
  return [...high, 0, ...low];
}
var BECH32 = 1;
var BECH32M = 734539939;
function decodeBech32(input, constant = BECH32) {
  for (let i = 0; i < input.length; i++) {
    const code = input.charCodeAt(i);
    if (code < 33 || code > 126)
      return null;
  }
  if (/[a-z]/.test(input) && /[A-Z]/.test(input))
    return null;
  const text = asciiLower(input);
  const separator = text.lastIndexOf("1");
  if (separator < 1 || separator + 7 > text.length)
    return null;
  const hrp = text.slice(0, separator);
  const values = [];
  for (const char of text.slice(separator + 1)) {
    const value = CHARSET.indexOf(char);
    if (value === -1)
      return null;
    values.push(value);
  }
  if (polymod([...expandHrp(hrp), ...values]) !== constant)
    return null;
  return { hrp, words: values.slice(0, -6) };
}
function wordsToBytes(words) {
  const bytes = [];
  let accumulator = 0;
  let bits = 0;
  for (const word of words) {
    accumulator = (accumulator << 5 | word) & 4095;
    bits += 5;
    if (bits >= 8) {
      bits -= 8;
      bytes.push(accumulator >> bits & 255);
    }
  }
  return Uint8Array.from(bytes);
}
function wordsToInteger(words) {
  let value = 0;
  for (const word of words)
    value = value * 32 + word;
  return value;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/lightning/bolt11.js
var HRP = /^ln(bcrt|bc|tbs|tb)(\d+)?([munp])?$/;
var MSAT_PER_UNIT = {
  "": 100000000000n,
  m: 100000000n,
  u: 100000n,
  n: 100n
};
var TIMESTAMP_WORDS = 7;
var SIGNATURE_WORDS = 104;
var TAG_PAYMENT_HASH = 1;
var TAG_DESCRIPTION_HASH = 23;
var TAG_EXPIRY = 6;
var HASH_WORDS = 52;
var DEFAULT_EXPIRY_SECONDS = 3600;
function amountMsat(digits, multiplier) {
  if (digits === void 0)
    return multiplier === void 0 ? null : invalidAmount();
  if (digits.length > 1 && digits.startsWith("0"))
    return invalidAmount();
  if (digits.length > 22)
    return invalidAmount();
  const value = BigInt(digits);
  let msat2;
  if (multiplier === "p") {
    if (value % 10n !== 0n)
      return invalidAmount();
    msat2 = value / 10n;
  } else {
    msat2 = value * MSAT_PER_UNIT[multiplier ?? ""];
  }
  return msat2 > MAX_BOLT11_AMOUNT_MSAT ? invalidAmount() : msat2;
}
function invalidAmount() {
  throw new Error("Invalid invoice amount");
}
function decodeBolt11(input) {
  const decoded = decodeBech32(input);
  if (!decoded)
    return null;
  const hrp = HRP.exec(decoded.hrp);
  if (!hrp)
    return null;
  let amount;
  try {
    amount = amountMsat(hrp[2], hrp[3]);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  const words = decoded.words;
  if (words.length < TIMESTAMP_WORDS + SIGNATURE_WORDS)
    return null;
  const timestamp = wordsToInteger(words.slice(0, TIMESTAMP_WORDS));
  const fields = words.slice(TIMESTAMP_WORDS, words.length - SIGNATURE_WORDS);
  let paymentHash = null;
  let descriptionHash = null;
  let expirySeconds = null;
  let i = 0;
  while (i < fields.length) {
    if (i + 3 > fields.length)
      return null;
    const tag = fields[i];
    const length = fields[i + 1] * 32 + fields[i + 2];
    const data2 = fields.slice(i + 3, i + 3 + length);
    if (data2.length !== length)
      return null;
    if (tag === TAG_PAYMENT_HASH && length === HASH_WORDS && paymentHash === null) {
      paymentHash = bytesToHex(wordsToBytes(data2));
    } else if (tag === TAG_DESCRIPTION_HASH && length === HASH_WORDS && descriptionHash === null) {
      descriptionHash = bytesToHex(wordsToBytes(data2));
    } else if (tag === TAG_EXPIRY) {
      if (length > MAX_BOLT11_EXPIRY_WORDS)
        return null;
      expirySeconds = wordsToInteger(data2);
    }
    i += 3 + length;
  }
  if (paymentHash === null)
    return null;
  return {
    network: hrp[1],
    amountMsat: amount,
    timestamp,
    expirySeconds: expirySeconds ?? DEFAULT_EXPIRY_SECONDS,
    paymentHash,
    descriptionHash
  };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/json.js
var MAX_JSON_DEPTH = 64;
function readJson(text) {
  if (fewOpenings(text))
    return JSON.parse(text);
  let depth = 0;
  let inString = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) {
    const c = text.charCodeAt(i);
    if (inString) {
      if (c === 92)
        i += 1;
      else if (c === 34)
        inString = false;
    } else if (c === 34) {
      inString = true;
    } else if (c === 91 || c === 123) {
      depth += 1;
      if (depth > MAX_JSON_DEPTH) {
        throw new SyntaxError(`More than ${MAX_JSON_DEPTH} arrays and objects nest in the JSON`);
      }
    } else if (c === 93 || c === 125) {
      depth -= 1;
    }
  }
  return JSON.parse(text);
}
function fewOpenings(text) {
  let count = 0;
  for (const opening of ["[", "{"]) {
    for (let at = text.indexOf(opening); at !== -1; at = text.indexOf(opening, at + 1)) {
      count += 1;
      if (count > MAX_JSON_DEPTH)
        return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/nostr/record.js
var NewerRecordError = class extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super("Record of a newer format");
    this.name = "NewerRecordError";
  }
};
function sealRecord({ type, value, signer, encryptionKey, address, createdAt, tags = [], random = secureRandomBytes }) {
  const text = JSON.stringify({ t: type, v: value });
  const length = utf8ToBytes(text).length;
  const padded = text + " ".repeat(Math.ceil(length / RECORD_BLOCK_BYTES) * RECORD_BLOCK_BYTES - length);
  return signEvent({
    kind: RECORD_KIND,
    created_at: createdAt,
    tags: [["d", address], ...tags],
    content: nip44Encrypt(padded, encryptionKey, random)
  }, signer, random);
}
function openRecord(value, options) {
  const claimed = value;
  if (claimed?.kind !== RECORD_KIND || claimed.pubkey !== options.author || typeof claimed.content !== "string" || !Array.isArray(claimed.tags) || !claimed.tags.some((tag) => Array.isArray(tag) && tag[0] === "d" && tag[1] === options.address)) {
    return null;
  }
  let payload = null;
  let newer = false;
  try {
    payload = readContent(claimed.content, options);
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof NewerRecordError))
      return null;
    newer = true;
  }
  if (!newer && payload === void 0)
    return null;
  if (!isValidEvent(value))
    return null;
  if (newer)
    return { status: "newer", event: value };
  try {
    const parsed = options.parse(payload, value);
    return parsed === null ? null : { status: "ok", event: value, value: parsed };
  } catch (error) {
    return error instanceof NewerRecordError ? { status: "newer", event: value } : null;
  }
}
function readContent(content, options) {
  let text;
  try {
    text = nip44Decrypt(content, options.encryptionKey);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof UnknownNip44VersionError)
      throw new NewerRecordError();
    return void 0;
  }
  let envelope;
  try {
    envelope = readJson(text);
  } catch {
    return void 0;
  }
  if (!isObject(envelope) || !("v" in envelope))
    return void 0;
  return envelope.t === options.type ? envelope.v : void 0;
}
function recordsThatOpen(keyOf) {
  const verified = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  return (value) => {
    if (!isObject(value) || value.kind !== RECORD_KIND || typeof value.content !== "string" || typeof value.pubkey !== "string") {
      return void 0;
    }
    const key = keyOf(dTagOf(value), value.pubkey);
    if (key === null)
      return void 0;
    try {
      nip44Decrypt(value.content, key);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof UnknownNip44VersionError))
        return void 0;
    }
    if (!hasValidId(value))
      return void 0;
    const copy = `${value.id}/${value.sig}`;
    let valid = verified.get(copy);
    if (valid === void 0)
      verified.set(copy, valid = signatureChecks(value));
    return valid ? nip01Members(value) : void 0;
  };
}
function dTagOf(value) {
  const tags = isObject(value) ? value.tags : void 0;
  const tag = Array.isArray(tags) ? tags.find((t2) => Array.isArray(t2) && t2[0] === "d") : void 0;
  const d = Array.isArray(tag) ? tag[1] : void 0;
  return isHex32(d) ? d : null;
}
function replaces(a, b) {
  return a.created_at > b.created_at || a.created_at === b.created_at && a.id < b.id;
}
function newestRecord(answers, open) {
  const checked = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  let newest = null;
  let newestUnreadable = null;
  for (const events of answers.values()) {
    for (const value of events) {
      if (isObject(value) && typeof value.id === "string" && checked.has(value.id))
        continue;
      const copy = open(value);
      if (!copy)
        continue;
      checked.add(copy.event.id);
      const createdAt = copy.event.created_at;
      if (copy.status === "newer") {
        newestUnreadable = Math.max(newestUnreadable ?? -1, createdAt);
        continue;
      }
      if (!newest || createdAt > newest.event.created_at || createdAt === newest.event.created_at && copy.event.id < newest.event.id) {
        newest = { event: copy.event, value: copy.value };
      }
    }
  }
  return { newest, newestUnreadable };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/address.js
var DEPOSIT_ADDRESS_LENGTH = 62;
var PROGRAM_WORDS = 53;
function isDepositAddress(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length !== DEPOSIT_ADDRESS_LENGTH || !/^bc1[02-9ac-hj-np-z]+$/.test(value)) {
    return false;
  }
  const decoded = decodeBech32(value, BECH32M);
  if (!decoded || decoded.hrp !== "bc")
    return false;
  const { words } = decoded;
  return words.length === PROGRAM_WORDS && words[0] === 1 && (words.at(-1) & 15) === 0;
}
function leaseAddress(registryKey, address) {
  return bytesToHex(hmac(sha256, registryKey, utf8ToBytes(LEASE_ADDRESS_PREFIX + address)));
}
function btcAmount(sats) {
  const whole = sats / SATS_PER_BTC;
  const fraction = (sats % SATS_PER_BTC).toString().padStart(8, "0").replace(/0+$/, "");
  return fraction === "" ? whole.toString() : `${whole}.${fraction}`;
}
function paymentUri(address, sats, bolt11) {
  if (address === null)
    return `lightning:${bolt11}`;
  const uri = `bitcoin:${address}?amount=${btcAmount(sats)}`;
  return bolt11 === null ? uri : `${uri}&lightning=${bolt11}`;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/node.js
var NODE_URL = /^(https?):\/\/([^/:?#@\\\s]+)(?::([1-9][0-9]{0,4}))?(\/[\x21-\x7e]*)?$/;
var OCTET = "(25[0-5]|2[0-4][0-9]|1[0-9]{2}|[1-9]?[0-9])";
var IPV4 = new RegExp(`^${OCTET}\\.${OCTET}\\.${OCTET}\\.${OCTET}$`);
var LABEL = /^[a-z0-9](?:[a-z0-9-]{0,61}[a-z0-9])?$/;
var MAX_NAME_LENGTH = 253;
function ipv4Of(host) {
  const match = IPV4.exec(host);
  return match ? match.slice(1, 5).map(Number) : null;
}
function isLocalIpv4([a, b]) {
  return a === 10 || a === 172 && b >= 16 && b <= 31 || a === 192 && b === 168 || a === 100 && b >= 64 && b <= 127;
}
function isName(host) {
  if (host.length > MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
    return false;
  const labels = host.split(".");
  if (labels.length < 2 || !labels.every((label) => LABEL.test(label)))
    return false;
  return /^[a-z]/.test(labels.at(-1)) && labels.at(-1) !== "localhost";
}
function isNodePath(path) {
  if (path === "")
    return true;
  if (/[?#@\\]/.test(path))
    return false;
  return !path.endsWith("/") && !/\/api$/i.test(path);
}
function parseNodeUrl(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > MAX_NODE_URL_LENGTH)
    return null;
  const match = NODE_URL.exec(value);
  if (!match)
    return null;
  const [, scheme, host, port, path = ""] = match;
  if (port !== void 0 && Number(port) > 65535)
    return null;
  if (!isNodePath(path))
    return null;
  const ipv4 = ipv4Of(host);
  let local;
  if (ipv4) {
    if (ipv4[0] === 0 || ipv4[0] === 127)
      return null;
    local = isLocalIpv4(ipv4);
  } else {
    if (!isName(host))
      return null;
    local = host.endsWith(".local");
  }
  if (scheme === "http" && !local)
    return null;
  return {
    url: value,
    rest: `${value}/api`,
    webSocket: `ws${value.slice("http".length)}/api/v1/ws`,
    local,
    internet: scheme === "https" && ipv4 === null && !local
  };
}
function isFallback(value) {
  return value === "mempool" || value === "none";
}
function bitcoinNodeOf(value) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    return null;
  const { url, fallback, payers } = value;
  const node = parseNodeUrl(url);
  if (!node || !isFallback(fallback) || typeof payers !== "boolean")
    return null;
  if (payers && !node.internet)
    return null;
  return { url: node.url, fallback, payers };
}
function recordNodeFor(node) {
  return node?.payers ? { url: node.url, fallback: node.fallback } : null;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/urls.js
var HOST = /^[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)*\.[a-z]{2,}$/;
var URL_CHARACTERS = /^(?:[A-Za-z0-9\-._~:/?#[\]@!$&'()*+,;=]|%[0-9A-Fa-f]{2})+$/;
var HTTPS = /^https:\/\/([^/?#]*)(?:[/?#].*)?$/;
var AUTHORITY = /^([^:]*)(?::([0-9]{1,5}))?$/;
function isHost(host) {
  return host.length <= MAX_HOST_LENGTH && HOST.test(host) && !/(?:^|\.)localhost$/.test(host);
}
function isPort(port) {
  return port === void 0 || Number(port) >= 1 && Number(port) <= 65535;
}
function parseHttpsUrl(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > MAX_HTTPS_URL_LENGTH || !URL_CHARACTERS.test(value)) {
    return null;
  }
  const authority = AUTHORITY.exec(HTTPS.exec(value)?.[1] ?? "");
  if (!authority || !isHost(authority[1]) || !isPort(authority[2]))
    return null;
  return { host: authority[1], port: authority[2] === void 0 ? 443 : Number(authority[2]) };
}
function sameHost(a, b) {
  return a.host === b.host && a.port === b.port;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/relays.js
var PREFIX = "wss://";
var ADDRESS = /^([a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)*\.[a-z]{2,})(:[0-9]{1,5})?(\/[^\t\n\v\f\r \u00a0\u1680\u2000-\u200a\u2028\u2029\u202f\u205f\u3000\ufeff?#,]*)?$/;
function isRelayUrl(url) {
  if (!url.startsWith(PREFIX))
    return false;
  const match = ADDRESS.exec(url.slice(PREFIX.length));
  if (!match)
    return false;
  const path = match[3] ?? "";
  return isHost(match[1]) && isPort(match[2]?.slice(1)) && !path.endsWith("/") && // A lone surrogate (a JSON escape in a channel makes one) is no text (SPEC.md §6).
  isWellFormed(path) && codePointLength(url) <= MAX_RELAY_URL_LENGTH;
}
function parseRelayList(value) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length === 0 || value.length > MAX_RELAYS)
    return null;
  const list = [];
  for (const item of value) {
    if (typeof item !== "string" || !isRelayUrl(item) || list.includes(item))
      return null;
    list.push(item);
  }
  return list;
}
function decodeRelayList(text) {
  const bytes = base64UrlToBytes(text);
  const decoded = bytes && bytes.length > 0 ? decodeUtf8(bytes) : null;
  if (decoded === null)
    return null;
  return parseRelayList(decoded.split(",").map((host) => `${PREFIX}${host}`));
}
function sameRelayList(a, b) {
  return a.length === b.length && a.every((relay, index) => relay === b[index]);
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/checkout.js
var HTTPS_AUTHORITY = /^https:\/\/([^/?#]*)/;
var APP_URL = /^([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*):\/\//;
var RELAY_PREFIX = "wss://";
function isCheckoutOrigin(value) {
  return parseHttpsUrl(value) !== null && /^https:\/\/[^/?#]*$/.test(value);
}
function checkoutOriginOf(checkout) {
  return isObject(checkout) && isCheckoutOrigin(checkout.origin) ? checkout.origin : null;
}
function redirectShown(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > MAX_REDIRECT_URL_LENGTH || !URL_CHARACTERS.test(value)) {
    return null;
  }
  if (value.startsWith("https://")) {
    return parseHttpsUrl(value) ? HTTPS_AUTHORITY.exec(value)[1] : null;
  }
  const app = APP_URL.exec(value);
  if (!app)
    return null;
  const scheme = app[1];
  if (scheme.length > MAX_REDIRECT_SCHEME_LENGTH || scheme === "https" || REFUSED_REDIRECT_SCHEMES.has(scheme)) {
    return null;
  }
  return `${scheme}://`;
}
function checkoutKeys(linkKey) {
  return {
    address: bytesToHex(deriveKey(linkKey, CHECKOUT_SALT, CHECKOUT_ADDRESS_INFO)),
    encryptionKey: deriveKey(linkKey, CHECKOUT_SALT, CHECKOUT_ENCRYPTION_INFO)
  };
}
function authorSecret(random) {
  for (; ; ) {
    const secret = random(KEY_BYTES);
    if (secp256k1.utils.isValidSecretKey(secret))
      return secret;
  }
}
function encodeRelayList(relays) {
  return bytesToBase64Url(utf8ToBytes(relays.map((relay) => relay.slice(RELAY_PREFIX.length)).join(",")));
}
function checkoutUrl(origin, author, linkKey, relays) {
  const parts = [
    CHECKOUT_MARKER,
    bytesToBase64Url(hexToBytes(author)),
    bytesToBase64Url(linkKey),
    encodeRelayList(relays)
  ];
  return `${origin}${CHECKOUT_PATH}#${parts.join(".")}`;
}
function checkoutValue(input) {
  const { businessName, invoice, verifyUrl, onchainAddress, fiat, note, redirectUrl } = input;
  if (onchainAddress === null) {
    return {
      version: CHECKOUT_VERSION,
      businessName,
      invoice,
      verifyUrl,
      fiat,
      note,
      redirectUrl
    };
  }
  return {
    version: CHECKOUT_ONCHAIN_VERSION,
    businessName,
    invoice,
    verifyUrl,
    onchainAddress,
    sats: Number(input.sats),
    createdAt: input.createdAt,
    expiresAt: input.expiresAt,
    fiat,
    note,
    redirectUrl,
    ...input.rest ? { restAuthor: input.rest.author, restKey: bytesToHex(input.rest.key) } : {},
    ...input.bitcoinNode ? { bitcoinNode: { url: input.bitcoinNode.url, fallback: input.bitcoinNode.fallback } } : {}
  };
}
function newCheckoutLink(input) {
  const linkKey = input.random(KEY_BYTES);
  const secretKey = authorSecret(input.random);
  const keys2 = checkoutKeys(linkKey);
  const event = sealRecord({
    type: CHECKOUT_RECORD_TYPE,
    value: input.value,
    signer: secretKey,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address: keys2.address,
    createdAt: input.createdAt,
    tags: [["expiration", String(input.expiresAt + CHECKOUT_LIFE_SECONDS)]],
    random: input.random
  });
  return {
    url: checkoutUrl(input.origin, publicKeyOf(secretKey), linkKey, input.relays),
    event,
    secretKey
  };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/errors.js
var SattlemeError = class extends Error {
  constructor(code, message, retryable = false, options) {
    super(message, options);
    /** A stable code, for programs: "invalid_key", "network"… */
    __publicField(this, "code");
    /** Whether the same call may succeed later, unchanged. */
    __publicField(this, "retryable");
    this.name = "SattlemeError";
    this.code = code;
    this.retryable = retryable;
  }
};
var InvalidKeyError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(message = "Not a sattle.me integration key") {
    super("invalid_key", message);
    this.name = "InvalidKeyError";
  }
};
var InvalidOptionError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(param, message) {
    super("invalid_option", `${param}: ${message}`);
    __publicField(this, "param");
    this.name = "InvalidOptionError";
    this.param = param;
  }
};
var InvalidOrderError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(param, message) {
    super("invalid_order", `${param}: ${message}`);
    __publicField(this, "param");
    this.name = "InvalidOrderError";
    this.param = param;
  }
};
var NetworkError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(message, options) {
    super("network", message, true, options);
    this.name = "NetworkError";
  }
};
var RevokedError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor() {
    super("revoked", "The merchant removed this integration");
    this.name = "RevokedError";
  }
};
var ChannelNotFoundError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(newerFormat) {
    super("channel_not_found", newerFormat ? "The integration's channel is of a newer format: update @sattleme/checkout" : "The integration's channel was not found on its relays", !newerFormat);
    __publicField(this, "newerFormat");
    this.name = "ChannelNotFoundError";
    this.newerFormat = newerFormat;
  }
};
var InvalidInvoiceError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(message, options) {
    super("invalid_invoice", message, false, options);
    this.name = "InvalidInvoiceError";
  }
};
var UnsupportedCurrencyError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(currency, source, message = `${source} has no price in ${currency}`) {
    super("unsupported_currency", message);
    __publicField(this, "currency");
    this.name = "UnsupportedCurrencyError";
    this.currency = currency;
  }
};
var RateError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(message, options) {
    super("rate", message, true, options);
    this.name = "RateError";
  }
};
var InvoicePendingError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(invoice, checkoutUrl2 = null) {
    super("invoice_pending", "This order still has an invoice that may be paid");
    __publicField(this, "invoice");
    __publicField(this, "checkoutUrl");
    this.name = "InvoicePendingError";
    this.invoice = invoice;
    this.checkoutUrl = checkoutUrl2;
  }
};
var CheckoutUnavailableError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor() {
    super("checkout_unavailable", "The merchant's app gives no web app for checkout links: show the invoice on the shop's page");
    this.name = "CheckoutUnavailableError";
  }
};
var OrderPaidError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor(invoice, status) {
    super("order_paid", "This order was already paid");
    __publicField(this, "invoice");
    __publicField(this, "status");
    __publicField(this, "preimage");
    this.name = "OrderPaidError";
    this.invoice = invoice;
    this.status = status;
    this.preimage = status.method === "lightning" ? status.preimage : null;
  }
};
var OnchainUnavailableError = class extends SattlemeError {
  constructor() {
    super("onchain_unavailable", "No deposit address of the integration's lot could be leased: try again later", true);
    this.name = "OnchainUnavailableError";
  }
};

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/http.js
var encoder2 = new TextEncoder();
function hostOf(url) {
  try {
    return new URL(url).host;
  } catch {
    return "(invalid URL)";
  }
}
async function getJson(url, options) {
  const { status, ok: ok2, bytes } = await getBytes(url, options);
  return { status, ok: ok2, body: parseJson(bytes) };
}
function bodyText(bytes) {
  const bom = bytes.length >= 3 && bytes[0] === 239 && bytes[1] === 187 && bytes[2] === 191;
  return decodeUtf8(bom ? bytes.subarray(3) : bytes);
}
async function getText(url, options) {
  const { status, ok: ok2, bytes } = await getBytes(url, options);
  return { status, ok: ok2, text: bodyText(bytes) };
}
async function getBytes(url, options) {
  const timeout = AbortSignal.timeout(options.timeoutMs);
  const signal = options.signal ? AbortSignal.any([options.signal, timeout]) : timeout;
  let answer;
  try {
    const response2 = await options.fetch(url, {
      headers: { ...options.headers, Accept: "application/json", "User-Agent": USER_AGENT },
      redirect: "error",
      signal
    });
    answer = { status: response2.status, ok: response2.ok, bytes: await readBody(response2) };
  } catch (error) {
    if (options.signal?.aborted)
      throw options.signal.reason;
    throw new NetworkError(`No answer from ${hostOf(url)}`, { cause: error });
  }
  if (answer.status >= 300 && answer.status < 400) {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} answered a redirect (HTTP ${answer.status})`);
  }
  if (answer.bytes === null) {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} answered more than ${MAX_HTTP_BODY_BYTES} bytes`);
  }
  return { status: answer.status, ok: answer.ok, bytes: answer.bytes };
}
async function readBody(response2) {
  const body = response2.body;
  if (body === null || body === void 0)
    return new Uint8Array(0);
  const chunks = [];
  let size = 0;
  const keep = (chunk) => {
    const bytes2 = chunk instanceof Uint8Array ? chunk : typeof chunk === "string" ? encoder2.encode(chunk) : null;
    if (!bytes2)
      throw new TypeError("Unexpected chunk of an HTTP body");
    size += bytes2.length;
    if (size > MAX_HTTP_BODY_BYTES)
      return false;
    chunks.push(bytes2);
    return true;
  };
  if (typeof body.getReader === "function") {
    const reader = body.getReader();
    for (; ; ) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done)
        break;
      if (!keep(value)) {
        await reader.cancel().catch(() => {
        });
        return null;
      }
    }
  } else if (typeof body === "object" && Symbol.asyncIterator in body) {
    for await (const chunk of body)
      if (!keep(chunk))
        return null;
  } else if (!keep(new Uint8Array(await response2.arrayBuffer()))) {
    return null;
  }
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(size);
  let offset = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    bytes.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.length;
  }
  return bytes;
}
function parseJson(bytes) {
  const text = bodyText(bytes);
  if (text === null)
    return null;
  try {
    return readJson(text);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/lnurl.js
function isServiceError(body) {
  return isObject(body) && typeof body.status === "string" && asciiLower(body.status) === "error";
}
async function lnurlGet(url, http) {
  const { status, ok: ok2, body } = await getJson(url, http);
  const refusalStatus = status >= 400 && status < 500 && status !== 408 && status !== 429;
  if ((ok2 || refusalStatus) && isServiceError(body)) {
    return { refusal: typeof body.reason === "string" ? body.reason : "" };
  }
  if (!ok2 || !isObject(body)) {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} answered HTTP ${status} without an LNURL answer`);
  }
  return { json: body };
}
function msat(value) {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0 ? BigInt(value) : null;
}
var LIGHTNING_ADDRESS = /^([a-z0-9\-_.+]+)@([a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+)$/;
function lightningAddressUrl(address) {
  const match = LIGHTNING_ADDRESS.exec(asciiLower(trimAscii(address)));
  if (!match)
    return null;
  const url = `https://${match[2]}/.well-known/lnurlp/${match[1]}`;
  return parseHttpsUrl(url) ? url : null;
}
async function fetchPayRequest(address, http) {
  const url = lightningAddressUrl(address);
  if (!url)
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The merchant's Lightning address is invalid");
  const answer = await lnurlGet(url, http);
  if ("refusal" in answer) {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} refused the Lightning address: ${answer.refusal}`);
  }
  const { json } = answer;
  const { callback } = json;
  const min = msat(json.minSendable);
  const max = msat(json.maxSendable);
  if (json.tag !== "payRequest" || typeof callback !== "string" || !parseHttpsUrl(callback) || min === null || max === null || min > max || typeof json.metadata !== "string" || // A lone surrogate (an escape in the JSON) has no UTF-8 to hash (SPEC.md §6, §16).
  !isWellFormed(json.metadata)) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError(`${hostOf(url)} gave no valid LNURL-pay request`);
  }
  const commentAllowed = typeof json.commentAllowed === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(json.commentAllowed) ? Math.max(0, json.commentAllowed) : 0;
  return {
    callback,
    minSendableMsat: min,
    maxSendableMsat: max,
    metadata: json.metadata,
    commentAllowed
  };
}
function descriptionHashOf(pay) {
  return bytesToHex(sha256(utf8ToBytes(pay.metadata)));
}
function sendableSats(pay) {
  return { min: (pay.minSendableMsat + 999n) / 1000n, max: pay.maxSendableMsat / 1000n };
}
async function requestInvoice(pay, { sats, expirySeconds, comment }, http) {
  let url;
  try {
    url = new URL(pay.callback);
  } catch (error) {
    throw new NetworkError("The Lightning address's callback cannot be asked", { cause: error });
  }
  url.hash = "";
  url.searchParams.set("amount", (sats * 1000n).toString());
  url.searchParams.set("expiry", String(expirySeconds));
  if (comment && utf8Length(comment) <= pay.commentAllowed)
    url.searchParams.set("comment", comment);
  const answer = await lnurlGet(url.toString(), http);
  if ("refusal" in answer) {
    throw new NetworkError(`The Lightning address refused the invoice: ${answer.refusal}`);
  }
  const { pr, verify } = answer.json;
  if (typeof pr !== "string" || typeof verify !== "string") {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The Lightning address gave no invoice or no verify URL (LUD-21)");
  }
  return { pr, verify };
}
function checkInvoice(answer, expected) {
  const decoded = decodeBolt11(answer.pr);
  if (!decoded)
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("Not a BOLT11 invoice");
  if (decoded.network !== "bc")
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("Not a Bitcoin mainnet invoice");
  if (decoded.amountMsat !== expected.sats * 1000n) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The invoice's amount differs from the order's");
  }
  if (decoded.descriptionHash !== descriptionHashOf(expected.pay)) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The invoice does not pay the merchant's Lightning address");
  }
  const verify = parseHttpsUrl(answer.verify);
  if (!verify || !sameHost(verify, parseHttpsUrl(expected.pay.callback))) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("No verify URL (LUD-21) on the Lightning address's host");
  }
  if (decoded.expirySeconds > expected.durationSeconds) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The invoice lasts longer than asked");
  }
  if (decoded.expirySeconds <= 0)
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The invoice has no time left");
  if (Math.abs(decoded.timestamp - expected.now) > CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SECONDS) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The invoice's date is too far from this server's clock");
  }
  if (decoded.timestamp + decoded.expirySeconds <= expected.now) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The invoice has no time left");
  }
  return decoded;
}
async function verifyPayment(invoice, http, now2) {
  const final = now2 >= invoice.expiresAt + GRACE_SECONDS2;
  const answer = await lnurlGet(invoice.verifyUrl, http);
  if ("refusal" in answer) {
    if (!final)
      return { state: "open" };
    if (answer.refusal === NOT_FOUND_REASON)
      return { state: "expired" };
    throw new NetworkError(`The payment check was refused: ${answer.refusal}`);
  }
  const { pr, settled, preimage } = answer.json;
  if (typeof pr !== "string") {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The payment check names no invoice (LUD-21 pr)");
  }
  if (asciiLower(pr) !== asciiLower(invoice.bolt11)) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The payment check is about another invoice");
  }
  if (settled !== true) {
    return final ? { state: "expired" } : { state: "open" };
  }
  if (typeof preimage !== "string" || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/i.test(preimage)) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("Paid without a valid preimage");
  }
  const proof = preimage.toLowerCase();
  if (bytesToHex(sha256(hexToBytes(proof))) !== invoice.paymentHash) {
    throw new InvalidInvoiceError("The preimage does not match the payment hash");
  }
  return { state: "paid", preimage: proof };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/tolerance.js
function toleranceOf(value) {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value <= MAX_TOLERANCE ? value : 0;
}
function leastAccepted(sats, tolerance) {
  return sats - sats * BigInt(toleranceOf(tolerance)) / TOLERANCE_SCALE;
}
function dueOnchain(sats, tolerance, paidByRests, merchantAccepted = null) {
  if (paidByRests >= sats)
    return 0n;
  const least = leastAccepted(sats, tolerance);
  const accepted = merchantAccepted !== null && merchantAccepted < least ? merchantAccepted : least;
  const due = accepted - paidByRests;
  return due > 0n ? due : 1n;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/channel.js
function parseChannel(value, createdAt) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    return null;
  const v = value;
  if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > CHANNEL_VERSION)
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  if (v.version !== CHANNEL_VERSION || v.updatedAt !== createdAt)
    return null;
  if (v.status === "removed")
    return { status: "removed", updatedAt: createdAt };
  if (v.status !== "active" || v.role !== "integration")
    return null;
  const profile = v.profile;
  if (!isObject(profile))
    return null;
  const { businessName, lightningAddress } = profile;
  if (typeof businessName !== "string" || !isWellFormed(businessName) || trimAscii(businessName) === "" || codePointLength(businessName) > MAX_BUSINESS_NAME_LENGTH) {
    return null;
  }
  if (typeof lightningAddress !== "string" || lightningAddressUrl(lightningAddress) === null) {
    return null;
  }
  if (!isHex32(v.registryKey) || !isPublicKey(v.registryReader))
    return null;
  const relays = v.relays === void 0 ? void 0 : parseRelayList(v.relays);
  if (relays === null)
    return null;
  return {
    status: "active",
    businessName,
    lightningAddress,
    registryKey: v.registryKey,
    registryReader: v.registryReader,
    ...relays ? { relays } : {},
    checkoutOrigin: checkoutOriginOf(v.checkout),
    // Absent or invalid, none: the channel stays valid (SPEC.md, "Reading the channel").
    tolerance: toleranceOf(v.onchainTolerance),
    // Absent or invalid in any way, none: mempool.space, and the channel stays valid.
    bitcoinNode: bitcoinNodeOf(v.bitcoinNode),
    updatedAt: createdAt
  };
}
async function lookupChannel(pool, access, relays, options) {
  const keys2 = channelKeys(access.key);
  const random = options.random ?? secureRandomBytes;
  const authKey = random(32);
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [access.teamPublicKey], "#d": [keys2.address] }, authKey, {
    paged: false,
    keep: recordsThatOpen((_, author) => author === access.teamPublicKey ? keys2.encryptionKey : null)
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  if (!answered)
    return { status: "unreachable" };
  const { newest, newestUnreadable } = newestRecord(answers, (value) => openRecord(value, {
    type: CHANNEL_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address: keys2.address,
    author: access.teamPublicKey,
    parse: (v, event) => parseChannel(v, event.created_at)
  }));
  if (newestUnreadable !== null && newestUnreadable > (newest?.event.created_at ?? -1)) {
    options.logger.warn("@sattleme/checkout: the integration's channel has a newer format than this version reads; update the package");
  }
  return newest ? { status: "found", channel: newest.value } : { status: "notFound", newer: newestUnreadable !== null };
}
async function readChannel(pool, access, options) {
  let relays = access.relays;
  let lookup = await lookupChannel(pool, access, relays, options);
  if (lookup.status === "unreachable") {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the integration key's relays answered");
  }
  if (lookup.status === "notFound")
    throw new ChannelNotFoundError(lookup.newer);
  let channel = lookup.channel;
  for (let move = 0; move < MAX_RELAY_MOVES; move += 1) {
    if (channel.status !== "active" || !channel.relays || sameRelayList(channel.relays, relays)) {
      break;
    }
    const next = channel.relays;
    lookup = await lookupChannel(pool, access, next, options);
    if (lookup.status === "unreachable") {
      throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
    }
    if (lookup.status === "notFound")
      break;
    channel = lookup.channel;
    relays = next;
  }
  if (channel.status === "removed")
    throw new RevokedError();
  return { channel, relays };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/declaration.js
function declarationEvent(input) {
  const { sale } = input;
  const declaration = {
    version: DECLARATION_VERSION,
    invoice: input.invoice,
    verifyUrl: input.verifyUrl,
    sale: { key: bytesToHex(sale.key), fiat: input.price, note: input.note },
    // "By" in the app: this integration.
    taker: sale.taker,
    // Only in a rest invoice's: the app ignores it, and counts the payment for the sale.
    ...input.rest === void 0 ? {} : { rest: input.rest }
  };
  const address = registryAddress(sale.registry.key, input.paymentHash);
  return sealRecord({
    type: DECLARATION_RECORD_TYPE,
    value: { sealed: nip44Encrypt(JSON.stringify(declaration), sale.innerKey, input.random) },
    signer: sale.secretKey,
    encryptionKey: outerKey(sale.registry.key, address, sale.author),
    address,
    createdAt: input.createdAt,
    ...input.random ? { random: input.random } : {}
  });
}
function restOfValue(value) {
  if (value === void 0)
    return null;
  if (!isObject(value))
    return void 0;
  const { address, expiresAt } = value;
  if (!isDepositAddress(address))
    return void 0;
  if (typeof expiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt) || expiresAt < 0) {
    return void 0;
  }
  return { address, expiresAt };
}
function parsePrice(value) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    return null;
  const { minor, digits, currency } = value;
  if (typeof minor !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(minor) || minor < 0)
    return null;
  if (typeof digits !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(digits) || digits < 0 || digits > MAX_DECLARED_DIGITS) {
    return null;
  }
  if (typeof currency !== "string" || !/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(currency))
    return null;
  return { minor, digits, currency };
}
function openDeclaration(value, sale) {
  const tags = isObject(value) ? value.tags : void 0;
  const address = Array.isArray(tags) ? tags.find((tag) => Array.isArray(tag) && tag[0] === "d") : void 0;
  const d = Array.isArray(address) ? address[1] : void 0;
  if (!isHex32(d))
    return null;
  const opened = openRecord(value, {
    type: DECLARATION_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: outerKey(sale.registry.key, d, sale.author),
    address: d,
    author: sale.author,
    parse: (outside, event) => {
      const sealed = isObject(outside) ? outside.sealed : void 0;
      if (typeof sealed !== "string")
        return null;
      let inside;
      try {
        inside = readJson(nip44Decrypt(sealed, sale.innerKey));
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
      if (!isObject(inside))
        return null;
      const v = inside;
      if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > DECLARATION_VERSION) {
        throw new NewerRecordError();
      }
      if (v.version !== DECLARATION_VERSION || typeof v.invoice !== "string")
        return null;
      if (typeof v.verifyUrl !== "string" || !parseHttpsUrl(v.verifyUrl))
        return null;
      const saleValue = v.sale;
      if (!isObject(saleValue))
        return null;
      if (saleValue.key !== bytesToHex(sale.key) || typeof saleValue.note !== "string") {
        return null;
      }
      const price = parsePrice(saleValue.fiat);
      if (!price)
        return null;
      const rest = restOfValue(v.rest);
      if (rest === void 0)
        return null;
      const decoded = decodeBolt11(v.invoice);
      if (!decoded || decoded.network !== "bc" || decoded.amountMsat === null)
        return null;
      if (decoded.amountMsat <= 0n || decoded.amountMsat % 1000n !== 0n)
        return null;
      if (d !== registryAddress(sale.registry.key, decoded.paymentHash))
        return null;
      return {
        event,
        invoice: v.invoice,
        decoded,
        verifyUrl: v.verifyUrl,
        price,
        note: saleValue.note,
        declaredAt: event.created_at,
        rest
      };
    }
  });
  if (opened?.status === "newer")
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  return opened?.status === "ok" ? opened.value : null;
}
function registryOf(registryKey, registryReader) {
  return { key: hexToBytes(registryKey), reader: registryReader };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/key.js
function parseIntegrationKey(text) {
  if (typeof text !== "string" || !text.startsWith(INTEGRATION_KEY_PREFIX)) {
    throw new InvalidKeyError();
  }
  const [teamText, keyText, relaysText, ...rest] = text.slice(INTEGRATION_KEY_PREFIX.length).split(".");
  if (teamText === void 0 || keyText === void 0 || relaysText === void 0) {
    throw new InvalidKeyError("Integration key cut short");
  }
  if (rest.length > 0)
    throw new InvalidKeyError();
  const team = base64UrlToBytes(teamText);
  const key = base64UrlToBytes(keyText);
  const relays = decodeRelayList(relaysText);
  if (team?.length !== KEY_BYTES || key?.length !== KEY_BYTES || relays === null) {
    throw new InvalidKeyError("Damaged integration key");
  }
  const teamPublicKey = bytesToHex(team);
  if (!isPublicKey(teamPublicKey))
    throw new InvalidKeyError("Damaged integration key");
  const parsed = { teamPublicKey, relays: Object.freeze(relays) };
  Object.defineProperty(parsed, "key", { value: key, enumerable: false });
  return Object.freeze(parsed);
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/nostr/relay.js
var AUTH_REQUIRED = "auth-required:";
var CONNECTING = 0;
var RelayRefusal = class extends Error {

    __publicField(this, "reason");
    this.name = "RelayRefusal";
    this.reason = reason;
  }

function entryOf(value, keep, read) {
  const v = typeof value === "object" && value !== null ? value : {};
  const id = typeof v.id !== "string" ? null : v.id.length <= 64 ? v.id : `#${bytesToHex(sha256(utf8ToBytes(v.id)))}`;
  const createdAt = Number.isSafeInteger(v.created_at) ? v.created_at : null;
  if (id !== null && read !== void 0) {
    if (read.has(id))
      return { id, createdAt };
    read.add(id);
  }
  const kept = keep(value);
  return kept === void 0 ? { id, createdAt } : { id, createdAt, value: kept };
}
function afterCompletePage(answer, seen, kept, pagesRead) {
  let oldest = null;
  let fresh = false;
  for (const entry of answer) {
    if (entry.id === null)
      continue;
    if (entry.createdAt !== null) {
      oldest = oldest === null ? entry.createdAt : Math.min(oldest, entry.createdAt);
    }
    if (seen.has(entry.id))
      continue;
    seen.add(entry.id);
    if ("value" in entry)
      kept.push(entry.value);
    fresh = true;
  }
  if (answer.length > COMPLETE_READ_LIMIT)
    return { complete: false };
  if (answer.length < COMPLETE_READ_LIMIT)
    return { complete: true };
  if (oldest === null || !fresh || pagesRead >= MAX_PAGES)
    return { complete: false };
  return { until: oldest };
}
function afterPagedPage(answer, seen, kept) {
  let oldest = null;
  let fresh = false;
  for (const entry of answer) {
    if (entry.id === null || entry.createdAt === null)
      continue;
    oldest = oldest === null ? entry.createdAt : Math.min(oldest, entry.createdAt);
    if (seen.has(entry.id))
      continue;
    seen.add(entry.id);
    if ("value" in entry)
      kept.push(entry.value);
    fresh = true;
  }
  if (oldest === null)
    return null;
  return fresh ? oldest : oldest - 1;
}
async function readPages(ask, until, events, signal) {
  const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  for (let page2 = 0; page2 < MAX_PAGES; page2 += 1) {
    let answer;
    try {
      answer = await ask(until);
    } catch (error) {
      if (page2 === 0 || signal?.aborted)
        throw error;
      break;
    }
    const next = afterPagedPage(answer, seen, events);
    if (next === null)
      break;
    until = next;
  }
  return events;
}
async function readCompletely(ask, until, events, signal) {
  const seen = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  for (let page2 = 1; ; page2 += 1) {
    let answer;
    try {
      answer = await ask(until);
    } catch (error) {
      if (page2 === 1 || signal?.aborted)
        throw error;
      return { events, complete: false };
    }
    const next = afterCompletePage(answer, seen, events, page2);
    if ("complete" in next)
      return { events, complete: next.complete };
    until = next.until;
  }
}
function tally(states) {
  let answered = 0;
  let complete = 0;
  let settled = 0;
  for (const state of states) {
    if (state === "complete" || state === "incomplete")
      answered += 1;
    if (state === "complete")
      complete += 1;
    if (state !== "reading")
      settled += 1;
  }
  return { answered, complete, settled };
}
function readOutcome(states) {
  const list = [...states];
  const needed = majority(list.length);
  const { answered, complete } = tally(list);
  return { answered: answered >= needed, complete: complete >= needed };
}
function keptOf(entries) {
  return entries.flatMap((entry) => "value" in entry ? [entry.value] : []);
}

function majority(count) {
  return Math.floor(count / 2) + 1;
}

  /**
   * What every relay of `relays` holds that matches `filter`, in parallel, page after page (NIP-01
   * `until`, for a filter that may match more than a relay sends at once; strfry: 500 events).
   * Resolves as soon as a majority of the relays answered (the others' reads are dropped), or once
   * every relay answered or failed: the events of each relay that answered, unchecked, and whether
   * a majority answered (readOutcome). Never rejects.
   */

   * may go on (SPEC.md §13).
   */
  publishToQuorum(relays, event, authKey, quorum) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      let taken = 0;
      let failures = 0;

        if (taken >= quorum)
          resolve();
        else if (relays.length - failures < quorum) {
          stop.abort();
          reject(new Error(`Fewer than ${quorum} relays took the event`));
        }
      };
      settle2();
      for (const relay of relays) {
        __privateMethod(this, _RelayPool_instances, publish_fn).call(this, relay, event, authKey, stop.signal).then(() => {
          taken += 1;
          settle2();
        }, () => {
          failures += 1;
          settle2();
        });
      }
    });
  }
  /** Closes every connection now: their requests fail. */
  close() {

    __privateGet(this, _reads).abort();
    for (const connection of __privateGet(this, _connections).values())
      connection.close();
    __privateGet(this, _connections).clear();
  }
  /**
   * Closes every connection once the requests still running are over (a write to the relays past
   * the quorum), each bounded by its timeout. Nothing new may be asked of the pool, and a read
   * still running (its call failed meanwhile) ends at once.
   */

    __privateGet(this, _reads).abort();

      return;
    }

  }

/**

 * the event itself only when `keep` takes it), unchecked, the caller verifies them. At most
 * MAX_EVENTS_PER_QUERY. Authenticates when the relay asks (NIP-42).
 */
query_fn = function(url, filter, authKey, keep, signal) {
  return __privateMethod(this, _RelayPool_instances, request_fn).call(this, url, signal, async (connection, onEnd, live) => {
    const ask = () => new Promise((done) => {
      const subscription = bytesToHex(__privateGet(this, _random).call(this, 8));
      const events = [];
      const read = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();

      const end = (closed, byRelay) => {
        if (!open)
          return;
        open = false;
        stop();

          connection.send(["CLOSE", subscription]);
        done({ events, closed });
      };
      const stop = connection.listen((message) => {
        if (message[1] !== subscription)
          return;

          events.push(entryOf(message[2], keep, read));
          if (events.length >= MAX_EVENTS_PER_QUERY)

        } else if (message[0] === "EOSE") {
          end(null, false);
        } else if (message[0] === "CLOSED") {
          end(typeof message[2] === "string" ? message[2] : "", true);
        }
      });
      onEnd(() => {

          return;
        open = false;

      });

      result = await ask();
    }

      throw new Error(`${url} closed the query: ${result.closed}`);
    return result.events;
  });
};
/** Publishes `event` to one relay, authenticating when it asks (NIP-42). Rejects unless taken. */
publish_fn = function(url, event, authKey, signal) {
  return __privateMethod(this, _RelayPool_instances, request_fn).call(this, url, signal, async (connection, onEnd, live) => {
    const write = () => new Promise((done) => {
      const stop = connection.listen((message) => {
        if (message[0] !== "OK" || message[1] !== event.id)
          return;
        stop();
        done({
          accepted: message[2] === true,
          reason: typeof message[3] === "string" ? message[3] : ""
        });
      });
      onEnd(stop);
      connection.send(["EVENT", event]);
    });
    let answer = await write();
    if (!answer.accepted && answer.reason.startsWith(AUTH_REQUIRED) && live() && await connection.authenticate(authKey, __privateGet(this, _options).now(), __privateGet(this, _random)) && live()) {
      answer = await write();
    }
    if (!answer.accepted)
      throw new RelayRefusal(url, answer.reason);
  });
};
/**
 * Runs `operation` over the connection to `url` once it is open, within `timeoutMs`. Fails when
 * the connection ends, the time runs out, `signal` or the pool's signal aborts. `onEnd` registers
 * what the operation leaves behind (listeners, subscriptions), cleaned up when it settles; `live`
 * tells whether it has not settled yet: once it has, the operation starts nothing more (SPEC.md
 * §13: no authentication, no query again).
 */
request_fn = function(url, signal, operation) {
  const request = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const cleanups = [];
    let settled = false;
    const settle2 = (outcome) => {
      if (settled)
        return;
      settled = true;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      for (const cleanup of cleanups.splice(0))
        cleanup();
      if ("value" in outcome)
        resolve(outcome.value);
      else
        reject(outcome.error);
    };
    const fail = (error) => {
      __privateGet(this, _failed).add(url);
      settle2({ error });
    };
    const timer = setTimeout(() => fail(new Error(`${url}: no answer in time`)), __privateGet(this, _options).timeoutMs);
    const abort = () => settle2({ error: new Error(`${url}: aborted`) });
    for (const s of [__privateGet(this, _options).signal, signal]) {
      if (!s)
        continue;
      s.addEventListener("abort", abort);
      cleanups.push(() => s.removeEventListener("abort", abort));
      if (s.aborted)
        abort();
    }
    if (settled)
      return;
    let connection;
    try {
      connection = __privateMethod(this, _RelayPool_instances, connection_fn).call(this, url);
    } catch (error) {
      fail(error);
      return;
    }
    cleanups.push(connection.onDeath((error) => fail(error)));
    if (settled)
      return;
    connection.opened.then(() => {
      if (settled)
        return void 0;
      return operation(connection, (cleanup) => {
        if (settled)
          cleanup();
        else
          cleanups.push(cleanup);
      }, () => !settled).then((value) => settle2({ value }));
    }).catch((error) => settle2({ error }));
  });
  __privateGet(this, _pending).add(request);
  const forget2 = () => __privateGet(this, _pending).delete(request);
  request.then(forget2, forget2);
  return request;
};
/** The pool's connection to `url`, opened at its first request. */
connection_fn = function(url) {
  if (__privateGet(this, _closed))
    throw new Error(`${url}: the pool is closed`);
  let connection = __privateGet(this, _connections).get(url);
  if (!connection) {
    let socket;
    try {
      socket = __privateGet(this, _options).createSocket(url);
    } catch (error) {
      throw new Error(`${url}: connection failed`, { cause: error });
    }
    connection = new Connection(url, socket);
    __privateGet(this, _connections).set(url, connection);
  }
  return connection;
};

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/explorer.js
var MAX_VOUT = 4294967295;
function isSafeNatural(value) {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(value) && value >= 0;
}
function parseTransaction(value) {
  if (!isObject(value) || !isHex32(value.txid))
    return null;
  const { vin, vout, status } = value;
  if (!Array.isArray(vin) || !Array.isArray(vout) || !isObject(status))
    return null;
  const coins = [];
  for (const input of vin) {
    if (!isObject(input))
      return null;
    if (input.is_coinbase === true)
      continue;
    const { txid, vout: index } = input;
    if (!isHex32(txid) || !isSafeNatural(index) || index > MAX_VOUT)
      return null;
    coins.push(`${txid}:${index}`);
  }
  const outputs = [];
  for (const output of vout) {
    if (!isObject(output) || !isSafeNatural(output.value))
      return null;
    const address = output.scriptpubkey_address;
    if (address !== void 0 && typeof address !== "string")
      return null;
    outputs.push({ sats: BigInt(output.value), address: address ?? null });
  }
  if (outputs.reduce((sum, output) => sum + output.sats, 0n) > MAX_SATS)
    return null;
  if (typeof status.confirmed !== "boolean")
    return null;
  let blockHeight = null;
  if (status.confirmed) {
    if (!isSafeNatural(status.block_height))
      return null;
    blockHeight = status.block_height;
  }
  return { txid: value.txid, coins, outputs, blockHeight };
}
function paysTo(tx, address) {
  return tx.outputs.reduce((sum, output) => output.address === address ? sum + output.sats : sum, 0n);
}
function confirmationsOf(tx, height) {
  return tx.blockHeight === null ? 0 : Math.max(1, height - tx.blockHeight + 1);
}
async function addressTransactions(base, address, http) {
  const url = `${base}/address/${address}/txs`;
  const { ok: ok2, status, body } = await getJson(url, http);
  if (!ok2)
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} answered HTTP ${status}`);
  if (!Array.isArray(body))
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} gave no transactions`);
  const txs = [];
  for (const value of body) {
    const tx = parseTransaction(value);
    if (!tx)
      throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} gave a transaction that is not one`);
    txs.push(tx);
  }
  return txs;
}
async function chainHeight(base, http) {
  const url = `${base}/blocks/tip/height`;
  const { ok: ok2, status, text } = await getText(url, http);
  if (!ok2)
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} answered HTTP ${status}`);
  if (text === null || !/^[0-9]{1,9}$/.test(text)) {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} gave no height`);
  }
  return Number(text);
}
var BLOCKCYPHER_TIME = /^([0-9]{4})-([0-9]{2})-([0-9]{2})T([0-9]{2}):([0-9]{2}):([0-9]{2})(?:\.([0-9]{1,9}))?Z$/;
function blockcypherMilliseconds(text) {
  const match = BLOCKCYPHER_TIME.exec(text);
  if (!match)
    return null;
  const [year, month, day, hour, minute, second] = match.slice(1, 7).map(Number);
  if (month < 1 || month > 12 || hour > 23 || minute > 59 || second > 59)
    return null;
  const utc = /* @__PURE__ */ new Date(0);
  utc.setUTCFullYear(year, month - 1, day);
  utc.setUTCHours(hour, minute, second, Number((match[7] ?? "").slice(0, 3).padEnd(3, "0")));
  if (day < 1 || utc.getUTCDate() !== day)
    return null;
  return utc.getTime();
}
async function receivedAt(txid, http) {
  const url = `${BLOCKCYPHER_URL}/txs/${txid}?limit=1&includeHex=false`;
  const { ok: ok2, status, body } = await getJson(url, http);
  if (status === 404)
    return null;
  if (!ok2)
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} answered HTTP ${status}`);
  if (!isObject(body) || body.hash !== txid || typeof body.received !== "string") {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} gave no time for the transaction`);
  }
  const time = blockcypherMilliseconds(body.received);
  if (time === null || time <= 0) {
    throw new NetworkError(`${hostOf(url)} gave no time for the transaction`);
  }
  return time;
}
function explorersToAsk(node) {
  if (node === null)
    return ["mempool"];
  if (node.fallback === "none")
    return ["node"];
  return node.failedRecently ? ["mempool", "node"] : ["node", "mempool"];
}
var MEMPOOL = {
  name: "mempool",
  rest: MEMPOOL_URL,
  webSocket: MEMPOOL_WEBSOCKET_URL
};
var _node, _failures, _now;
var Explorer = class {
  /** `now`: the clock, in milliseconds. */
  constructor(node, failures, now2) {
    __privateAdd(this, _node);
    __privateAdd(this, _failures);
    __privateAdd(this, _now);
    const at = node ? parseNodeUrl(node.url) : null;
    __privateSet(this, _node, node && at ? {
      name: "node",
      rest: at.rest,
      webSocket: at.webSocket,
      url: at.url,
      fallback: node.fallback
    } : null);
    __privateSet(this, _failures, failures);
    __privateSet(this, _now, now2);
  }
  /** The explorers the next read asks, in order (explorersToAsk). */
  sources() {
    const node = __privateGet(this, _node);
    if (!node)
      return [MEMPOOL];
    const failedAt = __privateGet(this, _failures).get(node.url);
    const failedRecently = failedAt !== void 0 && __privateGet(this, _now).call(this) - failedAt < NODE_FAILED_RECENTLY_MS;
    return explorersToAsk({ fallback: node.fallback, failedRecently }).map((name) => name === "node" ? node : MEMPOOL);
  }
  /** The node failed a read (or its WebSocket a connection): remembered for the call. */
  nodeFailed() {
    if (__privateGet(this, _node))
      __privateGet(this, _failures).set(__privateGet(this, _node).url, __privateGet(this, _now).call(this));
  }
  /**
   * One read of the explorer: `run` with the `<explorer>` URL of each explorer of sources(), in
   * order, until one answers. Each is asked with a signal of its own, aborted once it failed: what
   * `run` asked of it at the same time (the chain's height with the address's transactions) ends
   * then, and the whole read is asked again of the next one, never part of it. NetworkError when
   * every one failed; a cancellation (`http.signal`) is thrown as it is.
   */
  async read(http, run) {
    const failures = [];
    for (const source of this.sources()) {
      const attempt = new AbortController();
      const signal = http.signal ? AbortSignal.any([http.signal, attempt.signal]) : attempt.signal;
      try {
        return await run(source.rest, { ...http, signal });
      } catch (error) {
        attempt.abort();
        if (http.signal?.aborted)
          throw http.signal.reason;
        if (!(error instanceof NetworkError))
          throw error;
        if (source.name === "node")
          this.nodeFailed();
        failures.push(error);
      }
    }
    if (failures.length === 1)
      throw failures[0];
    throw new NetworkError(`Neither the merchant's Bitcoin node nor mempool.space answered (${failures.map(({ message }) => message).join("; ")})`, { cause: failures.at(-1) });
  }
};

function parseOutside(value) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    return null;
  if (typeof value.version === "number" && value.version > LEASE_VERSION) {
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  }
  if (value.version !== LEASE_VERSION)
    return null;
  if (value.withdrawn === true)
    return { kind: "withdrawn" };
  const { end, sealed } = value;
  if (typeof end !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(end) || typeof sealed !== "string") {
    return null;
  }
  return { kind: "lease", end, sealed };
}
function leaseRecordOf(event, d, outside) {

      if (signatures.includes(sig))
        return;
      signatures.push(sig);
      if (signed === false)
        signed = void 0;
    }
  };
}

function openLeaseOutside(value, registryKey, d) {
  if (!hasValidId(value) || value.kind !== RECORD_KIND || dTagOf(value) !== d)
    return null;
  if (!isHex32(value.pubkey))
    return null;
  let plaintext;
  try {
    plaintext = nip44Decrypt(value.content, outerKey(registryKey, d, value.pubkey));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }

  try {
    envelope = readJson(plaintext);
  } catch {

  }

    return null;
  }

  try {
    outside = parseOutside(envelope.v);

      return null;
    outside = { kind: "newer" };
  }
  if (!outside)
    return null;

  return { event: { id, pubkey, created_at, sig }, outside };
}
function holds({ outside }, t2) {
  return outside.kind === "newer" || outside.kind === "lease" && outside.end >= t2 - CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SECONDS;

function isLeased(records, t2) {
  const byAuthor = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const list = byAuthor.get(record.event.pubkey);
    if (list)
      list.push(record);
    else
      byAuthor.set(record.event.pubkey, [record]);
  }
  let leased = false;
  for (const list of byAuthor.values()) {
    const walk = list.sort((a, b) => replaces(a.event, b.event) ? -1 : 1);
    for (const [index, record] of walk.entries()) {
      if (holds(record, t2)) {
        leased = true;
        break;
      }
      if (!walk.slice(index + 1).some((older) => holds(older, t2)))
        break;
      if (record.signed())
        break;
    }
  }
  return leased;
}
function isConfirmed(records, own, t2) {
  if (!records.some(({ event }) => event.id === own.id))
    return false;
  if (isLeased(records.filter(({ event }) => event.pubkey !== own.pubkey), t2))
    return false;
  return !records.some(({ event }) => event.pubkey === own.pubkey && event.id !== own.id && replaces(event, own));
}
async function readLeases(pool, registryKey, addresses, relays, options) {
  const byD = new Map(addresses.map((address) => [leaseAddress(registryKey, address), address]));
  const found = new Map(addresses.map((address) => [address, []]));
  if (addresses.length === 0)
    return { records: found, answered: true, complete: true };
  const { answers, answered, complete } = await pool.queryComplete(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], "#d": [...byD.keys()], since: options.now - LEASE_LOOKBACK_SECONDS }, options.random(32), {
    everyRelay: options.everyRelay ?? false,
    // At a lease's address, by any author: the record each event opens to, as it comes (its id
    // checked; a content of a newer NIP-44 version is no record here: anybody writes one).
    keep: (value) => {
      const d = dTagOf(value);
      return d !== null && byD.has(d) ? openLeaseRecord(value, registryKey, d) ?? void 0 : void 0;
    }
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  const checked = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const records of answers.values()) {
    const ofRelay = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const record of records) {
      const { id } = record.event;
      if (ofRelay.has(id))
        continue;
      ofRelay.add(id);
      const known = checked.get(id);
      if (known) {
        known.addCopy(record.event.sig);
        continue;
      }
      checked.set(id, record);
      found.get(byD.get(record.d)).push(record);
    }
  }
  return { records: found, answered, complete };
}

function parsePrice2(value) {
  if (!isObject(value))

    return null;

  }
