function openLeaseInside(outside, d, sale) {
  let inside;
  try {
    inside = readJson(nip44Decrypt(outside.sealed, sale.innerKey));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (!isObject(inside))
    return null;
  const v = inside;
  if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > LEASE_VERSION)
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  if (v.version !== LEASE_VERSION)
    return null;
  const { address, sats, invoice, verifyUrl, createdAt, expiresAt } = v;
  if (!isDepositAddress(address) || leaseAddress(sale.registry.key, address) !== d)
    return null;
  if (typeof sats !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(sats) || sats < 1)
    return null;
  if (BigInt(sats) > MAX_SATS)
    return null;
  if (typeof createdAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(createdAt) || typeof expiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt) || createdAt >= expiresAt || outside.end !== expiresAt + GRACE_SECONDS2) {
    return null;
  }
  if (invoice === null) {
    if (verifyUrl !== null)
      return null;
  } else {
    if (typeof invoice !== "string" || typeof verifyUrl !== "string" || !parseHttpsUrl(verifyUrl)) {
      return null;
    }
    const decoded = decodeBolt11(invoice);
    if (!decoded || decoded.network !== "bc" || decoded.amountMsat !== BigInt(sats) * 1000n || decoded.timestamp !== createdAt || decoded.timestamp + decoded.expirySeconds !== expiresAt) {
      return null;
    }
  }
  const saleValue = v.sale;
  if (!isObject(saleValue))
    return null;
  if (saleValue.key !== bytesToHex(sale.key) || typeof saleValue.note !== "string")
    return null;
  const price = parsePrice2(saleValue.fiat);
  if (!price)
    return null;
  return {
    terms: {
      address,
      sats: BigInt(sats),
      invoice,
      verifyUrl,
      createdAt,
      expiresAt,
      price,
      note: saleValue.note,
      // Absent (a lease without tolerance) or invalid: none.
      tolerance: toleranceOf(v.tolerance),
      // Absent, or anything but true: not confirmed.
      ..."confirmed" in v ? { confirmed: v.confirmed === true } : {}
    },
    inside: v
  };
}
function openSaleRecord(value, sale, d) {
  const opened = openRecord(value, {
    type: LEASE_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: outerKey(sale.registry.key, d, sale.author),
    address: d,
    author: sale.author,
    parse: (outside) => parseOutside(outside)
  });
  if (!opened)
    return null;
  return {
    event: opened.event,
    outside: opened.status === "newer" ? { kind: "newer" } : opened.value
  };
}
function openSaleLease(value, sale) {
  const d = dTagOf(value);
  if (d === null || !isObject(value) || value.pubkey !== sale.author)
    return null;
  const record = openSaleRecord(value, sale, d);
  if (!record)
    return null;
  const { event, outside } = record;
  if (outside.kind === "newer")
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  if (outside.kind === "withdrawn")
    return { kind: "withdrawn", event, d };
  const opened = openLeaseInside(outside, d, sale);
  return opened && { kind: "lease", lease: { event, d, ...opened } };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/rest.js
function restInvoiceOf(declaration) {
  const { decoded } = declaration;
  return {
    bolt11: declaration.invoice,
    paymentHash: decoded.paymentHash,
    verifyUrl: declaration.verifyUrl,
    sats: decoded.amountMsat / 1000n,
    createdAt: decoded.timestamp,
    expiresAt: decoded.timestamp + decoded.expirySeconds
  };
}
function byRestOrder(a, b) {
  return a.createdAt - b.createdAt || (a.paymentHash < b.paymentHash ? -1 : a.paymentHash > b.paymentHash ? 1 : 0);
}
function restOf(input) {
  const { sats, address, received, now: now2 } = input;
  if (received <= 0n || received >= sats || now2 >= input.expiresAt)
    return null;
  const rest = sats - received;
  const invoice = [...input.rests].sort(byRestOrder).reverse().find((candidate) => candidate.sats === rest && !input.paid.has(candidate.paymentHash) && now2 < candidate.expiresAt) ?? null;
  const bolt11 = invoice?.bolt11 ?? null;
  return {
    sats: rest.toString(),
    bolt11,
    paymentUri: rest >= ONCHAIN_FLOOR_SATS ? paymentUri(address, rest, bolt11) : bolt11 === null ? null : `lightning:${bolt11}`
  };
}
function restToAsk(input) {
  const { rest, now: now2, lease } = input;
  if (rest === null || rest.bolt11 !== null)
    return null;
  if (now2 + MIN_REST_SECONDS > input.expiresAt)
    return null;
  if (lease === null || lease.sats !== input.sats || lease.expiresAt !== input.expiresAt) {
    return null;
  }
  return { sats: BigInt(rest.sats), expirySeconds: input.expiresAt - now2 };
}
function sendable(sats, bounds) {
  return sats >= bounds.min && sats <= bounds.max;
}
function restRecordEvent(input) {
  const { keys: keys2, invoice } = input;
  return sealRecord({
    type: REST_RECORD_TYPE,
    value: { version: REST_VERSION, invoice: invoice.bolt11, verifyUrl: invoice.verifyUrl },
    signer: keys2.secretKey,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address: restRecordAddress(keys2.key, invoice.paymentHash),
    createdAt: input.createdAt,
    tags: [["expiration", String(input.expiresAt + CHECKOUT_LIFE_SECONDS)]],
    ...input.random ? { random: input.random } : {}
  });
}
function openRestRecord(value, keys2, terms) {
  const d = dTagOf(value);
  if (d === null || d === acceptanceRecordAddress(keys2.key))
    return null;
  const opened = openRecord(value, {
    type: REST_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address: d,
    author: keys2.author,
    parse: (v) => {
      if (!isObject(v))
        return null;
      if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > REST_VERSION) {
        throw new NewerRecordError();
      }
      if (v.version !== REST_VERSION || typeof v.invoice !== "string")
        return null;
      if (typeof v.verifyUrl !== "string" || !parseHttpsUrl(v.verifyUrl))
        return null;
      const decoded = decodeBolt11(v.invoice);
      if (!decoded || decoded.network !== "bc" || decoded.amountMsat === null)
        return null;
      if (decoded.amountMsat <= 0n || decoded.amountMsat % 1000n !== 0n)
        return null;
      const sats = decoded.amountMsat / 1000n;
      const end = decoded.timestamp + decoded.expirySeconds;
      if (sats >= terms.sats || decoded.timestamp < terms.createdAt - CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SECONDS || end > terms.expiresAt + CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SECONDS) {
        return null;
      }
      if (d !== restRecordAddress(keys2.key, decoded.paymentHash))
        return null;
      return {
        bolt11: v.invoice,
        paymentHash: decoded.paymentHash,
        verifyUrl: v.verifyUrl,
        sats,
        createdAt: decoded.timestamp,
        expiresAt: end
      };
    }
  });
  if (!opened)
    return null;
  return opened.status === "newer" ? "newer" : { invoice: opened.value, event: opened.event };
}
async function readRestRecords(pool, keys2, terms, relays, options) {
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [keys2.author] }, options.random(32), {
    paged: false,
    keep: recordsThatOpen((_, author) => author === keys2.author ? keys2.encryptionKey : null)
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  if (!answered)
    return null;
  const needed = majority(relays.length);
  const sentBy = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const newer = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  for (const events of answers.values()) {
    const hashes = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const value of events) {
      const opened = openRestRecord(value, keys2, terms);
      if (opened === "newer")
        newer.add(dTagOf(value));
      else if (opened !== null)
        hashes.add(opened.invoice.paymentHash);
    }
    for (const hash of hashes)
      sentBy.set(hash, (sentBy.get(hash) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  const held = new Set([...sentBy].filter(([, count]) => count >= needed).map(([hash]) => hash));
  return { held, newer };
}
async function publishRestRecord(pool, event, keys2, relays, signal) {
  try {
    await pool.publishToQuorum(relays, event, keys2.secretKey, majority(relays.length));
  } catch (error) {
    if (signal?.aborted)
      throw signal.reason;
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays took the record of an invoice of the rest", { cause: error });
  }
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/acceptance.js
function acceptanceToPublish(status, invoice, now2) {
  if (now2 >= invoice.expiresAt + CHECKOUT_LIFE_SECONDS)
    return null;
  const accepted = status.state === "unconfirmed" || status.state === "paid" && status.method !== "lightning" && status.txids.length > 0;
  if (!accepted)
    return null;
  const received = BigInt(status.received);
  return received < invoice.sats ? received : null;
}
function acceptanceRecordEvent(input) {
  const { keys: keys2 } = input;
  return sealRecord({
    type: ACCEPTANCE_RECORD_TYPE,
    value: { version: ACCEPTANCE_VERSION, received: Number(input.received) },
    signer: keys2.secretKey,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address: acceptanceRecordAddress(keys2.key),
    createdAt: input.createdAt,
    tags: [["expiration", String(input.expiresAt + CHECKOUT_LIFE_SECONDS)]],
    ...input.random ? { random: input.random } : {}
  });
}
function openAcceptance(value, keys2, sats) {
  const address = acceptanceRecordAddress(keys2.key);
  if (dTagOf(value) !== address)
    return null;
  return openRecord(value, {
    type: ACCEPTANCE_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address,
    author: keys2.author,
    parse: (v) => {
      if (!isObject(v))
        return null;
      if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > ACCEPTANCE_VERSION) {
        throw new NewerRecordError();
      }
      if (v.version !== ACCEPTANCE_VERSION)
        return null;
      const { received } = v;
      if (typeof received !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(received) || received < 1) {
        return null;
      }
      return BigInt(received) < sats ? BigInt(received) : null;
    }
  });
}
async function acceptanceKnown(pool, keys2, sats, received, relays, options) {
  const needed = majority(relays.length);
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [keys2.author], "#d": [acceptanceRecordAddress(keys2.key)] }, options.random(32), {
    paged: false,
    keep: recordsThatOpen((_, author) => author === keys2.author ? keys2.encryptionKey : null)
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  let holders = 0;
  for (const [relay, events] of answers) {
    const { newest, newestUnreadable } = newestRecord(/* @__PURE__ */ new Map([[relay, events]]), (value) => openAcceptance(value, keys2, sats));
    if (newestUnreadable !== null) {
      options.logger.warn("@sattleme/checkout: an acceptance record of this order has a newer format than this version of @sattleme/checkout: update it");
      return true;
    }
    if (newest !== null && newest.value <= received)
      holders += 1;
  }
  if (!answered) {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
  }
  return holders >= needed;
}
async function publishAcceptance(pool, status, invoice, keys2, relays, options) {
  const received = acceptanceToPublish(status, invoice, options.clock());
  if (received === null)
    return null;
  const rest = keys2();
  if (await acceptanceKnown(pool, rest, invoice.sats, received, relays, options))
    return null;
  await publishAcceptanceRecord(pool, acceptanceRecordEvent({
    keys: rest,
    received,
    expiresAt: invoice.expiresAt,
    createdAt: options.clock(),
    random: options.random
  }), rest, relays, options.signal);
  return received;
}
async function publishAcceptanceRecord(pool, event, keys2, relays, signal) {
  try {
    await pool.publishToQuorum(relays, event, keys2.secretKey, majority(relays.length));
  } catch (error) {
    if (signal?.aborted)
      throw signal.reason;
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays took the acceptance record of a payment", { cause: error });
  }
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/lot.js
function parseLot(value, createdAt) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    return null;
  if (typeof value.version === "number" && value.version > LOT_VERSION) {
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  }
  if (value.version !== LOT_VERSION || value.updatedAt !== createdAt)
    return null;
  const { addresses } = value;
  if (!Array.isArray(addresses) || addresses.length > MAX_LOT_ADDRESSES)
    return null;
  const lot = [];
  for (const address of addresses) {
    if (!isDepositAddress(address) || lot.includes(address))
      return null;
    lot.push(address);
  }
  return lot;
}
async function readLot(pool, access, relays, options) {
  const address = lotAddress(access.key);
  const { encryptionKey } = channelKeys(access.key);
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [access.teamPublicKey], "#d": [address] }, options.random(32), {
    paged: false,
    keep: recordsThatOpen((_, author) => author === access.teamPublicKey ? encryptionKey : null)
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  if (!answered) {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
  }
  const { newest, newestUnreadable } = newestRecord(answers, (value) => openRecord(value, {
    type: LOT_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey,
    address,
    author: access.teamPublicKey,
    parse: (v, event) => parseLot(v, event.created_at)
  }));
  if (newestUnreadable !== null && newestUnreadable >= (newest?.event.created_at ?? -1)) {
    options.logger.warn("@sattleme/checkout: the integration's lot of addresses has a newer format than this version reads; update the package (no on-chain payment until then)");
    return [];
  }
  return newest?.value ?? [];
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/merchant-acceptance.js
function merchantAcceptanceKeys(integrationKey, team, saleAuthor, address, expiresAt) {
  return {
    team,
    encryptionKey: channelKeys(integrationKey).encryptionKey,
    address: merchantAcceptanceAddress(integrationKey, saleAuthor, address, expiresAt)
  };
}
function openMerchantAcceptance(value, keys2, invoice) {
  const opened = openRecord(value, {
    type: MERCHANT_ACCEPTANCE_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: keys2.encryptionKey,
    address: keys2.address,
    author: keys2.team,
    parse: (v) => {
      if (!isObject(v))
        return null;
      if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > MERCHANT_ACCEPTANCE_VERSION) {
        throw new NewerRecordError();
      }
      if (v.version !== MERCHANT_ACCEPTANCE_VERSION)
        return null;
      const { received } = v;
      if (typeof received !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(received) || received < 1) {
        return null;
      }
      return BigInt(received) < invoice.sats ? BigInt(received) : null;
    }
  });
  if (!opened)
    return null;
  const late = opened.event.created_at > invoice.expiresAt + GRACE_SECONDS2;
  if (opened.status === "newer") {
    return { status: late ? "newer, set aside" : "newer", event: opened.event };
  }
  return late ? null : { status: "ok", event: opened.event, received: opened.value };
}
function merchantAcceptanceOf(answers, keys2, invoice, relayCount) {
  const copies = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const open = (value) => {
    if (!isObject(value) || typeof value.id !== "string" || typeof value.sig !== "string") {
      return openMerchantAcceptance(value, keys2, invoice);
    }
    const copy = `${value.id}/${value.sig}`;
    if (!copies.has(copy))
      copies.set(copy, openMerchantAcceptance(value, keys2, invoice));
    return copies.get(copy);
  };
  const records = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  let newer = false;
  for (const events of answers.values()) {
    const sent = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const value of events) {
      const opened = open(value);
      if (opened === null)
        continue;
      if (opened.status !== "ok")
        newer = true;
      if (opened.status === "newer, set aside")
        continue;
      const { id } = opened.event;
      if (sent.has(id))
        continue;
      sent.add(id);
      const known = records.get(id);
      if (known)
        known.sentBy += 1;
      else
        records.set(id, { opened, sentBy: 1 });
    }
  }
  const needed = majority(relayCount);
  let newest = null;
  for (const { opened, sentBy } of records.values()) {
    if (sentBy < needed)
      continue;
    if (!newest || replaces(opened.event, newest.event))
      newest = opened;
  }
  return { received: newest?.status === "ok" ? newest.received : null, newer };
}
async function readMerchantAcceptance(pool, keys2, invoice, relays, options) {
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [keys2.team], "#d": [keys2.address] }, options.random(32), {
    paged: false,
    everyRelay: true,
    keep: recordsThatOpen((d, author) => author === keys2.team && d === keys2.address ? keys2.encryptionKey : null)
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  if (!answered) {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
  }
  return merchantAcceptanceOf(answers, keys2, invoice, relays.length);
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/methods.js
function paymentMethods(sats, sendable2, param) {
  const lightning = sats >= sendable2.min && sats <= sendable2.max;
  const onchain = sats >= ONCHAIN_FLOOR_SATS;
  if (!lightning && !onchain) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError(param, `${sats} sats is outside the merchant's bounds (${sendable2.min} to ${sendable2.max} sats)`);
  }
  return { lightning, onchain };
}
function methodsWith(methods, outcome) {
  if (!methods.onchain || outcome.status === "leased")
    return methods;
  if (!methods.lightning) {
    throw outcome.status === "explorer_failed" ? outcome.error : new OnchainUnavailableError();
  }
  return { lightning: true, onchain: false };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/replacements.js
function paymentParts(txs) {
  const unique = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const tx of txs)
    if (!unique.has(tx.txid))
      unique.set(tx.txid, tx);
  const sorted = [...unique.values()].sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.order !== b.order) {
      if (a.order === null)
        return 1;
      if (b.order === null)
        return -1;
      return a.order - b.order;
    }
    return a.txid < b.txid ? -1 : a.txid > b.txid ? 1 : 0;
  });
  const position = new Map(sorted.map((tx, index) => [tx.txid, index]));
  const roots = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  sorted.forEach((tx, index) => {
    const coins = new Set(tx.inputs);
    const before = sorted.slice(0, index).filter((other) => other.inputs.some((coin) => coins.has(coin)));
    if (before.length === 0) {
      roots.set(tx.txid, [tx.txid]);
      return;
    }
    const together = new Set(before.flatMap((other) => roots.get(other.txid)));
    roots.set(tx.txid, [...together].sort((a, b) => position.get(a) - position.get(b)));
  });
  const toGive = new Map(sorted.map((tx) => [tx.txid, tx.sats]));
  const parts = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const tx of sorted) {
    if (!tx.listed)
      continue;
    const own = roots.get(tx.txid);
    let owed = tx.sats;
    const list = [];
    if (own.length === 1 && own[0] === tx.txid) {
      toGive.set(tx.txid, 0n);
    } else {
      for (const root of own) {
        const left = toGive.get(root);
        const take = owed < left ? owed : left;
        if (take > 0n) {
          list.push({ sats: take, root });
          toGive.set(root, left - take);
          owed -= take;
        }
      }
    }
    if (owed > 0n)
      list.push({ sats: owed, root: tx.txid });
    parts.set(tx.txid, list);
  }
  return parts;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/payment.js
async function checkOnchainPart(check) {
  const { address, sats, http, sale } = check;
  const end = check.expiresAt + GRACE_SECONDS2;
  const [txs, height] = await check.explorer.read(http, (base, asked) => Promise.all([addressTransactions(base, address, asked), chainHeight(base, asked)]));
  const now2 = check.clock();
  const paying = [];
  for (const tx of txs) {
    const pays = paysTo(tx, address);
    if (pays > 0n && !paying.some((other) => other.tx.txid === tx.txid))
      paying.push({ tx, pays });
  }
  const noAcceptance = { received: null, newer: false };
  const [witnesses, accepted] = paying.length > 0 ? await Promise.all([
    check.readWitnesses(),
    check.readMerchantAcceptance?.() ?? Promise.resolve(noAcceptance)
  ]) : [{ sale: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map(), others: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map(), none: /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(), newer: /* @__PURE__ */ new Set() }, noAcceptance];
  const listed = new Set(paying.map(({ tx }) => tx.txid));
  if (witnesses.newer.size > 0) {
    check.logger.warn("@sattleme/checkout: a witness of this address has a newer format than this version of @sattleme/checkout: update it");
  }
  if (accepted.newer) {
    check.logger.warn("@sattleme/checkout: a merchant's acceptance of this order has a newer format than this version of @sattleme/checkout: update it");
  }
  const partsOf = (read) => paymentParts([
    ...[...read.sale, ...read.others].map(([txid, witness]) => ({
      txid,
      sats: witness.sats,
      inputs: witness.inputs,
      order: witness.seenAt,
      listed: listed.has(txid)
    })),
    ...paying.filter(({ tx }) => !read.sale.has(tx.txid) && !read.others.has(tx.txid)).map(({ tx, pays }) => ({
      txid: tx.txid,
      sats: pays,
      inputs: tx.coins,
      order: null,
      listed: true
    }))
  ]);
  const anothers = (read, partsRead, txid) => read.others.has(txid) || partsRead.get(txid).some(({ root }) => read.others.has(root));
  const parts = partsOf(witnesses);
  const newerNames = (txid, sale2) => witnesses.newer.has(check.witnessAddress(txid, sale2));
  const newerElsewhere = [...witnesses.newer].some((d) => !paying.some(({ tx }) => d === check.witnessAddress(tx.txid, sale) || d === check.witnessAddress(tx.txid, null)));
  const kinds = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const ofSale = [];
  const ofNone = [];
  const witnessOf = (tx, pays, forSale, replacement) => ({
    address,
    txid: tx.txid,
    sale: forSale ? sale : null,
    sats: pays,
    inputs: tx.coins.slice(0, MAX_WITNESS_INPUTS),
    replacement,
    expiresAt: forSale ? check.expiresAt : null
  });
  for (const { tx, pays } of paying) {
    const { txid } = tx;
    if (anothers(witnesses, parts, txid)) {
      kinds.set(txid, "another's");
      continue;
    }
    const witness = witnesses.sale.get(txid);
    if (witness && !witness.replacement) {
      kinds.set(txid, "in time");
      continue;
    }
    if (newerNames(txid, sale)) {
      kinds.set(txid, "undecided");
      continue;
    }
    if (now2 <= end) {
      kinds.set(txid, "in time");
      ofSale.push(witnessOf(tx, pays, true, false));
      continue;
    }
    if (witness) {
      kinds.set(txid, "replacement");
      continue;
    }
    const own = parts.get(txid);
    const replaces2 = own.some(({ root }) => root !== txid);
    if (own.some(({ root }) => root === txid)) {
      const time = await receivedAt(txid, http);
      if (time !== null && time <= end * 1e3) {
        kinds.set(txid, "in time");
        ofSale.push(witnessOf(tx, pays, true, false));
        continue;
      }
    }
    if (newerElsewhere) {
      kinds.set(txid, "undecided");
    } else if (replaces2) {
      kinds.set(txid, "replacement");
      ofSale.push(witnessOf(tx, pays, true, true));
    } else {
      kinds.set(txid, "late");
      if (!witnesses.none.has(txid) && !newerNames(txid, null)) {
        ofNone.push(witnessOf(tx, pays, false, false));
      }
    }
  }
  await Promise.all([
    ...ofSale.map((witness) => check.publish(witness)),
    ...ofNone.map((witness) => check.publish(witness).catch((error) => {
      if (!(error instanceof NetworkError))
        throw error;
      check.logger.warn(`@sattleme/checkout: the witness of a late payment was not published: ${error.message}`);
    }))
  ]);
  if (ofSale.length > 0) {
    const again = await check.readWitnesses({ everyRelay: true });
    const partsAgain = partsOf(again);
    for (const { tx } of paying) {
      if (anothers(again, partsAgain, tx.txid))
        kinds.set(tx.txid, "another's");
    }
  }
  const inTime = (root) => listed.has(root) ? kinds.get(root) === "in time" : witnesses.sale.get(root)?.replacement === false;
  const counted = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const { tx } of paying) {
    const kind = kinds.get(tx.txid);
    if (kind === "undecided" || kind === "another's")
      continue;
    const sum = parts.get(tx.txid).filter(({ root }) => inTime(root)).reduce((total, part) => total + part.sats, 0n);
    if (sum > 0n)
      counted.set(tx.txid, sum);
  }
  const received = [...counted.values()].reduce((total, sum) => total + sum, 0n);
  const [paidByRests, tolerance] = await Promise.all([
    check.paidByRests ?? Promise.resolve(0n),
    check.tolerance ?? Promise.resolve(0)
  ]);
  const due = dueOnchain(sats, tolerance, paidByRests, accepted.received);
  if (due === 0n)
    return { kind: "paid", received, txids: [] };
  if (received < due) {
    const undecided = [...kinds.values()].includes("undecided");
    return { kind: "underpaid", received, final: now2 > end && !undecided };
  }
  const order = paying.filter(({ tx }) => counted.has(tx.txid)).map(({ tx }) => tx);
  const txids = order.map(({ txid }) => txid);
  const confirmations = Math.min(...order.map((tx) => confirmationsOf(tx, height)));
  if (confirmations >= CONFIRMATIONS)
    return { kind: "paid", received, txids };
  return { kind: "unconfirmed", received, confirmations, txids };
}
function restSummary(rests) {
  const summary = {
    paid: 0n,
    preimages: [],
    hashes: /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(),
    final: true,
    error: null
  };
  if (rests && "error" in rests) {
    summary.final = false;
    summary.error = { error: rests.error };
  }
  const order = (rests?.invoices ?? []).map((invoice, index) => ({ invoice, result: rests.results[index] })).sort((a, b) => byRestOrder(a.invoice, b.invoice));
  for (const { invoice, result } of order) {
    if (!result.ok) {
      summary.final = false;
      summary.error ?? (summary.error = { error: result.error });
      continue;
    }
    if (result.value.state === "paid") {
      if (summary.hashes.has(invoice.paymentHash))
        continue;
      summary.hashes.add(invoice.paymentHash);
      summary.paid += invoice.sats;
      summary.preimages.push(result.value.preimage);
    } else if (result.value.state === "open") {
      summary.final = false;
    }
  }
  return summary;
}
function combineParts(invoice, now2, lightning, onchain, rests = null) {
  if (lightning?.ok && lightning.value.state === "paid") {
    return { state: "paid", method: "lightning", preimage: lightning.value.preimage };
  }
  const summary = restSummary(rests);
  if (summary.paid >= invoice.sats) {
    const onchainReceived = onchain?.ok ? onchain.value.received : 0n;
    return {
      state: "paid",
      method: "mixed",
      received: (onchainReceived + summary.paid).toString(),
      txids: [],
      preimages: summary.preimages
    };
  }
  if (onchain?.ok && onchain.value.kind === "paid") {
    const received2 = (onchain.value.received + summary.paid).toString();
    return summary.paid > 0n ? {
      state: "paid",
      method: "mixed",
      received: received2,
      txids: onchain.value.txids,
      preimages: summary.preimages
    } : { state: "paid", method: "onchain", received: received2, txids: onchain.value.txids };
  }
  if (onchain?.ok && onchain.value.kind === "unconfirmed") {
    return {
      state: "unconfirmed",
      received: (onchain.value.received + summary.paid).toString(),
      confirmations: onchain.value.confirmations,
      txids: onchain.value.txids
    };
  }
  if (lightning && !lightning.ok)
    throw lightning.error;
  if (summary.error)
    throw summary.error.error;
  if (onchain && !onchain.ok)
    throw onchain.error;
  const underpaid = onchain?.ok && onchain.value.kind === "underpaid" ? onchain.value : null;
  const received = (underpaid?.received ?? 0n) + summary.paid;
  const lightningFinal = lightning === null || lightning.value.state === "expired";
  const onchainFinal = onchain === null || underpaid?.final === true;
  if (lightningFinal && onchainFinal && summary.final) {
    return { state: "expired", received: received.toString() };
  }
  return {
    state: "open",
    expiresAt: invoice.expiresAt,
    received: received.toString(),
    rest: invoice.address === null ? null : restOf({
      sats: invoice.sats,
      expiresAt: invoice.expiresAt,
      address: invoice.address,
      received,
      now: now2,
      rests: (rests?.invoices ?? []).filter(({ paymentHash }) => !rests?.unrecorded?.has(paymentHash)),
      paid: summary.hashes
    })
  };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/watch.js
var CHANGES = [
  "address-transactions",
  "block-transactions",
  "address-removed-transactions",
  "block"
];
var ignore2 = () => {
};
function watchAddress(createSocket, explorer, address, onChange) {
  let closed = false;
  let socket = null;
  let timer = null;
  let delay = RECONNECT_FIRST_MS;
  const retry = () => {
    if (closed || timer !== null)
      return;
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      timer = null;
      connect();
    }, delay);
    delay = Math.min(delay * 2, RECONNECT_MAX_MS);
  };
  const connectTo = (sources, index) => {
    if (closed)
      return;
    const source = sources[index];
    if (!source) {
      retry();
      return;
    }
    const failed2 = () => {
      if (source.name === "node")
        explorer.nodeFailed();
      connectTo(sources, index + 1);
    };
    let current;
    try {
      current = createSocket(source.webSocket);
    } catch {
      failed2();
      return;
    }
    socket = current;
    let lost = false;
    let answered = false;
    const lose = () => {
      if (lost || socket !== current)
        return;
      lost = true;
      socket = null;
      if (answered)
        retry();
      else
        failed2();
    };
    current.onopen = () => {
      if (socket !== current)
        return;
      try {
        current.send(JSON.stringify({ action: "want", data: ["blocks"] }));
        current.send(JSON.stringify({ "track-address": address }));
      } catch {
        lose();
        return;
      }
      onChange();
    };
    current.onmessage = (event) => {
      if (socket !== current)
        return;
      answered = true;
      delay = RECONNECT_FIRST_MS;
      let message;
      try {
        message = readJson(String(event.data));
      } catch {
        return;
      }
      if (isObject(message) && CHANGES.some((key) => Object.hasOwn(message, key)))
        onChange();
    };
    current.onerror = lose;
    current.onclose = lose;
  };
  const connect = () => connectTo(explorer.sources(), 0);
  connect();
  return {
    close: () => {
      closed = true;
      if (timer !== null)
        clearTimeout(timer);
      const current = socket;
      socket = null;
      if (current) {
        current.onmessage = current.onerror = current.onclose = ignore2;
        current.onopen = current.readyState === 0 ? () => current.close() : ignore2;
        try {
          current.close();
        } catch {
        }
      }
    }
  };
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/onchain/witness.js
function seenOf(integrationKey) {
  return { ...seenKeys(integrationKey), encryptionKey: channelKeys(integrationKey).encryptionKey };
}
function mac2(key, text) {
  return bytesToHex(hmac(sha256, key, utf8ToBytes(text)));
}
function witnessAddress(addressKey, address, txid, sale) {
  return mac2(addressKey, `${address}/${txid}/${sale ?? NO_SALE}`);
}
function orderTag(addressKey, address, sale) {
  return mac2(addressKey, `order/${address}/${sale ?? NO_SALE}`);
}
function addressTag(addressKey, address) {
  return mac2(addressKey, `address/${address}`);
}
function isCoin(value) {
  return typeof value === "string" && /^[0-9a-f]{64}:(?:0|[1-9][0-9]{0,9})$/.test(value) && Number(value.slice(65)) <= 4294967295;
}
function witnessEvent(seen, witness, createdAt, random) {
  return sealRecord({
    type: WITNESS_RECORD_TYPE,
    value: {
      version: WITNESS_VERSION,
      address: witness.address,
      txid: witness.txid,
      sale: witness.sale,
      sats: Number(witness.sats),
      inputs: witness.inputs.slice(0, MAX_WITNESS_INPUTS),
      replacement: witness.replacement,
      expiresAt: witness.sale === null ? null : witness.expiresAt
    },
    signer: seen.secretKey,
    encryptionKey: seen.encryptionKey,
    address: witnessAddress(seen.addressKey, witness.address, witness.txid, witness.sale),
    createdAt,
    tags: [
      [ORDER_TAG, orderTag(seen.addressKey, witness.address, witness.sale)],
      [ORDER_TAG, addressTag(seen.addressKey, witness.address)]
    ],
    ...random ? { random } : {}
  });
}
function parseWitness(value, event, addressKey) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    return null;
  const v = value;
  if (typeof v.version === "number" && v.version > WITNESS_VERSION)
    throw new NewerRecordError();
  if (v.version !== WITNESS_VERSION)
    return null;
  const { address, txid, sale, sats, inputs, replacement } = v;
  if (!isDepositAddress(address) || !isHex32(txid))
    return null;
  if (sale !== null && !isHex32(sale))
    return null;
  if (typeof sats !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(sats) || sats < 1)
    return null;
  if (BigInt(sats) > MAX_SATS)
    return null;
  if (!Array.isArray(inputs) || inputs.length > MAX_WITNESS_INPUTS || !inputs.every(isCoin) || new Set(inputs).size !== inputs.length) {
    return null;
  }
  if (typeof replacement !== "boolean" || sale === null && replacement)
    return null;
  const { expiresAt } = v;
  if (sale === null ? expiresAt !== null : typeof expiresAt !== "number" || !Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt)) {
    return null;
  }
  const d = event.tags.find(([name]) => name === "d")?.[1];
  const hasTag = (tag) => event.tags.some(([name, content]) => name === ORDER_TAG && content === tag);
  if (d !== witnessAddress(addressKey, address, txid, sale) || !hasTag(orderTag(addressKey, address, sale)) || !hasTag(addressTag(addressKey, address))) {
    return null;
  }
  return {
    address,
    txid,
    sale,
    sats: BigInt(sats),
    inputs,
    replacement,
    expiresAt
  };
}
function openWitness(value, seen) {
  const d = dTagOf(value);
  if (d === null)
    return null;
  const opened = openRecord(value, {
    type: WITNESS_RECORD_TYPE,
    encryptionKey: seen.encryptionKey,
    address: d,
    author: seen.author,
    parse: (v, event) => parseWitness(v, event, seen.addressKey)
  });
  if (!opened)
    return null;
  return opened.status === "newer" ? { status: "newer", event: opened.event, d } : { status: "ok", event: opened.event, witness: opened.value };
}
async function readOrderWitnesses(pool, seen, address, saleAuthor, expiresAt, relays, options) {
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [seen.author], "#o": readTags(seen, address, saleAuthor) }, options.random(32), {
    paged: false,
    everyRelay: options.everyRelay ?? false,
    keep: recordsThatOpen((_, author) => author === seen.author ? seen.encryptionKey : null)
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  if (!answered) {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
  }
  return orderWitnessesOf([...answers.values()].flat(), seen, address, saleAuthor, expiresAt);
}
function readTags(seen, address, saleAuthor) {
  return [
    orderTag(seen.addressKey, address, saleAuthor),
    orderTag(seen.addressKey, address, null),
    addressTag(seen.addressKey, address)
  ];
}
function orderWitnessesOf(events, seen, address, saleAuthor, expiresAt) {
  const tags = new Set(readTags(seen, address, saleAuthor));
  const newest = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const checked = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  for (const value of events) {
    if (isObject(value) && typeof value.id === "string" && checked.has(value.id))
      continue;
    const opened = openWitness(value, seen);
    if (!opened)
      continue;
    checked.add(opened.event.id);
    if (!opened.event.tags.some(([name, content]) => name === ORDER_TAG && tags.has(content))) {
      continue;
    }
    if (opened.status === "ok" && opened.witness.address !== address)
      continue;
    const d = opened.event.tags.find(([name]) => name === "d")[1];
    const known = newest.get(d);
    if (!known || replaces(opened.event, known.event))
      newest.set(d, opened);
  }
  const witnesses = {
    sale: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map(),
    others: /* @__PURE__ */ new Map(),
    none: /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(),
    newer: /* @__PURE__ */ new Set()
  };
  for (const [d, opened] of newest) {
    if (opened.status === "newer") {
      witnesses.newer.add(d);
      continue;
    }
    const { witness, event } = opened;
    const of = {
      sats: witness.sats,
      inputs: witness.inputs,
      replacement: witness.replacement,
      seenAt: event.created_at
    };
    if (witness.sale === null) {
      witnesses.none.add(witness.txid);
    } else if (witness.sale === saleAuthor && witness.expiresAt === expiresAt) {
      witnesses.sale.set(witness.txid, of);
    } else {
      witnesses.others.set(witness.txid, of);
    }
  }
  return witnesses;
}
function addressesNamed(value, seenAuthor, byTag, checks = signatureChecks) {
  if (!isObject(value) || value.pubkey !== seenAuthor || !Array.isArray(value.tags))
    return [];
  const tagged = new Set(value.tags.flatMap((tag) => Array.isArray(tag) && tag[0] === ORDER_TAG && byTag.has(tag[1]) ? [byTag.get(tag[1])] : []));
  return tagged.size > 0 && hasValidId(value) && checks(value) ? [...tagged] : [];
}
async function readNamedAddresses(pool, seen, addresses, relays, options) {
  const named = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  if (addresses.length === 0)
    return named;
  const byTag = new Map(addresses.map((address) => [addressTag(seen.addressKey, address), address]));
  const verdicts = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const checks = (event) => {
    const copy = `${event.id}/${event.sig}`;
    let valid = verdicts.get(copy);
    if (valid === void 0)
      verdicts.set(copy, valid = signatureChecks(event));
    return valid;
  };
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [seen.author], "#o": [...byTag.keys()] }, options.random(32), {
    paged: false,
    // Of each event, the addresses it names: nothing else is kept.
    keep: (value) => {
      const names = addressesNamed(value, seen.author, byTag, checks);
      return names.length > 0 ? names : void 0;
    }
  });
  if (options.signal?.aborted)
    throw options.signal.reason;
  if (!answered) {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
  }
  for (const kept of answers.values()) {
    for (const names of kept)
      for (const address of names)
        named.add(address);
  }
  return named;
}
async function publishWitness(pool, seen, witness, relays, options) {
  const event = witnessEvent(seen, witness, options.createdAt, options.random);
  try {
    await pool.publishToQuorum(relays, event, seen.secretKey, majority(relays.length));
  } catch (error) {
    if (options.signal?.aborted)
      throw options.signal.reason;
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays took the witness of a payment", { cause: error });
  }
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/rates.js
var SATS_PER_BTC2 = 100000000n;
var DECIMAL = /^([0-9]+)(?:\.([0-9]+))?(?:[eE]([+-]?[0-9]+))?$/;
function parseDecimal2(value) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length > MAX_PRICE_LENGTH)
    return null;
  const match = DECIMAL.exec(value);
  if (!match)
    return null;
  const fraction = match[2] ?? "";
  const exponent = Number(match[3] ?? "0");
  if (Math.abs(exponent) > MAX_PRICE_EXPONENT)
    return null;
  let units = BigInt(match[1] + fraction);
  let scale = fraction.length - exponent;
  if (scale < 0) {
    units *= 10n ** BigInt(-scale);
    scale = 0;
  }
  while (scale > 0 && units % 10n === 0n) {
    units /= 10n;
    scale -= 1;
  }
  return units > 0n ? { units, scale } : null;
}
function numberText(value) {
  return Number.isFinite(value) ? String(value) : null;
}
function formatDecimal({ units, scale }) {
  if (scale === 0)
    return units.toString();
  const digits = units.toString().padStart(scale + 1, "0");
  return `${digits.slice(0, -scale)}.${digits.slice(-scale)}`;
}
function parseFiat(fiat) {
  if (!isObject(fiat))
    throw new InvalidOrderError("fiat", "expected { amount, currency }");
  const { currency, amount } = fiat;
  const digits = typeof currency === "string" ? currencyDigits(currency) : null;
  if (typeof currency !== "string" || digits === null) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("fiat", "currency: an ISO 4217 code in upper case, as CHF");
  }
  if (typeof amount !== "string") {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("fiat", 'amount: a string of digits, as "24.50" (never a float)');
  }
  const match = /^([0-9]{1,15})(?:\.([0-9]{1,15}))?$/.exec(amount);
  if (!match)
    throw new InvalidOrderError("fiat", 'amount: digits, as "24.50"');
  const fraction = (match[2] ?? "").replace(/0+$/, "");
  if (fraction.length > digits) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("fiat", `amount: ${currency} has ${digits} decimals at most`);
  }
  const minor = BigInt(match[1]) * 10n ** BigInt(digits) + BigInt(fraction.padEnd(digits, "0") || "0");
  if (minor < 1n || minor > MAX_MINOR_UNITS) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("fiat", "amount: more than 0, and not that large");
  }
  return {
    amount: formatDecimal({ units: minor, scale: digits }),
    currency,
    minor: minor.toString(),
    digits
  };
}
function minorToSats(minor, digits, price) {
  const dividend = minor * SATS_PER_BTC2 * 10n ** BigInt(price.scale);
  const divisor = price.units * 10n ** BigInt(digits);
  return (dividend + divisor - 1n) / divisor;
}
function fiatToSats(fiat, price) {
  const parsed = parseFiat(fiat);
  const decimal2 = parseDecimal2(price);
  if (!decimal2)
    throw new RateError("A price must be a decimal string above 0");
  return minorToSats(BigInt(parsed.minor), parsed.digits, decimal2);
}
var RATE_SOURCES2 = ["coingecko", "mempool", "opennode", "coinbase", "kraken"];
var DEFAULT_RATE_SOURCES = Object.freeze([
  "coingecko",
  "mempool",
  "coinbase",
  "kraken",
  "opennode"
]);
function isRateSource(value) {
  return typeof value === "function" || RATE_SOURCES2.includes(value);
}
function rateSourceList(value) {
  if (isRateSource(value))
    return [value];
  if (!Array.isArray(value) || value.length === 0)
    return null;
  const sources = value.slice();
  if (!sources.every(isRateSource))
    return null;
  const names = sources.filter((source) => typeof source === "string");
  return new Set(names).size === names.length ? sources : null;
}
function sourceOf(source) {
  if (typeof source === "function") {
    return {
      name: "custom",
      fetchRate: async (currency, { signal }) => {
        let answer;
        try {
          answer = await source(currency, { signal });
        } catch (error) {
          if (signal?.aborted)
            throw signal.reason;
          if (error instanceof SattlemeError)
            throw error;
          throw new RateError("The custom rate source failed", { cause: error });
        }
        if (typeof answer === "string")
          return { price: answer };
        if (!isObject(answer))
          throw new RateError("The custom rate source gave no price");
        return answer;
      }
    };
  }
  switch (source) {
    case "coingecko":
      return coingecko;
    case "mempool":
      return mempool;
    case "opennode":
      return opennode;
    case "coinbase":
      return coinbase;
    case "kraken":
      return kraken;
    default:
      throw new RateError(`No rate source named ${String(source)}`);
  }
}
async function fetchRate(source, currency, context, now2) {
  const { name, fetchRate: fetchQuote } = sourceOf(source);
  const quote = await fetchQuote(currency, context);
  const price = parseDecimal2(quote?.price);
  if (!price)
    throw new RateError(`${name} gave no valid price in ${currency}`);
  const time = quote.time ?? null;
  if (time !== null) {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(time) || time > now2 + CLOCK_TOLERANCE_SECONDS) {
      throw new RateError(`${name} gave an invalid date for its price`);
    }
    if (now2 - time > MAX_RATE_AGE_SECONDS) {
      throw new RateError(`${name}'s price in ${currency} is too old`);
    }
  }
  return {
    price,
    info: {
      source: name,
      currency,
      price: formatDecimal(price),
      fetchedAt: now2,
      time
    }
  };
}
async function fetchFirstRate(sources, currency, context, now2, logger, ask = fetchRate) {
  let failure = null;
  let unsupported = null;
  for (const [index, source] of sources.entries()) {
    try {
      return await ask(source, currency, context, now2());
    } catch (error) {
      if (context.signal?.aborted)
        throw context.signal.reason;
      if (error instanceof UnsupportedCurrencyError)
        unsupported = error;
      else if (error instanceof NetworkError || error instanceof RateError)
        failure = error;
      else
        throw error;
      const next = sources[index + 1];
      if (next !== void 0) {
        logger.warn(`Rate source ${sourceName(source)} failed (${error.code}): ${error.message}; trying ${sourceName(next)}`);
      }
    }
  }
  if (failure)
    throw failure;
  if (sources.length > 1) {
    const names = sources.map(sourceName).join(", ");
    throw new UnsupportedCurrencyError(currency, names, `None of ${names} has a price in ${currency}`);
  }
  throw unsupported;
}
function sourceName(source) {
  return typeof source === "function" ? "custom" : source;
}
async function sourceJson(name, url, context, headers) {
  const http = {
    fetch: context.fetch,
    timeoutMs: context.timeoutMs,
    signal: context.signal,
    ...headers ? { headers } : {}
  };
  const { status, ok: ok2, body } = await getJson(url, http);
  if (!ok2) {
    if (status >= 500 && status <= 599 || status === 408 || status === 429) {
      throw new NetworkError(`${name} answered HTTP ${status}`);
    }
    throw new RateError(`${name} answered HTTP ${status}`);
  }
  if (!isObject(body))
    throw new RateError(`${name} gave no JSON object`);
  return body;
}
function objectAt(value, name) {
  if (!isObject(value))
    throw new RateError(`${name}: unexpected answer`);
  return value;
}
function priceOf2(value, name, currency) {
  if (value === void 0)
    throw new UnsupportedCurrencyError(currency, name);
  const text = typeof value === "string" ? value : typeof value === "number" ? numberText(value) : null;
  if (text === null)
    throw new RateError(`${name}: unexpected price`);
  return text;
}
var coingecko = {
  name: "coingecko",
  fetchRate: async (currency, context) => {
    const code = currency.toLowerCase();
    const url = `https://api.coingecko.com/api/v3/simple/price?ids=bitcoin&vs_currencies=${code}&include_last_updated_at=true`;
    const headers = context.coingeckoApiKey ? { "x-cg-demo-api-key": context.coingeckoApiKey } : void 0;
    const body = await sourceJson("coingecko", url, context, headers);
    const bitcoin = objectAt(body.bitcoin, "coingecko");
    const time = bitcoin.last_updated_at;
    return {
      price: priceOf2(bitcoin[code], "coingecko", currency),
      time: typeof time === "number" ? time : null
    };
  }
};
var MEMPOOL_CURRENCIES = ["USD", "EUR", "GBP", "CAD", "CHF", "AUD", "JPY"];
var mempool = {
  name: "mempool",
  fetchRate: async (currency, context) => {
    if (!MEMPOOL_CURRENCIES.includes(currency)) {
      throw new UnsupportedCurrencyError(currency, "mempool");
    }
    const body = await sourceJson("mempool", "https://mempool.space/api/v1/prices", context);
    const time = body.time;
    if (typeof time !== "number")
      throw new RateError("mempool: no time");
    return { price: priceOf2(body[currency], "mempool", currency), time };
  }
};
var opennode = {
  name: "opennode",
  fetchRate: async (currency, context) => {
    const body = await sourceJson("opennode", "https://api.opennode.com/v1/rates", context);
    const data2 = objectAt(body.data, "opennode");
    const pair = data2[`BTC${currency}`];
    if (pair === void 0)
      throw new UnsupportedCurrencyError(currency, "opennode");
    return { price: priceOf2(objectAt(pair, "opennode")[currency], "opennode", currency) };
  }
};
var coinbase = {
  name: "coinbase",
  fetchRate: async (currency, context) => {
    const body = await sourceJson("coinbase", "https://api.coinbase.com/v2/exchange-rates?currency=BTC", context);
    const data2 = objectAt(body.data, "coinbase");
    if (data2.currency !== "BTC")
      throw new RateError("coinbase: not the rates of BTC");
    return { price: priceOf2(objectAt(data2.rates, "coinbase")[currency], "coinbase", currency) };
  }
};
var kraken = {
  name: "kraken",
  fetchRate: async (currency, context) => {
    const body = await sourceJson("kraken", `https://api.kraken.com/0/public/Ticker?pair=XBT${currency}`, context);
    const errors = body.error;
    if (!Array.isArray(errors))
      throw new RateError("kraken: unexpected answer");
    if (errors.some((e) => typeof e === "string" && e.startsWith("EQuery:Unknown asset pair"))) {
      throw new UnsupportedCurrencyError(currency, "kraken");
    }
    if (errors.length > 0)
      throw new RateError(`kraken: ${String(errors[0])}`);
    const entries = Object.values(objectAt(body.result, "kraken"));
    if (entries.length !== 1)
      throw new RateError("kraken: unexpected answer");
    const last = objectAt(entries[0], "kraken").c;
    if (!Array.isArray(last))
      throw new RateError("kraken: unexpected answer");
    return { price: priceOf2(last[0], "kraken", currency) };
  }
};

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/order.js
var RATE_SOURCE_RULE = `${RATE_SOURCES2.map((name) => `"${name}"`).join(", ")} or a function, or a non-empty list of them without the same name twice`;
function rateSourceOption(value) {
  const sources = value === void 0 ? DEFAULT_RATE_SOURCES : rateSourceList(value);
  if (!sources)
    throw new InvalidOptionError("rateSource", RATE_SOURCE_RULE);
  return sources;
}
function checkRef(ref) {
  if (typeof ref !== "string" || !REF_PATTERN.test(ref))
    throw new InvalidOrderError("ref", REF_RULE);
  return ref;
}
function checkOrder(order, clientRateSources) {
  if (!isObject(order)) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("order", "expected { ref, sats | fiat, durationSeconds?, note? }");
  }
  const ref = checkRef(order.ref);
  if (order.sats === void 0 === (order.fiat === void 0)) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("sats", "give either sats or fiat");
  }
  const sats = order.sats === void 0 ? null : checkSats(order.sats);
  const fiat = order.fiat === void 0 ? null : parseFiat(order.fiat);
  let rateSources = null;
  if (order.rateSource !== void 0) {
    if (!fiat) {
      throw new InvalidOrderError("rateSource", "only for an order priced in a currency (fiat)");
    }
    rateSources = rateSourceList(order.rateSource);
    if (!rateSources)
      throw new InvalidOrderError("rateSource", RATE_SOURCE_RULE);
  } else if (fiat) {
    rateSources = clientRateSources;
  }
  const durationSeconds = order.durationSeconds === void 0 ? DEFAULT_DURATION_SECONDS : order.durationSeconds;
  if (!Number.isInteger(durationSeconds) || durationSeconds < MIN_DURATION_SECONDS || durationSeconds > MAX_DURATION_SECONDS) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("durationSeconds", `${MIN_DURATION_SECONDS} to ${MAX_DURATION_SECONDS} seconds`);
  }
  const label = labelOf(order.note, ref);
  const { checkout, redirectUrl } = checkoutOf(order);
  return { ref, sats, fiat, rateSources, durationSeconds, label, checkout, redirectUrl };
}
function checkoutOf(order) {
  if (order.checkout !== void 0 && typeof order.checkout !== "boolean") {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("checkout", "true or false");
  }
  const checkout = order.checkout ?? false;
  if (order.redirectUrl === void 0 || order.redirectUrl === null) {
    return { checkout, redirectUrl: null };
  }
  if (!checkout) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("redirectUrl", "only with checkout: true");
  }
  if (redirectShown(order.redirectUrl) === null) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("redirectUrl", "an https URL (lowercase host, no user name nor password) or an app's link (scheme://\u2026), 2048 characters at most, without spaces nor other characters to escape");
  }
  return { checkout, redirectUrl: order.redirectUrl };
}
function checkSats(value) {
  const sats = typeof value === "bigint" ? value : typeof value === "number" && Number.isSafeInteger(value) || typeof value === "string" && /^[0-9]{1,18}$/.test(value) ? BigInt(value) : null;
  if (sats === null || sats < 1n || sats > MAX_SATS) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("sats", `a whole number of sats, from 1 to ${MAX_SATS}`);
  }
  return sats;
}
function labelOf(note, ref) {
  if (note === void 0)
    return ref;
  if (typeof note !== "string")
    throw new InvalidOrderError("note", "a string");
  if (hasControlCharacter(note))
    throw new InvalidOrderError("note", "no control characters");
  if (!isWellFormed(note))
    throw new InvalidOrderError("note", "well-formed Unicode");
  const label = note.replace(/^ +| +$/g, "") || ref;
  if (codePointLength(label) > MAX_NOTE_LENGTH) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("note", `${MAX_NOTE_LENGTH} characters at most`);
  }
  return label;
}
var NOT_STORED = "not an invoice made by createInvoice";
function checkStoredInvoice(invoice) {
  if (!isObject(invoice))
    throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", NOT_STORED);
  const { bolt11, paymentHash, verifyUrl, expiresAt } = invoice;
  const sats = typeof invoice.sats === "string" && /^[0-9]{1,16}$/.test(invoice.sats) ? BigInt(invoice.sats) : null;
  if (sats === null || sats < 1n || sats > MAX_SATS || !Number.isSafeInteger(expiresAt)) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", NOT_STORED);
  }
  let lightning = null;
  if (bolt11 === null) {
    if (paymentHash !== null || verifyUrl !== null) {
      throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", NOT_STORED);
    }
  } else {
    const decoded = typeof bolt11 === "string" ? decodeBolt11(bolt11) : null;
    if (!decoded || decoded.network !== "bc" || decoded.amountMsat !== sats * 1000n || !isHex32(paymentHash) || decoded.paymentHash !== paymentHash || decoded.timestamp + decoded.expirySeconds !== expiresAt || !parseHttpsUrl(verifyUrl)) {
      throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", NOT_STORED);
    }
    lightning = { bolt11, paymentHash, verifyUrl, expiresAt };
  }
  const address = invoice.onchainAddress ?? null;
  if (address !== null && !isDepositAddress(address)) {
    throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", NOT_STORED);
  }
  if (lightning === null && address === null)
    throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", NOT_STORED);
  let ref = null;
  if (address !== null) {
    if (typeof invoice.ref !== "string" || !REF_PATTERN.test(invoice.ref)) {
      throw new InvalidOrderError("invoice", `${NOT_STORED}: with an address, its ref (${REF_RULE})`);
    }
    ref = invoice.ref;
  }
  return { ref, lightning, onchainAddress: address, sats, expiresAt };
}
function checkMilliseconds(value, fallback, min, max, name) {
  if (value === void 0)
    return fallback;
  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(value) || value < min || value > max) {
    throw new InvalidOptionError(name, `a whole number of milliseconds, ${min} to ${max}`);
  }
  return value;
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/sales.js
async function readSaleDeclarations(pool, sale, relays, { signal, logger, everyRelay = false }) {
  const needed = majority(relays.length);
  const { answers, answered } = await pool.queryRelays(relays, { kinds: [RECORD_KIND], authors: [sale.author] }, sale.secretKey, {
    paged: true,
    everyRelay,
    // Its declarations and its records at registry addresses: sealed with the outer key of their
    // address and of the sale.
    keep: recordsThatOpen((d, author) => author === sale.author && d !== null ? outerKey(sale.registry.key, d, author) : null)
  });
  if (signal?.aborted)
    throw signal.reason;
  if (!answered) {
    throw new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
  }
  const declarations = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const records = [];
  const checked = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  const sentBy = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  let newer = false;
  for (const events of answers.values()) {
    const ids = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
    for (const value of events)
      if (hasValidId(value))
        ids.add(value.id);
    for (const id of ids)
      sentBy.set(id, (sentBy.get(id) ?? 0) + 1);
    for (const value of events) {
      if (isObject(value) && typeof value.id === "string" && checked.has(value.id))
        continue;
      let declaration;
      let lease = null;
      try {
        declaration = openDeclaration(value, sale);
        if (!declaration)
          lease = openSaleLease(value, sale);
      } catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof NewerRecordError))
          continue;
        newer = true;
        const d = dTagOf(value);
        if (d !== null) {
          const event = value;
          checked.add(event.id);
          records.push({ kind: "newer", event, d });
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (declaration) {
        checked.add(declaration.event.id);
        const hash = declaration.decoded.paymentHash;
        if (!declarations.has(hash))
          declarations.set(hash, declaration);
      } else if (lease) {
        checked.add(leaseEventOf(lease).id);
        records.push(lease);
      }
    }
  }
  if (newer) {
    logger.warn("@sattleme/checkout: a declaration or a lease of this order has a newer format than this version reads; update the package");
  }
  const newest = newestRecords(records);
  const held = newest.flatMap((record) => record.kind === "lease" ? [record.lease] : []);
  const rests = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const unsureRests = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  const invoices = [];
  for (const declaration of declarations.values()) {
    if (declaration.rest === null) {
      invoices.push(declaration);
      continue;
    }
    if ((sentBy.get(declaration.event.id) ?? 0) < needed) {
      unsureRests.set(declaration.decoded.paymentHash, declaration.event);
    }
    const key = restKeyOf(declaration.rest.address, declaration.rest.expiresAt);
    const list = rests.get(key);
    if (list)
      list.push(restInvoiceOf(declaration));
    else
      rests.set(key, [restInvoiceOf(declaration)]);
  }
  for (const list of rests.values())
    list.sort(byRestOrder);
  return {
    invoices: invoicesOf(sale.ref, invoices, newest),
    rests,
    unsureRests,
    leases: new Map(held.map((lease) => [lease.terms.address, lease])),
    sentBy
  };
}
function newestRecords(records) {
  const newest = /* @__PURE__ */ new Map();
  for (const record of records) {
    const d = record.kind === "lease" ? record.lease.d : record.d;
    const known = newest.get(d);
    if (!known || replaces(leaseEventOf(record), leaseEventOf(known)))
      newest.set(d, record);
  }
  return [...newest.values()];
}
function invoicesOf(ref, declarations, newest) {
  const confirmed = newest.flatMap((record) => record.kind === "lease" && isConfirmedLease(record.lease.terms) ? [record.lease] : []);
  const found = [];
  for (const declaration of declarations) {
    if (declaration.rest !== null)
      continue;
    const { decoded } = declaration;
    const invoice = asciiLower(declaration.invoice);
    const lease = confirmed.filter(({ terms }) => terms.invoice !== null && asciiLower(terms.invoice) === invoice).sort((a, b) => replaces(a.event, b.event) ? -1 : 1)[0];
    const sats = decoded.amountMsat / 1000n;
    const onchainAddress = lease?.terms.address ?? null;
    found.push({
      invoice: {
        ref,
        bolt11: declaration.invoice,
        paymentHash: decoded.paymentHash,
        verifyUrl: declaration.verifyUrl,
        onchainAddress,
        paymentUri: paymentUri(onchainAddress, sats, declaration.invoice),
        sats: sats.toString(),
        fiat: fiatOf(declaration.price),
        note: declaration.note,
        createdAt: decoded.timestamp,
        expiresAt: decoded.timestamp + decoded.expirySeconds,
        declaredAt: declaration.declaredAt
      },
      event: declaration.event,
      lease: lease?.event ?? null
    });
  }
  for (const lease of confirmed) {
    if (lease.terms.invoice !== null)
      continue;
    const { terms } = lease;
    found.push({
      invoice: {
        ref,
        bolt11: null,
        paymentHash: null,
        verifyUrl: null,
        onchainAddress: terms.address,
        paymentUri: paymentUri(terms.address, terms.sats, null),
        sats: terms.sats.toString(),
        fiat: fiatOf(terms.price),
        note: terms.note,
        createdAt: terms.createdAt,
        expiresAt: terms.expiresAt,
        // Its mark's date (SPEC.md, "Leasing an address").
        declaredAt: lease.event.created_at
      },
      event: null,
      lease: lease.event
    });
  }
  return found.sort(({ invoice: a }, { invoice: b }) => a.createdAt - b.createdAt || compare(a.paymentHash ?? "", b.paymentHash ?? "") || compare(a.onchainAddress ?? "", b.onchainAddress ?? ""));
}
function mayStillBePaid(invoices, now2) {
  return invoices.flatMap(({ expiresAt }, index) => now2 < expiresAt + GRACE_SECONDS2 ? [index] : []);
}
function lastRecordOf(invoice) {
  return invoice.event ?? invoice.lease;
}
function heldBy(invoices, sentBy, relays) {
  if (sentBy === null)
    return null;
  const needed = majority(relays);
  return invoices.map((invoice) => (sentBy.get(lastRecordOf(invoice).id) ?? 0) >= needed);
}
function pendingOutcome(statuses, held) {
  const paid = statuses.findIndex((status) => status.status === "fulfilled" && status.value.state === "paid");
  if (paid >= 0)
    return { paid };
  const failed2 = statuses.findIndex((status) => status.status === "rejected");
  if (failed2 >= 0)
    return { failed: failed2 };
  if (statuses.length === 1)
    return { handOut: 0 };
  if (held === null)
    return { unread: true };
  const shown = held.flatMap((isHeld, index) => isHeld ? [index] : []);
  if (shown.length === 1)
    return { handOut: shown[0] };
  const received = statuses.findLastIndex((status) => status.status === "fulfilled" && (status.value.state === "unconfirmed" || status.value.state === "open" && BigInt(status.value.received ?? "0") > 0n));
  return { handOut: received >= 0 ? received : statuses.length - 1 };
}
function restKeyOf(address, expiresAt) {
  return `${address}/${expiresAt}`;
}
function leaseEventOf(record) {
  return record.kind === "lease" ? record.lease.event : record.event;
}
function compare(a, b) {
  return a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0;
}
function fiatOf(price) {
  if (price.currency === SATS_CURRENCY)
    return null;
  return {
    amount: formatDecimal({ units: BigInt(price.minor), scale: price.digits }),
    currency: price.currency,
    minor: price.minor.toString(),
    digits: price.digits
  };
}

    maxPayload: MAX_RELAY_MESSAGE_BYTES,
    handshakeTimeout: timeoutMs,
    followRedirects: false,
    perMessageDeflate: false,
    headers: { "User-Agent": USER_AGENT }
  });
}

// node_modules/@sattleme/checkout/dist/client.js
var _access, _rateSources, _coingeckoApiKey, _fetch, _createSocket, _relayTimeoutMs, _httpTimeoutMs, _logger, _now2, _random2, _orders, _restAsks, _Sattleme_instances, createInvoice_fn, leaseAddress_fn, withdraw_fn, publishLeaseRecord_fn, publishWitnessesOfNone_fn, lot_fn, publishCheckout_fn, checkEarlierInvoices_fn, declare_fn, publishLease_fn, check_fn, addressParts_fn, publishAcceptance_fn, askRest_fn, publishRest_fn, publishMissingRests_fn, waitWithAddress_fn, declared_fn, channel_fn, withRelays_fn, http_fn, seconds_fn;

    return { teamPublicKey: __privateGet(this, _access).teamPublicKey, relays: __privateGet(this, _access).relays };
  }
  /**
   * The merchant: its business's name, the bounds of its Lightning address in sats, and how many
   * deposit addresses the integration's lot holds. Throws RevokedError when the merchant removed
   * the integration. Never its Lightning address.
   */
  async merchant({ signal } = {}) {
    return __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, withRelays_fn).call(this, signal, async (pool, signal2) => {
      const merchant = await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, channel_fn).call(this, pool, signal2);
      const payRequest3 = fetchPayRequest(merchant.lightningAddress, __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, http_fn).call(this, signal2));
      payRequest3.catch(ignore3);
      const lot = await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, lot_fn).call(this, pool, merchant, signal2);
      const { min, max } = sendableSats(await payRequest3);
      return {
        businessName: merchant.businessName,
        minSats: min.toString(),

      };
    });
  }
  /**
   * The invoice of an order: a Lightning invoice asked to the merchant's Lightning address and
   * checked, when its amount is within the address's bounds; a deposit address of the merchant's
   * wallet leased from the integration's lot, from 1 000 sats (or both). Then the invoice is
   * declared in the merchant's registry on a majority of its relays. Only then may it be shown.
   * Store the result with the order.
   *
   * One order, one invoice at a time: throws InvoicePendingError (with that invoice) while an
   * earlier invoice of the same `ref` may still be paid (or its on-chain payment is being
   * confirmed), OrderPaidError when one was paid. OnchainUnavailableError when the order can only
   * be paid on-chain and no address could be leased.
   *
   * With `checkout: true`, the invoice also gets a checkout link (`checkoutUrl`): a page of the
   * merchant's web app that shows it to the customer, checks its payment and, once it is paid,
   * sends the customer to `redirectUrl`. It is published on a majority of the merchant's relays
   * before the invoice is declared. Throws CheckoutUnavailableError when the merchant's app gives
   * no web app for it.
   */
  async createInvoice(order, options = {}) {
    const checked = checkOrder(order, __privateGet(this, _rateSources));
    const previous = __privateGet(this, _orders).get(checked.ref) ?? Promise.resolve();
    const run = previous.catch(ignore3).then(() => __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, createInvoice_fn).call(this, checked, options));
    const settled = run.catch(ignore3);
    __privateGet(this, _orders).set(checked.ref, settled);
    void settled.then(() => {
      if (__privateGet(this, _orders).get(checked.ref) === settled)
        __privateGet(this, _orders).delete(checked.ref);
    });
    return run;
  }
  /**
   * Where the payment of an invoice is, checked: its Lightning part from the merchant's Lightning
   * address (LUD-21), its on-chain part from the explorer (mempool.space, or the merchant's Bitcoin
   * node with its fallback, SPEC.md §31) and the witnesses of its order, and the Lightning invoices
   * of the rest of an on-chain underpayment, all at once. `open` (not paid yet,
   * 120 s after its end included; `received` above 0 for an on-chain underpayment, whose `rest` is
   * still payable), `unconfirmed` (enough received in time, its on-chain part not 3 confirmations
   * deep yet), `paid` (in Lightning, with the preimage, the proof of payment; on-chain; or mixed:
   * on-chain and invoices of the rest), or `expired`, for good (but for a merchant's acceptance
   * a relay that holds it failed to give, SPEC.md §3). Underpaid on-chain, it asks the
   * merchant's Lightning address for an invoice of the rest, declares it and publishes it for the
   * order's checkout links (`rest.bolt11`; a failure is logged, the next check asks again). It
   * never opens the explorer's WebSocket. Throws NetworkError (retry) when a service does not
   * answer (the merchant's node with `fallback` `"none"` included: never `expired` for want of an
   * answer), InvalidInvoiceError when an answer is not about this invoice or its preimage is wrong.
   */
  async getStatus(invoice, options = {}) {
    const stored = checkStoredInvoice(invoice);
    return statusOf(stored, await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, check_fn).call(this, stored, options.signal), __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, seconds_fn).call(this));
  }
  /**
   * Checks the payment until it is `paid` or `expired`: its Lightning part, and the invoices of the
   * rest of an on-chain underpayment, every `intervalMs`; its on-chain part (with an address) each
   * time the explorer's WebSocket (mempool.space's, or the merchant's Bitcoin node's with its
   * fallback) says something changed for the address, every 60 s at least, and at once when an
   * invoice of the rest is paid. Network errors are retried (one that each check
   * meets again logged once, and again only after it stopped), `onStatus` hears each status.
   * Rejects with `signal`'s reason when it aborts, and with any error that is not a network
   * one. An on-chain payment takes about 30 minutes to be `paid` once `unconfirmed`: a server that
   * cannot wait that long checks again later with getStatus.
   */
  async waitForPayment(invoice, { intervalMs, signal, onStatus } = {}) {
    const interval = checkMilliseconds(intervalMs, DEFAULT_INTERVAL_MS, MIN_INTERVAL_MS, MAX_INTERVAL_MS, "intervalMs");
    optionalFunction(onStatus, "onStatus");
    const stored = checkStoredInvoice(invoice);
    const warnings = onceWarnings(__privateGet(this, _logger));
    if (stored.onchainAddress) {
      return __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, waitWithAddress_fn).call(this, stored, interval, signal, onStatus, warnings);
    }
    for (; ; ) {
      if (signal?.aborted)
        throw signal.reason;
      try {
        const parts = await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, check_fn).call(this, stored, signal, void 0, warnings.logger);
        const status = statusOf(stored, parts, __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, seconds_fn).call(this));
        onStatus?.(status);
        if (status.state === "paid" || status.state === "expired")
          return status;
      } catch (error) {
        if (signal?.aborted)
          throw signal.reason;
        if (!(error instanceof NetworkError))
          throw error;
        warnings.failed(error);
      }
      warnings.checked();
      await sleep(interval, signal);
    }
  }
  /**
   * The invoices declared for the order `ref` in the merchant's registry, oldest first: to find an
   * order's invoice again after a failure. Read on a majority of the merchant's relays (any
   * declaration and lease was published on one); throws NetworkError when fewer answered.
   */
  async findInvoices(ref, { signal } = {}) {
    checkRef(ref);
    return __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, withRelays_fn).call(this, signal, async (pool, signal2) => {
      const merchant = await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, channel_fn).call(this, pool, signal2);
      const sale = saleOf(__privateGet(this, _access).key, merchant.registry, ref);
      const declared = await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, declared_fn).call(this, pool, sale, merchant, signal2);
      return declared.invoices.map(({ invoice }) => invoice);
    });
  }
};

checkEarlierInvoices_fn = async function(pool, sale, merchant, order, signal) {
  const records = await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, declared_fn).call(this, pool, sale, merchant, signal);
  const earlier = records.invoices;
  const check = (invoices) => settleEach(invoices, MAX_PARALLEL_PAYMENT_CHECKS, async ({ invoice }) => {
    const stored = storedOf(invoice);
    return statusOf(stored, await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, check_fn).call(this, stored, signal, { pool, merchant, records }), __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, seconds_fn).call(this));
  });
  const mayBePaid = mayStillBePaid(earlier.map(({ invoice }) => invoice), __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, seconds_fn).call(this)).map((index) => earlier[index]);
  if (mayBePaid.length > 0) {
    const [statuses2, everywhere] = await Promise.all([
      check(mayBePaid),
      mayBePaid.length > 1 ? settle(__privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, declared_fn).call(this, pool, sale, merchant, signal, { everyRelay: true })) : null
    ]);
    if (signal?.aborted)
      throw signal.reason;
    const held = heldBy(mayBePaid, everywhere?.ok ? everywhere.value.sentBy : null, merchant.relays.length);
    const outcome = pendingOutcome(statuses2, held);
    if ("paid" in outcome) {
      const status = statuses2[outcome.paid];
      throw new OrderPaidError(mayBePaid[outcome.paid].invoice, status.value);
    }
    if ("failed" in outcome)
      throw statuses2[outcome.failed].reason;
    if ("unread" in outcome) {
      throw everywhere?.ok === false ? everywhere.error : new NetworkError("Fewer than a majority of the merchant's relays answered");
    }
    const open = mayBePaid[outcome.handOut];
    if (open.event)
      await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, declare_fn).call(this, pool, open.event, sale, merchant, signal);
    if (open.lease)
      await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, publishLease_fn).call(this, pool, open.lease, sale, merchant, signal);
    const { invoice } = open;
    const checkoutUrl2 = order.checkout ? await __privateMethod(this, _Sattleme_instances, publishCheckout_fn).call(this, pool, merchant, checkoutValue({
      businessName: merchant.businessName,
      invoice: invoice.bolt11,
      verifyUrl: invoice.verifyUrl,
      onchainAddress: invoice.onchainAddress,
      sats: BigInt(invoice.sats),
      createdAt: invoice.createdAt,
      expiresAt: invoice.expiresAt,
      fiat: invoice.fiat ? {
        minor: Number(invoice.fiat.minor),
        digits: invoice.fiat.digits,
        currency: invoice.fiat.currency
      } : null,
      note: invoice.note,
      redirectUrl: order.redirectUrl,
      rest: invoice.onchainAddress === null ? null : restKeys(__privateGet(this, _access).key, order.ref, invoice.onchainAddress, invoice.expiresAt),
      bitcoinNode: recordNodeFor(merchant.bitcoinNode)
    }), invoice.expiresAt, signal) : null;
    throw new InvoicePendingError(invoice, checkoutUrl2);
  }
  const statuses = await check(earlier);
  if (signal?.aborted)
    throw signal.reason;
  for (const [index, status] of statuses.entries()) {
    if (status.status === "fulfilled" && status.value.state === "paid") {
      throw new OrderPaidError(earlier[index].invoice, status.value);
    }
  }
  const pending = statuses.findIndex((status) => status.status === "fulfilled" && (status.value.state === "unconfirmed" || status.value.state === "open"));

    throw new InvoicePendingError(earlier[pending].invoice, null);

  if (failed2)
    throw failed2.reason;

function onceWarnings(logger) {

  let now2 = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();

function shuffle(items, random) {
  const shuffled = [...items];
  for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i -= 1) {
    const bound = i + 1;
    const limit = 2 ** 32 - 2 ** 32 % bound;
    let draw;
    do {
      const bytes = random(4);
      draw = (bytes[0] << 24 | bytes[1] << 16 | bytes[2] << 8 | bytes[3]) >>> 0;
    } while (draw >= limit);
    const j = draw % bound;
    [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
  }
  return shuffled;
}

function optionalFunction(value, name) {
  if (value !== void 0 && typeof value !== "function") {
    throw new InvalidOptionError(name, "a function");
  }
  return value;
}
async function settleEach(items, limit, run) {
  const results = new Array(items.length);
  let next = 0;
  const worker = async () => {
    while (next < items.length) {
      const index = next++;
      try {
        results[index] = { status: "fulfilled", value: await run(items[index]) };
      } catch (reason) {
        results[index] = { status: "rejected", reason };
      }
    }
  };
  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(limit, items.length) }, worker));
  return results;
}
function sleep(ms, signal) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) {
      reject(signal.reason);
      return;
    }
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      signal?.removeEventListener("abort", abort);
      resolve();
    }, ms);
    const abort = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      reject(signal.reason);
    };
    signal?.addEventListener("abort", abort, { once: true });
  });
}
