// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/wix.js
var wix_exports = {};
__export(wix_exports, {
  LIMITS: () => LIMITS,
  addOrderComment: () => addOrderComment,
  copyOf: () => copyOf,
  createTask: () => createTask,
  data: () => data,
  ensureCollection: () => ensureCollection,
  getOrder: () => getOrder,
  isDuplicate: () => isDuplicate,
  notifyOwner: () => notifyOwner,
  orderComments: () => orderComments,
  readSecret: () => readSecret,
  siteLanguage: () => siteLanguage,
  siteTimeZone: () => siteTimeZone,
  submitTransactionEvent: () => submitTransactionEvent,
  tasksSince: () => tasksSince,
  writeCount: () => writeCount
});
import wixData from "wix-data";
import { collections } from "wix-data.v2";
import { elevate } from "wix-auth";
import { secrets } from "wix-secrets-backend.v2";
import wixPaymentProviderBackend from "wix-payment-provider-backend";
import { orders as veloOrders } from "wix-ecom-backend";
import { notifications } from "wix-crm-backend";
import { tasks } from "wix-crm.v2";
import wixSiteBackend from "wix-site-backend";
import { orders as sdkOrders } from "@wix/ecom";
import { auth } from "@wix/essentials";
var LIMITS = {
  taskTitle: 250,
  taskDescription: 500,
  comment: 5e3,
  notice: 512
};
var GUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
var DATA = { suppressAuth: true, suppressHooks: true };
var CONSISTENT = { suppressAuth: true, suppressHooks: true, consistentRead: true };
async function readSecret(name) {
  let answer;
  try {
    answer = await elevate(secrets.getSecretValue)(name);
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
  const value = typeof answer === "string" ? answer : answer && typeof answer.value === "string" ? answer.value : null;
  if (value === null || value.trim() === "") return null;
  const secret = value.trim();
  rememberSecret(secret);
  return secret;
}
async function ensureCollection() {
  try {
    await elevate(collections.getDataCollection)(COLLECTION);
  } catch (missing) {
    try {
      await elevate(collections.createDataCollection)(
        /** @type {*} */
        {
          _id: COLLECTION,
          displayName: "sattle.me Checkout",
          permissions: { read: "ADMIN", insert: "ADMIN", update: "ADMIN", remove: "ADMIN" },
          fields: [
            { key: "kind", displayName: "kind", type: "TEXT" },
            { key: "active", displayName: "active", type: "BOOLEAN" },
            { key: "dueAt", displayName: "dueAt", type: "NUMBER" },
            { key: "contactId", displayName: "contactId", type: "TEXT" },
            { key: "unpaid", displayName: "unpaid", type: "BOOLEAN" },
            { key: "data", displayName: "data", type: "OBJECT" }
          ]
        }
      );
    } catch (error) {
      await elevate(collections.getDataCollection)(COLLECTION);
    }
  }
}
function isDuplicate(error) {
  return !!error && (error.code === "WDE0074" || /WDE0074/.test(String(error.message)));
}
function copyOf(value) {
  if (value instanceof Date) return new Date(value.getTime());
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(copyOf);
  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    const copy = {};
    for (const key of Object.keys(value)) copy[key] = copyOf(value[key]);
    return copy;
  }
  return value;
}
var writes = 0;
function writeCount() {
  return writes;
}
var data = {
  /** Inserts `item`; rejects (isDuplicate) when an item of its _id exists. */
  insert(item) {
    writes += 1;
    return wixData.insert(COLLECTION, copyOf(item), DATA);
  },
  /** Inserts or replaces `item`. */
  save(item) {
    writes += 1;
    return wixData.save(COLLECTION, copyOf(item), DATA);
  },
  /** The item `id` as Wix Data's primary instance has it (consistentRead), or null. */
  async get(id) {
    return await wixData.get(COLLECTION, id, CONSISTENT) || null;
  },
  bulkRemove(ids) {
    writes += 1;
    return wixData.bulkRemove(COLLECTION, ids, DATA);
  },
  /**
   * Items of `kind`, at most `limit`: with `dueBefore`, those due by then (`active` ones only, when
   * `active` is given, `unpaid` ones only when `unpaid` is), the soonest first; with `contactId`,
   * those of that contact, by id (from `skip`); else the latest first. From the primary instance
   * with `consistent`, else from a mirror (seconds behind).
   * @param {{ kind: string, active?: boolean, unpaid?: boolean, dueBefore?: number,
   *   contactId?: string, limit: number, skip?: number, consistent?: boolean }} criteria
   */
  async query({ kind, active, unpaid, dueBefore, contactId, limit, skip, consistent }) {
    let query = wixData.query(COLLECTION).eq("kind", kind);
    if (active !== void 0) query = query.eq("active", active);
    if (unpaid !== void 0) query = query.eq("unpaid", unpaid);
    if (contactId !== void 0) {
      query = query.eq("contactId", contactId).ascending("_id");
    } else {
      query = dueBefore !== void 0 ? query.le("dueAt", dueBefore).ascending("dueAt") : query.descending("dueAt");
    }
    if (skip) query = query.skip(skip);
    const result = await query.limit(limit).find(consistent ? CONSISTENT : DATA);
    return result.items;
  }
};
async function submitTransactionEvent(transaction) {
  await wixPaymentProviderBackend.submitEvent({ event: { transaction } });
}
async function getOrder(orderId) {
  try {
    const order = await elevate(veloOrders.getOrder)(orderId);
    return order ? { order } : { error: "no order" };
  } catch (error) {
    return { error: String(error && error.message || error).slice(0, 200) };
  }
}
function orderComments(order) {
  const activities = order && Array.isArray(order.activities) ? order.activities : [];
  const messages = [];
  for (const activity of activities) {
    const type = (
      /** @type {string | undefined} */
      activity && activity.type
    );
    const comment = activity && activity.merchantComment;
    if (type === "MERCHANT_COMMENT" && comment && typeof comment.message === "string") {
      messages.push(comment.message);
    }
  }
  return messages;
}
async function addOrderComment(orderId, message) {
  const comment = { message: clip(message, LIMITS.comment) };
  await auth.elevate(sdkOrders.addActivities)(orderId, {
    orderActivities: [{ activityType: "MERCHANT_COMMENT", merchantComment: comment }]
  });
}
async function notifyOwner(title, body, link) {
  const options = { title: clip(title, LIMITS.notice), recipients: { role: "Owner" } };
  if (link) {
    Object.assign(options, {
      actionTitle: clip(link.title, LIMITS.notice),
      actionTarget: { url: link.url }
    });
  }
  await notifications.notify(clip(body, LIMITS.notice), ["Dashboard", "Mobile"], options);
}
async function createTask(title, description, contactId) {
  const task = (
    /** @type {*} */
    {
      title: clip(title, LIMITS.taskTitle),
      description: clip(description, LIMITS.taskDescription),
      dueDate: new Date(Date.now()),
      status: "ACTION_NEEDED"
    }
  );
  if (typeof contactId === "string" && GUID.test(contactId)) task.contact = { _id: contactId };
  const created = await elevate(tasks.createTask)(task);
  return created && created._id ? created._id : null;
}
async function tasksSince(since) {
  const found = await elevate(tasks.queryTasks)().ge("_createdDate", new Date(since)).find();
  return found.items.map((task) => ({
    title: typeof task.title === "string" ? task.title : "",
    description: typeof task.description === "string" ? task.description : ""
  }));
}
async function siteTimeZone() {
  try {
    const zone = await wixSiteBackend.generalInfo.getTimeZone();
    return typeof zone === "string" ? zone.slice(0, 100) : null;
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
}
async function siteLanguage() {
  try {
    const language = await wixSiteBackend.generalInfo.getLanguage();
    return typeof language === "string" ? language : null;
  } catch (error) {
    return null;
  }
}

// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/store.js
var LOCK_WALK = 20;
var LOCK_POLL_MS = 250;
var WRITE_MARGIN_MS = 15e3;
function now() {
  return Date.now();
}
function paymentIdOf(orderKey) {
  return `p${sha256Hex(orderKey).slice(0, 32)}`;
}
function isPaymentId(id) {
  return typeof id === "string" && /^p[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(id);
}
function readPayment(id) {
  return data.get(id);
}
function savePayment(item) {
  item.contactId = typeof item.data.contactId === "string" ? item.data.contactId : null;
  return data.save(item);
}
async function paymentIdsOfContact(contactId) {
  const ids = [];
  for (let skip = 0; ; skip += 1e3) {
    const page2 = await data.query({
      kind: "payment",
      contactId,
      limit: 1e3,
      skip,
      consistent: true
    });
    for (const item of page2) ids.push(item._id);
    if (page2.length < 1e3) return ids;
  }
}
function duePayments(at, limit) {
  return data.query({ kind: "payment", active: true, dueBefore: at, limit });
}
function activePayments(limit) {
  return data.query({
    kind: "payment",
    active: true,
    dueBefore: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
    limit,
    consistent: true
  });
}
function latest(kind, limit) {
  return data.query({ kind, limit, consistent: true });
}
async function forget(kind, before, active) {
  const old = await data.query({ kind, active, dueBefore: before, limit: 50 });
  if (old.length) await data.bulkRemove(old.map((item) => item._id));
  return old.length;
}
async function forgetUnpaid(before) {
  const old = await data.query({
    kind: "payment",
    active: false,
    unpaid: true,
    dueBefore: before,
    limit: 50
  });
  if (old.length) await data.bulkRemove(old.map((item) => item._id));
  return old.length;
}
async function forgetLocks(before) {
  const old = await data.query({ kind: "lock", dueBefore: before, limit: 50 });
  const ids = [];
  for (const item of old) {
    const named = /^lock-(p[0-9a-f]{32})-([0-9]+)$/.exec(item._id);
    if (!named) continue;
    const payment = await readPayment(named[1]);
    const lockGen = payment && payment.data ? payment.data.lockGen : null;
    if (!payment || Number.isSafeInteger(lockGen) && lockGen > Number(named[2])) {
      ids.push(item._id);
    }
  }
  if (ids.length) await data.bulkRemove(ids);
  return ids.length;
}
async function readConfig(id) {
  const item = await data.get(id);
  return item && item.data ? item.data : null;
}
function saveConfig(id, value) {
  return data.save({ _id: id, kind: "config", active: false, dueAt: now(), data: value });
}
function rememberedId(kind, key) {
  return `${kind}-${sha256Hex(key).slice(0, 24)}`;
}
async function recall(kind, key) {
  const item = await data.get(rememberedId(kind, key));
  return item && item.data ? item.data : null;
}
async function remember(kind, key, value) {
  const id = rememberedId(kind, key);
  const before = await data.get(id);
  const count = before && before.data ? (before.data.count || 1) + 1 : 1;
  const first = before && before.data ? before.data.first : now();
  return data.save({
    _id: id,
    kind,
    active: false,
    dueAt: now(),
    data: Object.assign({}, value, { first, count })
  });
}
function saveRun(hour, value) {
  return data.save({ _id: `run-${hour}`, kind: "run", active: false, dueAt: now(), data: value });
}
function lockId(paymentId, gen) {
  return `lock-${paymentId}-${gen}`;
}
function holding(taken) {
  return !!taken && taken.active === true && taken.dueAt > now();
}
function pause(ms) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}
async function lock(paymentId, fromGen, waitMs) {
  const end = now() + (waitMs || 0);
  const from = Number.isSafeInteger(fromGen) && fromGen >= 0 ? fromGen : 0;
  let gen = from;
  let seenHeld = false;
  for (let walked = 0; walked < LOCK_WALK; ) {
    const id = lockId(paymentId, gen);
    const taken = await data.get(id);
    if (holding(taken)) {
      if (now() + LOCK_POLL_MS > end) return { busy: true };
      seenHeld = true;
      await pause(LOCK_POLL_MS);
      continue;
    }
    if (taken) {
      gen += 1;
      walked += 1;
      seenHeld = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (seenHeld) return { freed: true };
    const until = now() + LOCK_MS;
    try {
      await data.insert({ _id: id, kind: "lock", active: true, dueAt: until, data: { gen } });
      return { held: { paymentId, id, gen, from, until } };
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isDuplicate(error)) throw error;
      seenHeld = true;
    }
  }
  return { busy: true };
}
function holdsLatest(held, lockGen) {
  return !Number.isSafeInteger(lockGen) || held.gen >= lockGen;
}
function leaseLeft(held, marginMs) {
  return now() + marginMs < held.until;
}
async function unlock(held) {
  const ids = [];
  for (let gen = Math.min(held.from, held.gen); gen <= held.gen; gen += 1) {
    ids.push(lockId(held.paymentId, gen));
  }
  await data.bulkRemove(ids);
}
async function leave(held) {
  if (leaseLeft(held, WRITE_MARGIN_MS)) await data.bulkRemove([held.id]);
}
async function dropLock(held) {
  await data.bulkRemove([held.id]);
}
async function abandon(held) {
  await data.save({
    _id: held.id,
    kind: "lock",
    active: false,
    dueAt: held.until,
    data: { gen: held.gen }
  });
}
async function dropLocks(held) {
  const ids = [];
  for (let gen = 0; gen <= held.gen; gen += 1) ids.push(lockId(held.paymentId, gen));
  for (let start = 0; start < ids.length; start += 500) {
    await data.bulkRemove(ids.slice(start, start + 500));
  }
}

// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/payments.js
var MINUTE = 6e4;
var DAY = 24 * 60 * MINUTE;
var GIVE_UP_MS = 90 * DAY;
var TRIES = REPORT_TRIES;
var STEPS = ["comment", "notice", "task"];
var TASK_LOOKBACK_MS = 10 * MINUTE;
var APPROVAL_TRIES = 2;
var APPROVAL_PATIENCE_MS = MINUTE;
var ORDER_LOOKUPS = 2;
var LOCK_WAIT_MS = 2500;
var REPEAT_WAIT_MS = 9e3;
var MARGIN_MS = 3e3;
var MIN_CUT = 40;
var VERIFY_AFTER_MS = 2 * MINUTE;
var VERIFY_EVERY_MS = 30 * MINUTE;
var VERIFY_LOOKUPS = 2;
var RECORDED = ["PAID", "PARTIALLY_REFUNDED", "FULLY_REFUNDED"];
var UNRECORDED = [
  "NOT_PAID",
  "PENDING",
  "PARTIALLY_PAID",
  "PENDING_MERCHANT",
  "CANCELED",
  "DECLINED"
];
var REASON = {
  pending: 5009,
  expired: 3035,
  amount: 3001,
  currency: 3003,
  general: 6e3,
  refund: 3022,
  accountInvalid: 2002,
  authorization: 2004,
  accountCurrency: 2009
};
var FINAL = ["paid", "expired", "attention"];
function isFinal2(data2) {
  return FINAL.indexOf(data2.state) !== -1;
}
function positive(sats) {
  return typeof sats === "string" && /^[0-9]+$/.test(sats) && BigInt(sats) > 0n;
}
function eventTarget(data2) {
  const paid = data2.paidTx ? findTransaction(data2, data2.paidTx) : null;
  if (paid) return paid;
  for (let i = data2.transactions.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
    if (data2.transactions[i].refused !== true) return data2.transactions[i];
  }
  return null;
}
function findTransaction(data2, wixTransactionId) {
  return data2.transactions.find((tx) => tx.wixTransactionId === wixTransactionId) || null;
}
function transactionOfKey(data2, key) {
  if (typeof key !== "string" || !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(key)) return null;
  return data2.transactions.find((tx) => sameSecret(tx.pageKey, key)) || null;
}
function newContext(timeouts) {
  let key;
  let client = null;
  let language = null;
  let site;
  return {
    timeouts,
    async key() {
      if (key === void 0) key = await readSecret(KEY_SECRET);
      return key;
    },
    /** The SDK client; SdkFailure "no_key" when the Secrets Manager has no key. */
    async client() {
      if (!client) {
        const value = await this.key();
        if (!value) throw new SdkFailure("no_key", false, {});
        client = openClient(
          value,
          { relayMs: timeouts.relayMs, httpMs: timeouts.httpMs, rateSources: RATE_SOURCES },
          rememberWarning
        );
      }
      return client;
    },
    /** The merchant's language: the site's (as connectAccount saw it), one of the plugin's. */
    async language() {
      if (!language) {
        const merchant = await readConfig("merchant").catch(() => null);
        language = pickLanguage(
          merchant && merchant.language || await siteLanguage() || "en"
        );
      }
      return language;
    },
    /** The site's URL of HTTP functions ("https://…/_functions"), as its status page saw it. */
    async siteUrl() {
      if (site === void 0) {
        const saved = await readConfig("site").catch(() => null);
        site = saved && typeof saved.baseUrl === "string" ? saved.baseUrl : null;
      }
      return site;
    }
  };
}
function rememberWarning(text) {
  if (!/newer format/i.test(text)) return;
  remember("warning", text, { kind: "sdk", text, at: Date.now() }).catch((error) => {
    logError("could not keep a warning of the SDK", error);
  });
}
async function rememberLightningOnly(data2) {
  logWarning("an invoice was made without an on-chain address: Lightning only");
  await remember("warning", "lightningOnly", {
    kind: "lightningOnly",
    ref: data2.ref,
    at: Date.now()
  }).catch((error) => logError("could not keep a warning", error));
}
function deadline(ms) {
  return AbortSignal.timeout(Math.max(100, ms));
}
var REFUSALS = {
  unsupported_currency: { reasonCode: REASON.currency, errorCode: "CURRENCY_NOT_SUPPORTED" },
  bounds: { reasonCode: REASON.amount, errorCode: "AMOUNT_OUT_OF_BOUNDS" },
  onchain_unavailable: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "ONCHAIN_UNAVAILABLE" },
  revoked: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "INTEGRATION_REMOVED" },
  invalid_key: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "INVALID_KEY" },
  no_key: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "KEY_MISSING" },
  no_site: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "SITE_ADDRESS_UNKNOWN" },
  channel_not_found: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "CHANNEL_NOT_FOUND" },
  newer_format: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "UPDATE_PLUGIN" },
  network: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "TRY_AGAIN" },
  rate: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "TRY_AGAIN" },
  deadline: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "TRY_AGAIN" },
  busy: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "TRY_AGAIN" },
  invalid_invoice: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "INVALID_INVOICE" },
  currency: { reasonCode: REASON.currency, errorCode: "CURRENCY_NOT_SUPPORTED" },
  amount: { reasonCode: REASON.amount, errorCode: "INVALID_AMOUNT" },
  precision: { reasonCode: REASON.amount, errorCode: "AMOUNT_NOT_EXACT" },
  paid: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "ALREADY_PAID" },
  attention: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "NEEDS_MERCHANT" },
  unexpected: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "GENERAL_ERROR" }
};
function reasonOf(failure) {
  const code = failure && failure.code;
  if (code === "invalid_order") {
    const param = failure.detail && failure.detail.param;
    return param === "sats" || param === "fiat" ? "bounds" : "unexpected";
  }
  if (code === "channel_not_found" && failure.detail && failure.detail.newerFormat) {
    return "newer_format";
  }
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(REFUSALS, code) ? code : "unexpected";
}
async function refusal(reason, language, facts, pluginTransactionId) {
  const refused = REFUSALS[reason] || REFUSALS.unexpected;
  const answer = {
    reasonCode: refused.reasonCode,
    errorCode: refused.errorCode,
    errorMessage: t(language, `refusal.${reason}`, facts)
  };
  if (pluginTransactionId) answer.pluginTransactionId = pluginTransactionId;
  logWarning(`payment refused: ${reason} (${refused.errorCode})`);
  const key = `${facts.orderKey || "-"}:${reason}`;
  await remember("refusal", key, {
    at: Date.now(),
    reason,
    errorCode: refused.errorCode,
    currency: facts.currency || null,
    orderId: facts.orderId || null
  }).catch((error) => logError("could not keep a refusal", error));
  return answer;
}
function pageAnswer(tx, pageUrl) {
  return { pluginTransactionId: tx.pluginTransactionId, redirectUrl: pageUrl };
}
function pendingAnswer(tx) {
  tx.sent = "pending";
  tx.sentAt = Date.now();
  return { pluginTransactionId: tx.pluginTransactionId, reasonCode: REASON.pending };
}
function approvedAnswer(data2, tx) {
  tx.sent = "approved";
  tx.sentAt = Date.now();
  data2.paidTx = tx.wixTransactionId;
  expectRecorded(data2, tx.sentAt);
  return { pluginTransactionId: tx.pluginTransactionId };
}
function httpsUrl(value) {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.length <= 2048 && /^https:\/\/[^\s"<>]+$/.test(value) ? value : null;
}
function newPayment(paymentId, orderKey, orderId, now2) {
  return {
    _id: paymentId,
    kind: "payment",
    active: false,
    dueAt: now2,
    data: {
      v: 1,
      orderKey,
      orderId,
      orderNumber: null,
      contactId: null,
      ref: null,
      note: null,
      price: null,
      invoice: null,
      state: "new",
      received: "0",
      rest: null,
      confirmations: null,
      txids: [],
      proof: null,
      attention: null,
      report: null,
      orderChecked: false,
      orderLookups: 0,
      recorded: null,
      transactions: [],
      // Transactions made so far (the list keeps the last 20): the next one's number.
      txCount: 0,
      lockGen: 0,
      checks: 0,
      lastCheck: null,
      lastError: null,
      finalAt: null,
      createdAt: now2,
      updatedAt: now2
    }
  };
}
function addTransaction(data2, options, now2) {
  const urls = options.order && options.order.returnUrls || {};
  const buyer = options.order && options.order.description && options.order.description.buyerInfo;
  data2.txCount = (data2.txCount || 0) + 1;
  const tx = {
    wixTransactionId: options.wixTransactionId,
    // The order's reference and the transaction's number: what Wix shows on its payment line.
    pluginTransactionId: transactionIdOf(data2.ref, data2.txCount),
    n: data2.txCount,
    // The key of this transaction's payment page (http.js): the customer's alone.
    pageKey: randomHex(16),
    returnUrls: {
      successUrl: httpsUrl(urls.successUrl),
      pendingUrl: httpsUrl(urls.pendingUrl),
      errorUrl: httpsUrl(urls.errorUrl),
      cancelUrl: httpsUrl(urls.cancelUrl)
    },
    // The order's language, when the plugin has it; else none: its page follows the browser's.
    language: languageOf(buyer && buyer.buyerLanguage),
    at: now2,
    sent: "none",
    sentAt: null,
    answer: null
  };
  data2.transactions = data2.transactions.concat([tx]).slice(-20);
  return tx;
}
function pageUrlOf(siteUrl, paymentId, tx) {
  return `${siteUrl}/${PAY_FUNCTION}?o=${paymentId}&k=${tx.pageKey}`;
}
function samePrice(a, b) {
  return !!a && !!b && a.currency === b.currency && a.minor === b.minor && a.digits === b.digits;
}
function setAttention(data2, reason, now2, detail) {
  data2.state = "attention";
  data2.attention = { reason, at: now2, detail: detail || {} };
  data2.report = {
    kind: "attention",
    comment: data2.orderId ? "todo" : "none",
    notice: "todo",
    task: "todo",
    tries: 0
  };
  data2.finalAt = now2;
}
function setPaid(data2, now2) {
  data2.state = "paid";
  data2.report = {
    kind: "paid",
    comment: data2.orderId ? "todo" : "none",
    notice: "none",
    task: "none",
    tries: 0
  };
  data2.finalAt = now2;
}
function paidReceived(status, invoice) {
  return status.method === "lightning" ? invoice.sats : status.received;
}
function proofOf(status, now2) {
  const proof = { method: status.method, at: now2 };
  if (status.method === "lightning") proof.preimage = status.preimage;
  if (status.method === "onchain" || status.method === "mixed") {
    proof.received = status.received;
    proof.txids = status.txids.slice(0, 50);
  }
  if (status.method === "mixed") proof.preimages = status.preimages.slice(0, 50);
  return proof;
}
async function answerFromState(data2, tx, price, now2, facts, pageUrl) {
  switch (data2.state) {
    case "paid":
    case "attention":
      Object.assign(tx, { sent: "declined", refused: true });
      return refusal(
        data2.state === "paid" ? "paid" : "attention",
        facts.language,
        facts,
        tx.pluginTransactionId
      );
    case "unconfirmed":
      return pageAnswer(tx, pageUrl);
    case "open":
      if (!samePrice(data2.price, price)) {
        setAttention(data2, "mismatch", now2, { invoice: data2.invoice && data2.invoice.fiat });
        return pendingAnswer(tx);
      }
      return pageAnswer(tx, pageUrl);
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
async function invoiceOf(item, tx, ctx, facts, pageUrl) {
  const data2 = item.data;
  const language = facts.language;
  const order = {
    ref: data2.ref,
    fiat: { amount: data2.price.amount, currency: data2.price.currency },
    note: data2.note,
    durationSeconds: facts.minutesToPay * 60
  };
  const now2 = Date.now();
  try {
    const client = await ctx.client();
    const invoice = await client.createInvoice(order, deadline(ctx.timeouts.deadlineMs));
    if (!invoiceMatches(invoice, data2.price)) {
      data2.invoice = invoice;
      setAttention(data2, "mismatch", now2, { invoice: invoice.fiat });
      Object.assign(tx, { sent: "declined", refused: true });
      return refusal("invalid_invoice", language, facts, tx.pluginTransactionId);
    }
    data2.invoice = invoice;
    data2.state = "open";
    data2.received = "0";
    if (!invoice.onchainAddress && BigInt(invoice.sats) >= 1000n) await rememberLightningOnly(data2);
    return pageAnswer(tx, pageUrl);
  } catch (failure) {
    const code = failure && failure.code;
    const detail = failure && failure.detail || {};
    if (code === "invoice_pending" && detail.invoice) {
      data2.invoice = detail.invoice;
      if (!invoiceMatches(detail.invoice, data2.price)) {
        setAttention(data2, "mismatch", now2, { invoice: detail.invoice.fiat });
        return pendingAnswer(tx);
      }
      data2.state = "open";
      return pageAnswer(tx, pageUrl);
    }
    if (code === "order_paid" && detail.invoice && detail.status) {
      data2.invoice = detail.invoice;
      data2.proof = proofOf(detail.status, now2);
      data2.received = paidReceived(detail.status, detail.invoice);
      if (!invoiceMatches(detail.invoice, data2.price)) {
        setAttention(data2, "mismatch", now2, { invoice: detail.invoice.fiat });
        return pendingAnswer(tx);
      }
      setPaid(data2, now2);
      data2.orderChecked = true;
      return approvedAnswer(data2, tx);
    }
    Object.assign(tx, { sent: "declined", refused: true });
    data2.lastError = { code: code || "unexpected", at: now2 };
    return refusal(reasonOf(failure), language, facts, tx.pluginTransactionId);
  }
}
async function createTransaction(options, ctx) {
  const timing = stopwatch();
  const language = await ctx.language();
  timing.lap("language");
  const wixTransactionId = options && options.wixTransactionId;
  const order = options && options.order || {};
  const description = order.description || {};
  const orderId = typeof order._id === "string" && /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,100}$/.test(order._id) ? order._id : null;
  const currency = typeof description.currency === "string" ? description.currency : "";
  if (typeof wixTransactionId !== "string" || !/^[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,100}$/.test(wixTransactionId)) {
    return refusal("unexpected", language, { currency });
  }
  const orderKey = orderId || `tx:${wixTransactionId}`;
  const credentials = options && options.merchantCredentials || {};
  let minutesToPay = minutesToPayOf(credentials[MINUTES_FIELD]);
  if (minutesToPay === null) {
    logWarning(`the connection's time to pay is not 1 to 60 minutes: ${DEFAULT_MINUTES_TO_PAY}`);
    minutesToPay = DEFAULT_MINUTES_TO_PAY;
  }
  const facts = { orderKey, orderId, currency, language, minutesToPay };
  const priced = priceOf(description.totalAmount, currency, currencyDigits2(currency));
  if (priced.refused) return refusal(priced.refused, language, facts);
  await ensureCollection();
  const paymentId = paymentIdOf(orderKey);
  let item = await readPayment(paymentId);
  const repeated = item && findTransaction(item.data, wixTransactionId);
  if (repeated) logInfo("Wix asked again for a transaction: it gets the same answer");
  if (repeated && repeated.answer) return answerAgain(paymentId, repeated, ctx, language, facts);
  timing.lap("payment");
  if (!await ctx.key()) return refusal("no_key", language, facts);
  const siteUrl = await ctx.siteUrl();
  if (!siteUrl) return refusal("no_site", language, facts);
  const wait = repeated ? REPEAT_WAIT_MS : LOCK_WAIT_MS;
  const taken = await acquire(paymentId, item ? item.data.lockGen : 0, wait, (fresh) => {
    const made = fresh && findTransaction(fresh.data, wixTransactionId);
    return made && made.answer ? { tx: made } : null;
  });
  if (taken.result) return answerAgain(paymentId, taken.result.tx, ctx, language, facts);
  if (!taken.held) return refusal("busy", language, facts);
  const held = taken.held;
  timing.lap("lock");
  let release = "leave";
  try {
    const now2 = Date.now();
    item = taken.item || newPayment(paymentId, orderKey, orderId, now2);
    const data2 = item.data;
    let tx = findTransaction(data2, wixTransactionId);
    if (tx && tx.answer) return await answerAgain(paymentId, tx, ctx, language, facts);
    if (!data2.ref) {
      data2.ref = newRef(orderKey);
      data2.note = noteOf(orderKey);
    }
    if (!tx) tx = addTransaction(data2, options, now2);
    const pageUrl = pageUrlOf(siteUrl, paymentId, tx);
    let answer = await answerFromState(data2, tx, priced.price, now2, facts, pageUrl);
    if (!answer) {
      data2.price = priced.price;
      if (data2.state === "expired") {
        Object.assign(data2, {
          state: "new",
          invoice: null,
          received: "0",
          rest: null,
          confirmations: null,
          txids: [],
          finalAt: null
        });
      }
      data2.lockGen = held.gen;
      await saveHeld(item, held);
      answer = await invoiceOf(item, tx, ctx, facts, pageUrl);
      timing.lap("invoice");
    }
    tx.answer = answer;
    schedule(item, Date.now());
    await eraseIfAsked(item);
    item.data.lockGen = held.gen + 1;
    item.data.updatedAt = Date.now();
    await saveHeld(item, held);
    release = item.active ? "done" : "all";
    timing.lap("records");
    logInfo(`a transaction answered in ${timing.text()}`);
    return answer;
  } catch (error) {
    release = "failed";
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await releaseLock(held, release);
  }
}
async function answerAgain(paymentId, tx, ctx, language, facts) {
  if (!tx.answer.page) return tx.answer;
  const siteUrl = await ctx.siteUrl();
  if (!siteUrl) return refusal("no_site", language, facts);
  return pageAnswer(tx, pageUrlOf(siteUrl, paymentId, tx));
}
var ACQUIRE_TRIES = 4;
async function acquire(paymentId, fromGen, waitMs, settled) {
  const end = Date.now() + waitMs;
  let gen = fromGen;
  for (let tries = 0; tries < ACQUIRE_TRIES; tries += 1) {
    const taken = await lock(paymentId, gen, Math.max(0, end - Date.now()));
    if (taken.busy) return { busy: true };
    let item;
    if (taken.held) {
      item = await readPayment(paymentId);
      if (!item || holdsLatest(taken.held, item.data.lockGen)) {
        return { held: taken.held, item };
      }
      await releaseLock(taken.held, "stale");
    } else {
      item = await readPayment(paymentId);
    }
    const result = settled(item);
    if (result) return { result };
    gen = item ? item.data.lockGen : 0;
  }
  return { busy: true };
}
async function saveHeld(item, held) {
  if (!leaseLeft(held, WRITE_MARGIN_MS)) {
    throw new Error("a payment's lock ran out of time before its record was saved");
  }
  await savePayment(item);
}
function releaseLock(held, how) {
  const ended = how === "done" ? unlock(held) : how === "all" ? dropLocks(held) : how === "stale" ? dropLock(held) : how === "failed" ? abandon(held) : leave(held);
  return ended.catch((error) => logError("could not release a lock", error));
}
function applyStatus(data2, status, now2) {
  switch (status.state) {
    case "open":
      data2.state = "open";
      data2.received = status.received;
      data2.rest = status.rest ? { sats: status.rest.sats, bolt11: status.rest.bolt11, paymentUri: status.rest.paymentUri } : null;
      break;
    case "unconfirmed":
      data2.state = "unconfirmed";
      data2.rest = null;
      data2.received = status.received;
      data2.confirmations = status.confirmations;
      data2.txids = status.txids.slice(0, 50);
      break;
    case "paid": {
      data2.proof = proofOf(status, now2);
      data2.received = paidReceived(status, data2.invoice);
      data2.state = "paid";
      const target = eventTarget(data2);
      data2.paidTx = target ? target.wixTransactionId : null;
      data2.confirmations = null;
      data2.finalAt = now2;
      break;
    }
    case "expired":
      data2.received = status.received;
      if (positive(status.received)) {
        setAttention(data2, "underpaid", now2, {
          received: status.received,
          due: data2.invoice.sats,
          address: data2.invoice.onchainAddress
        });
      } else {
        data2.state = "expired";
        data2.finalAt = now2;
      }
      break;
    default:
      throw new Error(`a payment status of an unknown state: ${status.state}`);
  }
}
function wantedEvent(data2, fresh) {
  switch (data2.state) {
    case "paid":
      return data2.report ? "approved" : null;
    case "expired":
      return "declined";
    case "attention":
      return data2.attention && data2.attention.reason === "unrecorded" ? null : "pending";
    case "unconfirmed":
      return "pending";
    case "open":
      return fresh ? "pending" : null;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}
function mayFollow(sent, wanted) {
  if (!wanted || sent === "approved" || sent === "declined") return false;
  return wanted === "pending" ? sent === "none" : true;
}
function eventOf(tx, wanted) {
  const transaction = {
    wixTransactionId: tx.wixTransactionId,
    pluginTransactionId: tx.pluginTransactionId
  };
  if (wanted === "pending") transaction.reasonCode = REASON.pending;
  if (wanted === "declined") {
    Object.assign(transaction, {
      reasonCode: REASON.expired,
      errorCode: "INVOICE_EXPIRED",
      errorMessage: "The sattle.me invoice expired unpaid."
    });
  }
  return transaction;
}
function keepOrderFacts(data2, order) {
  const number = order.number === void 0 || order.number === null ? "" : String(order.number);
  if (number !== "" && number !== "0") data2.orderNumber = number;
  const contactId = order.buyerInfo && order.buyerInfo.contactId;
  if (typeof contactId === "string" && contactId !== "") data2.contactId = contactId;
}
async function checkOrder2(data2, now2) {
  if (!data2.orderId) {
    setPaid(data2, now2);
    return;
  }
  const looked = await getOrder(data2.orderId);
  if (looked.order) {
    data2.orderChecked = true;
    keepOrderFacts(data2, looked.order);
    const status = (
      /** @type {string} */
      looked.order.status
    );
    if (status === "CANCELED" || status === "REJECTED") {
      setAttention(data2, "canceled", now2, { orderStatus: status });
      return;
    }
    const due = orderDue(looked.order, data2.price);
    if (due && !due.same) {
      logWarning(
        `a paid order's total changed since its invoice (${due.due} ${due.currency} due, ${data2.price.amount} ${data2.price.currency} paid): left to the merchant`
      );
      setAttention(data2, "changed", now2, {
        total: due.total,
        due: due.due,
        currency: due.currency
      });
      return;
    }
    if (!due) logWarning("the total of a paid order could not be read: approved all the same");
    setPaid(data2, now2);
    return;
  }
  data2.orderLookups += 1;
  if (data2.orderLookups >= ORDER_LOOKUPS) {
    logWarning(`the order of a paid payment could not be read (${looked.error}): approved`);
    data2.orderChecked = true;
    setPaid(data2, now2);
  }
}
function expectRecorded(data2, at) {
  if (!data2.orderId) return;
  data2.recorded = { state: "todo", lookups: 0, nextAt: at + VERIFY_AFTER_MS, paymentStatus: null };
}
function isVerifying(data2) {
  return data2.state === "paid" && !!data2.recorded && data2.recorded.state === "todo";
}
async function verifyRecorded(data2, now2) {
  const recorded = data2.recorded;
  recorded.lookups += 1;
  const looked = await getOrder(data2.orderId);
  const order = looked.order;
  const status = order && typeof order.paymentStatus === "string" ? order.paymentStatus.slice(0, 40) : null;
  if (status) recorded.paymentStatus = status;
  if (status !== null && RECORDED.indexOf(status) !== -1) {
    recorded.state = "yes";
    return;
  }
  if (recorded.lookups < VERIFY_LOOKUPS) {
    recorded.nextAt = now2 + VERIFY_EVERY_MS;
    return;
  }
  if (UNRECORDED.indexOf(recorded.paymentStatus) === -1) {
    recorded.state = "unknown";
    const why = recorded.paymentStatus || looked.error || "none";
    logWarning(
      `could not tell whether Wix recorded paid order ${data2.orderId} (its status: ${why})`
    );
    return;
  }
  recorded.state = "no";
  logWarning(
    `Wix did not record paid order ${data2.orderId} as paid (its status: ${recorded.paymentStatus})`
  );
  setAttention(data2, "unrecorded", now2, { paymentStatus: recorded.paymentStatus });
}
async function deliver(item, held, ctx, now2, fresh) {
  const data2 = item.data;
  await settleSending(data2);
  if (data2.state === "paid" && !data2.report && leaseLeft2(held)) await checkOrder2(data2, now2);
  if (isVerifying(data2) && Date.now() >= data2.recorded.nextAt && leaseLeft2(held)) {
    await verifyRecorded(data2, Date.now());
  }
  for (let round = 0; round < 2; round += 1) {
    const report = data2.report;
    const tx = eventTarget(data2);
    const wanted = wantedEvent(data2, fresh);
    const event = tx && mayFollow(tx.sent, wanted) ? wanted : null;
    const steps = report ? STEPS.filter((step) => report[step] === "todo") : [];
    if (!event && !steps.length || !leaseLeft(held, WRITE_MARGIN_MS)) return;
    if (steps.length && data2.orderId && (!data2.orderNumber || !data2.contactId)) {
      const looked = await getOrder(data2.orderId);
      if (looked.order) keepOrderFacts(data2, looked.order);
    }
    if (steps.length) await eraseIfAsked(item);
    const at = Date.now();
    if (tx && event) Object.assign(tx, { sending: event, sendingAt: at });
    for (const step of steps) if (report) report[step] = "sending";
    if (report && steps.length) report.sendingAt = at;
    data2.lockGen = held.gen;
    await saveHeld(item, held);
    if (tx && event) await sendEvent(data2, tx, event, held);
    if (report && steps.length) await sendReport(data2, report, steps, held, ctx);
    if (data2.report === report) return;
  }
}
async function sendEvent(data2, tx, wanted, held) {
  if (!leaseLeft2(held)) return;
  try {
    await submitTransactionEvent(eventOf(tx, wanted));
    tx.sent = wanted;
    tx.sentAt = Date.now();
    if (wanted === "approved") expectRecorded(data2, tx.sentAt);
  } catch (error) {
    data2.lastError = { code: "submit_event", at: Date.now() };
    logError("Wix refused an event of a transaction", error);
    if (wanted === "approved") refusedApproval(data2, tx, Date.now());
  }
  tx.sending = null;
  tx.sendingAt = null;
}
function refusedApproval(data2, tx, now2) {
  tx.failures = (tx.failures || 0) + 1;
  if (!tx.failedAt) tx.failedAt = now2;
  if (tx.failures < APPROVAL_TRIES || now2 - tx.failedAt < APPROVAL_PATIENCE_MS) return;
  logWarning(
    `Wix refused the approval of paid order ${data2.orderId || "-"} ${tx.failures} times: given up, the merchant is told`
  );
  setAttention(data2, "unrecorded", now2, { approval: "refused" });
}
function awaitingWix(data2) {
  if (data2.state !== "paid") return false;
  const tx = eventTarget(data2);
  return !!tx && tx.sent !== "approved" && tx.sending !== "approved";
}
async function sendReport(data2, report, steps, held, ctx) {
  const language = await ctx.language();
  const text = reportText(data2, language);
  report.tries += 1;
  for (const step of steps) {
    if (!leaseLeft2(held)) continue;
    try {
      if (step === "comment") {
        await addOrderComment(data2.orderId, text);
      } else if (step === "notice") {
        const siteUrl = await ctx.siteUrl();
        const link = siteUrl ? { title: t(language, "notice.open"), url: `${siteUrl}/${STATUS_FUNCTION}` } : null;
        const body = `${noticeText(data2, language)} ${t(language, "notice.details")}`;
        await notifyOwner(t(language, "notice.title"), body, link);
      } else {
        await createTask(
          taskTitle(data2, language),
          taskDescription(data2, language),
          data2.contactId
        );
      }
      report[step] = "done";
      if (report.errors) delete report.errors[step];
    } catch (error) {
      logError(STEP_FAILURES[step], error);
      report.errors = Object.assign({}, report.errors, { [step]: failureText(error) });
      if (report.tries >= TRIES) report[step] = "failed";
      else if (step === "notice" || refusedOutright(error)) report[step] = "todo";
    }
  }
}
function refusedOutright(error) {
  const described = describeError(error);
  return /\bINVALID_ARGUMENT\b/.test(`${described.code || ""} ${described.message}`);
}
function failureText(error) {
  const described = describeError(error);
  const text = described.message || described.code || described.name;
  return clip(redact(text).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(), 160);
}
var STEP_FAILURES = {
  comment: "could not comment the order",
  notice: "could not notify the site's owner",
  task: "could not make the merchant's task"
};
async function settleSending(data2) {
  for (const tx of data2.transactions) {
    if (!tx.sending) continue;
    logWarning(`an event of a transaction may have reached Wix: counted as sent (${tx.sending})`);
    tx.sent = tx.sending;
    tx.sentAt = tx.sendingAt || Date.now();
    tx.sending = null;
    tx.sendingAt = null;
    if (tx.sent === "approved") expectRecorded(data2, tx.sentAt);
  }
  const report = data2.report;
  if (!report) return;
  if (report.notice === "sending") report.notice = "done";
  for (const step of ["comment", "task"]) {
    if (report[step] !== "sending") continue;
    try {
      const made = step === "comment" ? await commentMade(data2) : await taskMade(data2, (report.sendingAt || Date.now()) - TASK_LOOKBACK_MS);
      report[step] = made ? "done" : "todo";
    } catch (error) {
      logError(`could not tell whether a ${step} was made`, error);
      report.errors = Object.assign({}, report.errors, { [step]: failureText(error) });
      report.tries += 1;
      if (report.tries >= TRIES) report[step] = "failed";
    }
  }
}
function isReportOf(text, data2) {
  if (typeof text !== "string" || typeof data2.ref !== "string" || !text.includes(data2.ref)) {
    return false;
  }
  const key = data2.attention ? `report.${data2.attention.reason}` : "report.paid";
  const first = text.split("\n")[0];
  return LANGUAGES.some((language) => {
    const source = textOf(key, language);
    return typeof source === "string" && first.startsWith(source.split("{")[0]);
  });
}
async function commentMade(data2) {
  const looked = await getOrder(data2.orderId);
  if (!looked.order) throw new Error(`the order could not be read (${looked.error})`);
  return orderComments(looked.order).some((message) => isReportOf(message, data2));
}
async function taskMade(data2, since) {
  const made = await tasksSince(since);
  return made.some((task) => {
    const first = typeof task.description === "string" ? task.description.split("\n")[0] : "";
    return LANGUAGES.some((language) => first === t(language, "report.ref", { ref: data2.ref }));
  });
}
function reportLeft(report) {
  return STEPS.some((step) => report[step] === "todo" || report[step] === "sending");
}
function leaseLeft2(held) {
  return leaseLeft(held, MARGIN_MS);
}
var METHODS = { lightning: "Lightning", onchain: "on-chain", mixed: "on-chain + Lightning" };
function transactionUrl(txid) {
  return /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(txid) ? `https://mempool.space/tx/${txid}` : String(txid);
}
function reportFacts(data2, language) {
  const address = data2.invoice && data2.invoice.onchainAddress ? data2.invoice.onchainAddress : "";
  return {
    ref: data2.ref,
    number: data2.orderNumber || data2.note || data2.ref,
    method: data2.proof ? METHODS[data2.proof.method] || data2.proof.method : "",
    received: data2.received,
    // What came of a paid payment (paidReceived), never the invoice's amount for an on-chain one.
    came: data2.received,
    due: data2.invoice ? data2.invoice.sats : "",
    address: data2.erased ? t(language, "report.erasedValue") : address,
    price: data2.price ? `${data2.price.amount} ${data2.price.currency}` : "",
    sats: data2.invoice ? data2.invoice.sats : "",
    // An order's total that changed after its invoice: as Wix gave it.
    total: data2.attention && data2.attention.detail && data2.attention.detail.total ? `${data2.attention.detail.total} ${data2.attention.detail.currency}` : "",
    invoicePrice: data2.attention && data2.attention.detail && data2.attention.detail.invoice ? `${data2.attention.detail.invoice.amount} ${data2.attention.detail.invoice.currency}` : "sats"
  };
}
function fitLines(lines, max) {
  let reserved = lines.reduce((sum, line) => sum + (line.essential ? line.text.length + 1 : 0), 0);
  const kept = [];
  let used = 0;
  let full = false;
  for (const line of lines) {
    const separator = kept.length ? 1 : 0;
    if (line.essential) {
      reserved -= line.text.length + 1;
    } else if (full) {
      continue;
    } else {
      const room = max - used - separator - reserved;
      if (line.text.length > room) {
        full = true;
        if (room < MIN_CUT) continue;
        kept.push(clip(line.text, room));
        used += separator + kept[kept.length - 1].length;
        continue;
      }
    }
    kept.push(line.text);
    used += separator + line.text.length;
  }
  return clip(kept.join("\n"), max);
}
function taskTitle(data2, language) {
  if (!data2.attention) return clip(noticeText(data2, language), LIMITS.taskTitle);
  return clip(
    t(language, `task.${data2.attention.reason}`, reportFacts(data2, language)),
    LIMITS.taskTitle
  );
}
function taskDescription(data2, language) {
  const facts = reportFacts(data2, language);
  const lines = [
    { text: t(language, "report.ref", facts), essential: true },
    { text: t(language, "task.order", { orderId: data2.orderId || "\u2013" }), essential: true }
  ];
  const detail = data2.attention && data2.attention.detail || {};
  if (detail.received && detail.due) {
    lines.push({ text: t(language, "report.receivedOf", detail) });
    if (detail.address) lines.push({ text: t(language, "report.address", detail) });
  }
  const report = reportLines(data2, language);
  for (const line of report.slice(1)) if (!line.ref) lines.push({ text: line.text });
  if (report.length) lines.push({ text: report[0].text });
  return fitLines(lines, LIMITS.taskDescription);
}
function reportText(data2, language) {
  return fitLines(reportLines(data2, language), LIMITS.comment);
}
function reportLines(data2, language) {
  const facts = reportFacts(data2, language);
  const lines = [];
  if (data2.report && data2.report.kind === "paid") {
    lines.push(t(language, "report.paid", facts));
  } else if (data2.attention) {
    lines.push(t(language, `report.${data2.attention.reason}`, facts));
  }
  const invoice = data2.invoice;
  if (invoice && invoice.sats && data2.price) {
    lines.push(t(language, "report.amount", { sats: invoice.sats, price: facts.price }));
    const rate = invoice.rate;
    if (rate && rate.price && rate.currency && rate.source) {
      const values = { rate: rate.price, currency: rate.currency, source: rate.source };
      lines.push(t(language, "report.rate", values));
    }
  }
  if (data2.proof) {
    lines.push(t(language, "report.method", facts));
    if (data2.proof.preimage)
      lines.push(`${t(language, "report.preimage")}: ${data2.proof.preimage}`);
    if (data2.proof.preimages && data2.proof.preimages.length) {
      lines.push(`${t(language, "report.preimages")}: ${data2.proof.preimages.join(", ")}`);
    }
    const onchain = data2.proof.method === "onchain" || data2.proof.method === "mixed";
    if (!data2.erased && facts.address && onchain) {
      lines.push(t(language, "report.address", facts));
    }
    if (data2.proof.txids && data2.proof.txids.length) {
      const links = data2.proof.txids.map(transactionUrl).join(", ");
      lines.push(`${t(language, "report.txids")}: ${links}`);
    }
    if (data2.proof.received)
      lines.push(t(language, "report.received", { received: data2.proof.received }));
    const due = data2.invoice && data2.invoice.sats;
    if (data2.proof.received && due && BigInt(data2.proof.received) < BigInt(due)) {
      lines.push(t(language, "report.accepted", { received: data2.proof.received, due }));
    }
  }
  const fitted = lines.map((text, index) => ({ text, essential: index === 0 }));
  if (data2.erased) fitted.splice(1, 0, { text: t(language, "report.erased"), essential: true });
  fitted.push({ text: t(language, "report.ref", facts), essential: true, ref: true });
  return fitted;
}
function noticeText(data2, language) {
  const reason = data2.attention ? data2.attention.reason : "paid";
  return t(language, `notice.${reason}`, { number: data2.orderNumber || data2.note || data2.ref });
}
function schedule(item, now2) {
  const data2 = item.data;
  item.dueAt = now2;
  item.unpaid = false;
  if (data2.state === "new") {
    item.active = false;
    item.unpaid = true;
    return;
  }
  if (!isFinal2(data2)) {
    item.active = true;
    return;
  }
  const tx = eventTarget(data2);
  const toTell = !!tx && mayFollow(tx.sent, wantedEvent(data2)) || !!data2.report && reportLeft(data2.report) || data2.state === "paid" && !data2.report;
  const verifying = isVerifying(data2);
  const tooLong = now2 - (data2.finalAt || now2) > GIVE_UP_MS;
  if (tooLong && toTell) logWarning("a payment's events could not be delivered for 90 days");
  item.active = (toTell || verifying) && !tooLong;
  if (verifying && !toTell) item.dueAt = Math.max(now2, data2.recorded.nextAt);
  item.unpaid = !item.active && endedUnpaid(data2);
}
function endedUnpaid(data2) {
  return data2.state === "expired" && !positive(data2.received) || data2.state === "new";
}
function checkFailureReason(failure) {
  const message = failure && failure.detail ? failure.detail.message : describeError(failure).message;
  return typeof message === "string" ? redact(message).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 300) : "";
}
function checkedWithin(data2, ms, now2) {
  if (!ms || typeof data2.lastCheck !== "number") return false;
  const age = now2 - data2.lastCheck;
  return age >= 0 && age < ms;
}
async function checkPayment(paymentId, fromGen, ctx, { waitMs = 0, minIntervalMs = 0 } = {}) {
  const needsNone = (item2) => !item2 || !item2.active || checkedWithin(item2.data, minIntervalMs, Date.now()) ? { item: item2 } : null;
  const taken = await acquire(paymentId, fromGen, waitMs, needsNone);
  if (taken.result) return taken.result;
  if (!taken.held) return { busy: true, item: null };
  const held = taken.held;
  const item = taken.item;
  let release = "leave";
  try {
    if (needsNone(item)) return { item };
    const data2 = item.data;
    let now2 = Date.now();
    let fresh = false;
    data2.lastCheck = now2;
    if (!isFinal2(data2) && data2.invoice) {
      data2.checks += 1;
      try {
        const client = await ctx.client();
        const ms = Math.min(ctx.timeouts.deadlineMs, held.until - now2 - WRITE_MARGIN_MS);
        applyStatus(data2, await client.getStatus(data2.invoice, deadline(ms)), Date.now());
        data2.lastError = null;
        fresh = true;
      } catch (failure) {
        data2.lastError = { code: failure && failure.code || "unexpected", at: Date.now() };
        const reason = checkFailureReason(failure);
        logWarning(`a payment check failed: ${data2.lastError.code}${reason ? ` (${reason})` : ""}`);
      }
      now2 = Date.now();
      const end = (data2.invoice.expiresAt + GRACE_SECONDS) * 1e3;
      if (!isFinal2(data2) && now2 - end > GIVE_UP_MS) setAttention(data2, "stale", now2, {});
    }
    await deliver(item, held, ctx, Date.now(), fresh);
    schedule(item, Date.now());
    await eraseIfAsked(item);
    data2.lockGen = held.gen + 1;
    data2.updatedAt = Date.now();
    await saveHeld(item, held);
    release = item.active ? "done" : "all";
    return { item, checked: true };
  } catch (error) {
    release = "failed";
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await releaseLock(held, release);
  }
}
function finalTarget(data2, tx) {
  const urls = tx.returnUrls;
  const pending = urls.pendingUrl || urls.successUrl;
  if (data2.attention && data2.attention.reason === "unrecorded") return urls.successUrl;
  switch (data2.state) {
    case "paid":
      return urls.successUrl;
    case "expired":
      return urls.errorUrl || urls.cancelUrl || pending;
    default:
      return pending;
  }
}
function erasePayment(data2, now2, anonymize) {
  data2.contactId = null;
  data2.txids = [];
  data2.rest = null;
  if (data2.invoice) {
    Object.assign(data2.invoice, {
      onchainAddress: null,
      paymentUri: null,
      bolt11: null,
      verifyUrl: null,
      checkoutUrl: null,
      redirectUrl: null
    });
  }
  if (data2.proof && data2.proof.txids) data2.proof.txids = [];
  if (data2.attention && data2.attention.detail && data2.attention.detail.address) {
    data2.attention.detail.address = null;
  }
  for (const tx of data2.transactions) {
    if (tx.answer && typeof tx.answer.redirectUrl === "string") {
      tx.answer = { pluginTransactionId: tx.answer.pluginTransactionId, page: true };
    }
  }
  data2.erased = { at: now2, anonymize: !!anonymize };
}
async function eraseIfAsked(item) {
  const data2 = item.data;
  if (!isFinal2(data2) || data2.erased || typeof data2.contactId !== "string") return;
  const erasure = await recall("erasure", data2.contactId);
  if (erasure) erasePayment(data2, Date.now(), erasure.anonymize);
}
async function changePayment(paymentId, change, { waitMs = 5e3 } = {}) {
  const before = await readPayment(paymentId);
  if (!before) return { missing: true };
  const taken = await acquire(
    paymentId,
    before.data.lockGen,
    waitMs,
    (item) => item ? null : { missing: true }
  );
  if (taken.result) return taken.result;
  if (!taken.held) return { busy: true };
  const held = taken.held;
  let release = "leave";
  try {
    const item = taken.item;
    if (!item) return { missing: true };
    const changed = change(item.data) === true;
    const wasErased = !!item.data.erased;
    await eraseIfAsked(item);
    const erased = !wasErased && !!item.data.erased;
    item.data.lockGen = held.gen + 1;
    if (changed || erased) item.data.updatedAt = Date.now();
    await saveHeld(item, held);
    release = item.active ? "done" : "all";
    return { changed, erased, item };
  } catch (error) {
    release = "failed";
    throw error;
  } finally {
    await releaseLock(held, release);
  }
}
var CONNECT_FAILURES = {
  no_key: { reasonCode: REASON.authorization, errorCode: "KEY_MISSING" },
  invalid_key: { reasonCode: REASON.authorization, errorCode: "INVALID_KEY" },
  revoked: { reasonCode: REASON.accountInvalid, errorCode: "INTEGRATION_REMOVED" },
  newer_format: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "UPDATE_PLUGIN" },
  channel_not_found: { reasonCode: REASON.accountInvalid, errorCode: "CHANNEL_NOT_FOUND" },
  network: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "TRY_AGAIN" },
  deadline: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "TRY_AGAIN" },
  unexpected: { reasonCode: REASON.general, errorCode: "GENERAL_ERROR" }
};
var SECRET_NAME_FIELD = "keySecretName";
var MINUTES_FIELD = "minutesToPay";
function minutesToPayOf(value) {
  if (value === void 0 || value === null) return DEFAULT_MINUTES_TO_PAY;
  if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  const text = value.trim();
  if (text === "") return DEFAULT_MINUTES_TO_PAY;
  if (!/^[0-9]{1,2}$/.test(text)) return null;
  const minutes = Number(text);
  return minutes >= MIN_MINUTES_TO_PAY && minutes <= MAX_MINUTES_TO_PAY ? minutes : null;
}
function stopwatch() {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  let last = startedAt;
  const steps = [];
  return {
    lap(name) {
      const now2 = Date.now();
      steps.push(`${name} ${now2 - last}`);
      last = now2;
    },
    text() {
      return `${Date.now() - startedAt} ms (${steps.join(", ")})`;
    }
  };
}
async function connectRefused(reasonCode, errorCode, errorMessage, timing) {
  logWarning(`connection refused: ${errorCode}, after ${timing.text()}`);
  await ensureCollection().then(() => saveConfig("connection", { refusedAt: Date.now(), errorCode })).catch((error) => logError("could not keep a refused connection", error));
  return { reasonCode, errorCode, errorMessage };
}
async function connectAccount(options, ctx) {
  const timing = stopwatch();
  const language = await ctx.language();
  timing.lap("language");
  const credentials = options && options.credentials || {};
  const typed = typeof credentials[SECRET_NAME_FIELD] === "string" ? credentials[SECRET_NAME_FIELD].trim() : "";
  if (typed.startsWith("sattleme_ck_")) {
    return connectRefused(
      REASON.authorization,
      "KEY_TYPED",
      t(language, "connect.keyTyped"),
      timing
    );
  }
  if (typed !== KEY_SECRET) {
    return connectRefused(
      REASON.authorization,
      "SECRET_NAME",
      t(language, "connect.secretName"),
      timing
    );
  }
  const minutesToPay = minutesToPayOf(credentials[MINUTES_FIELD]);
  if (minutesToPay === null) {
    return connectRefused(REASON.general, "MINUTES_TO_PAY", t(language, "connect.minutes"), timing);
  }
  const currency = options && typeof options.currency === "string" ? options.currency : null;
  if (currency && (wixDigits(currency) === null || currencyDigits2(currency) === null)) {
    return connectRefused(
      REASON.accountCurrency,
      "CURRENCY_NOT_SUPPORTED",
      t(language, "connect.currency", { currency }),
      timing
    );
  }
  if (!await ctx.siteUrl()) {
    return connectRefused(
      REASON.general,
      "SITE_ADDRESS_UNKNOWN",
      t(language, "connect.noSite"),
      timing
    );
  }
  try {
    const client = await ctx.client();
    timing.lap("key");
    const merchant = await client.merchant(deadline(ctx.timeouts.deadlineMs));
    timing.lap("merchant");
    const identity = client.identity();
    await saveConfig("merchant", {
      businessName: merchant.businessName,
      minSats: merchant.minSats,
      maxSats: merchant.maxSats,
      onchainAddresses: merchant.onchainAddresses,
      teamPublicKey: identity.teamPublicKey,
      relays: identity.relays,
      minutesToPay,
      language: pickLanguage(await siteLanguage() || language),
      connectedAt: Date.now()
    });
    timing.lap("records");
    logInfo(`connected in ${timing.text()}`);
    return {
      credentials: { [SECRET_NAME_FIELD]: KEY_SECRET, [MINUTES_FIELD]: String(minutesToPay) },
      accountId: identity.teamPublicKey,
      accountName: tn(language, "connect.accountName", merchant.onchainAddresses, {
        business: clip(merchant.businessName, 60),
        min: merchant.minSats,
        max: merchant.maxSats,
        addresses: String(merchant.onchainAddresses)
      })
    };
  } catch (failure) {
    const reason = reasonOf(failure);
    const failed2 = CONNECT_FAILURES[reason] || CONNECT_FAILURES.unexpected;
    const messageKey = CONNECT_FAILURES[reason] ? reason : "unexpected";
    return connectRefused(
      failed2.reasonCode,
      failed2.errorCode,
      t(language, `connect.${messageKey}`),
      timing
    );
  }
}

// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/http.js
var PAGE_CHECK_EVERY_MS = 5e3;
var PAGE_LOCK_WAIT_MS = 2e3;
function header(request, name) {
  const headers = request && request.headers || {};
  for (const key of Object.keys(headers)) {
    if (key.toLowerCase() === name) return String(headers[key]).slice(0, 500);
  }
  return null;
}
function html(make, body) {
  return make({ headers: HTML_HEADERS, body });
}
function invalidLink(request) {
  const language = acceptedLanguage(header(request, "accept-language"));
  return html(notFound, messagePage(language, "link.title", "link.invalid"));
}
async function payRequest(request) {
  const query = request && request.query || {};
  const paymentId = query.o;
  const key = query.k;
  if (!isPaymentId(paymentId) || typeof key !== "string" || !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/.test(key)) {
    return invalidLink(request);
  }
  let item = await readPayment(paymentId).catch(() => null);
  let tx = item ? transactionOfKey(item.data, key) : null;
  if (!tx || !item.data.invoice) return invalidLink(request);
  if (item.active && !checkedWithin(item.data, PAGE_CHECK_EVERY_MS, Date.now())) {
    try {
      const result = await checkPayment(paymentId, item.data.lockGen, newContext(TIMEOUTS.page), {
        waitMs: PAGE_LOCK_WAIT_MS,
        minIntervalMs: PAGE_CHECK_EVERY_MS
      });
      if (result.item) {
        item = result.item;
        tx = transactionOfKey(item.data, key) || tx;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      logError("the check of a payment page failed", error);
    }
  }
  const data2 = item.data;
  if (data2.state === "attention" && data2.attention && data2.attention.reason === "underpaid") {
    const language2 = tx.language || acceptedLanguage(header(request, "accept-language"));
    return html(
      ok,
      underpaidPage(language2, {
        received: data2.received,
        sats: data2.invoice.sats,
        back: tx.returnUrls.pendingUrl || tx.returnUrls.successUrl
      })
    );
  }
  if (isFinal2(data2) && !awaitingWix(data2)) {
    const target = finalTarget(data2, tx);
    if (!target) return invalidLink(request);
    return response({
      status: 303,
      headers: { Location: target, "Cache-Control": "no-store", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer" },
      body: ""
    });
  }
  const language = tx.language || acceptedLanguage(header(request, "accept-language"));
  return html(ok, paymentPage(language, paymentView(data2, tx, paymentId, key)));
}
function paymentView(data2, tx, paymentId, key) {
  const invoice = data2.invoice;
  const now2 = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1e3);
  const rest = data2.state === "open" ? data2.rest : null;
  return {
    paymentId,
    key,
    state: data2.state,
    sats: invoice.sats,
    price: data2.price ? `${data2.price.amount} ${data2.price.currency}` : null,
    secondsLeft: Math.max(0, invoice.expiresAt - now2),
    received: data2.received,
    rest: rest ? { sats: rest.sats, bolt11: rest.bolt11, paymentUri: rest.paymentUri } : null,
    paymentUri: invoice.paymentUri,
    bolt11: invoice.bolt11,
    address: invoice.onchainAddress,
    confirmations: data2.confirmations,
    back: tx.returnUrls.pendingUrl || tx.returnUrls.successUrl
  };
}
function nodeVersion() {
  const version = typeof process === "object" && process && process.version;
  return typeof version === "string" && /^v[0-9]{1,3}(\.[0-9]{1,4}){2}$/.test(version) ? version : "?";
}
function styleRequest() {
  return ok({ headers: STYLE_HEADERS, body: STYLESHEET });
}
async function statusForm() {
  const ctx = newContext(TIMEOUTS.status);
  return html(ok, tokenPage(await ctx.language()));
}
async function statusRequest(request) {
  const ctx = newContext(TIMEOUTS.status);
  const language = await ctx.language();
  const expected = await readSecret(STATUS_TOKEN_SECRET);
  if (!expected || expected.length < 32) {
    return html(forbidden, tokenPage(language, t(language, "status.noToken")));
  }
  let token = null;
  try {
    token = new URLSearchParams(await request.body.text()).get("t");
  } catch (error) {
    return html(badRequest, tokenPage(language, t(language, "status.wrongToken")));
  }
  if (!sameSecret(token, expected)) {
    return html(forbidden, tokenPage(language, t(language, "status.wrongToken")));
  }
  await ensureCollection();
  const site = await keepSiteUrl(request.baseUrl);
  return html(ok, statusPage(language, await statusView(ctx, site)));
}
async function keepSiteUrl(baseUrl) {
  const url = typeof baseUrl === "string" ? baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, "") : "";
  const valid = /^https:\/\/[^\s"'<>?#\\]+\/_functions$/.test(url) && url.length <= 1500;
  if (valid) await saveConfig("site", { baseUrl: url, savedAt: Date.now() });
  return { url: url || null, valid };
}
async function merchantView(ctx) {
  try {
    const client = await ctx.client();
    const merchant = await client.merchant(AbortSignal.timeout(ctx.timeouts.deadlineMs));
    return {
      ok: true,
      business: merchant.businessName,
      min: merchant.minSats,
      max: merchant.maxSats,
      addresses: String(merchant.onchainAddresses)
    };
  } catch (failure) {
    return { ok: false, code: failure && failure.code || "unexpected" };
  }
}
function paymentRow(item) {
  const data2 = item.data;
  return {
    ref: data2.ref,
    state: data2.state,
    received: data2.received,
    due: data2.invoice ? data2.invoice.sats : null,
    address: data2.invoice && data2.invoice.onchainAddress ? data2.invoice.onchainAddress : null,
    expiresAt: data2.invoice ? data2.invoice.expiresAt * 1e3 : null,
    lastCheck: data2.lastCheck,
    error: data2.lastError ? data2.lastError.code : null,
    orderId: data2.orderId,
    reason: data2.attention ? data2.attention.reason : null,
    task: data2.report && data2.report.task ? data2.report.task : null,
    // Why the task failed last, and how many checks tried it (payments.js, sendReport).
    taskError: data2.report && data2.report.errors && typeof data2.report.errors.task === "string" ? data2.report.errors.task : null,
    tries: data2.report && typeof data2.report.tries === "number" ? data2.report.tries : 0,
    at: data2.attention ? data2.attention.at : null
  };
}
async function statusView(ctx, site) {
  const keyPresent = !!await ctx.key();
  const [merchant, connection, connected, runs, active, recent, refusals, warnings, timeZone] = await Promise.all([
    keyPresent ? merchantView(ctx) : Promise.resolve(null),
    readConfig("connection").catch(() => null),
    readConfig("merchant").catch(() => null),
    latest("run", 24),
    activePayments(50),
    latest("payment", 100),
    latest("refusal", 20),
    latest("warning", 10),
    siteTimeZone()
  ]);
  const connectedAt = connected && typeof connected.connectedAt === "number" ? connected.connectedAt : 0;
  const refusedConnection = connection && typeof connection.refusedAt === "number" && connection.refusedAt > connectedAt ? { at: connection.refusedAt, code: String(connection.errorCode) } : null;
  return {
    now: Date.now(),
    versions: { plugin: PLUGIN_VERSION, sdk: SDK_VERSION, spec: SPEC_VERSION, node: nodeVersion() },
    // The site's time zone, which the page's times are in (pages.js).
    timeZone,
    keyPresent,
    merchant,
    // The time to pay of the last connection that succeeded.
    minutesToPay: connected && typeof connected.minutesToPay === "number" ? connected.minutesToPay : null,
    refusedConnection,
    site,
    runs: runs.map((item) => item.data).filter((run) => run && typeof run.startedAt === "number").sort((a, b) => b.startedAt - a.startedAt),
    open: active.filter((item) => !isFinal2(item.data)).map(paymentRow),
    attention: recent.filter((item) => item.data.state === "attention").slice(0, 20).map(paymentRow),
    refusals: refusals.map((item) => item.data),
    warnings: warnings.map((item) => Object.assign({ at: item.dueAt }, item.data))
  };
}

// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/jobs.js
var jobs_exports = {};
__export(jobs_exports, {
  WRITES_PER_MINUTE: () => WRITES_PER_MINUTE,
  runJob: () => runJob,
  sattlemeCheckoutJob: () => sattlemeCheckoutJob
});
var AT_ONCE = 3;
var BATCH = 30;
var MAX_QUERY = 1e3;
var WRITES_PER_MINUTE = 60;
var WRITES_PER_CHECK = 4;
var MINUTE2 = 6e4;
var MARGIN_MS2 = TIMEOUTS.job.deadlineMs + 15e3;
var KEEP_MS = 90 * 24 * 3600 * 1e3;
var UNPAID_KEEP_MS = 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1e3;
var ERASURE_KEEP_MS = 200 * 24 * 3600 * 1e3;
async function checkIfDue(item, startedAt, ctx) {
  const fresh = await readPayment(item._id);
  if (!fresh || !fresh.active || fresh.dueAt > startedAt) return { skipped: true };
  return checkPayment(fresh._id, fresh.data.lockGen, ctx);
}
function pause2(ms) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}
function budget(sleep2) {
  const spent = [];
  let counted = writeCount();
  return {
    spend() {
      const count = writeCount();
      if (count > counted) spent.push({ at: Date.now(), writes: count - counted });
      counted = count;
    },
    async room(n, until) {
      for (; ; ) {
        const now2 = Date.now();
        while (spent.length && spent[0].at <= now2 - MINUTE2) spent.shift();
        const used = spent.reduce((sum, step) => sum + step.writes, 0);
        if (used + n <= WRITES_PER_MINUTE || !spent.length) return true;
        const wait = spent[0].at + MINUTE2 - now2;
        if (now2 + wait > until) return false;
        await sleep2(wait);
      }
    }
  };
}
async function runJob({ lifeMs = JOB_LIFE_MS, marginMs = MARGIN_MS2, sleep: sleep2 = pause2 } = {}) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  const stopAt = startedAt + lifeMs;
  const hour = new Date(startedAt).toISOString().slice(11, 13);
  const run = {
    startedAt,
    endedAt: (
      /** @type {number | null} */
      null
    ),
    checks: 0,
    busy: 0,
    errors: 0,
    left: 0
  };
  const ctx = newContext(TIMEOUTS.job);
  const tried = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
  const writes2 = budget(sleep2);
  let complete = false;
  let stopped = false;
  const timeLeft = () => !stopped && Date.now() + marginMs < stopAt;
  try {
    await ensureCollection();
    await saveRun(hour, run);
    const before = startedAt - KEEP_MS;
    await Promise.all([
      forget("refusal", startedAt - UNPAID_KEEP_MS),
      forget("warning", before),
      forget("payment", before, false),
      forgetUnpaid(startedAt - UNPAID_KEEP_MS),
      forgetLocks(before),
      forget("erasure", startedAt - ERASURE_KEEP_MS)
    ]).catch((error) => logError("could not remove old records", error));
    writes2.spend();
    while (timeLeft() && tried.size < MAX_QUERY) {
      const limit = Math.min(MAX_QUERY, tried.size + BATCH);
      const due = (await duePayments(startedAt, limit)).filter(
        (item) => !tried.has(item._id)
      );
      if (!due.length) {
        complete = true;
        break;
      }
      for (let start = 0; start < due.length && timeLeft(); start += AT_ONCE) {
        const some = due.slice(start, start + AT_ONCE);
        if (!await writes2.room(some.length * WRITES_PER_CHECK + 1, stopAt - marginMs)) {
          stopped = true;
          break;
        }
        for (const item of some) tried.add(item._id);
        const results = await Promise.all(
          some.map((item) => checkIfDue(item, startedAt, ctx).catch((error) => ({ error })))
        );
        for (const result of results) {
          if (result.error) {
            run.errors += 1;
            logError("a background check failed", result.error);
          } else if (result.busy) {
            run.busy += 1;
          } else if (result.checked) {
            run.checks += 1;
          }
        }
        writes2.spend();
      }
    }
    if (!complete) {
      const due = await duePayments(startedAt, MAX_QUERY);
      run.left = due.filter((item) => !tried.has(item._id)).length;
    }
  } catch (error) {
    run.errors += 1;
    logError("the background job stopped", error);
  } finally {
    run.endedAt = Date.now();
    await saveRun(hour, run).catch((error) => logError("could not keep the job's run", error));
  }
}
var sattlemeCheckoutJob = () => runJob();

// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/plugin.js
var plugin_exports = {};
__export(plugin_exports, {
  connectAccount: () => connectAccount2,
  createTransaction: () => createTransaction2,
  refundTransaction: () => refundTransaction
});
function failed(error) {
  logError("the payment plugin failed", error);
  return {
    reasonCode: REASON.general,
    errorCode: "GENERAL_ERROR",
    errorMessage: "Unexpected error."
  };
}
async function connectAccount2(options) {
  try {
    return await connectAccount(options, newContext(TIMEOUTS.connect));
  } catch (error) {
    return failed(error);
  }
}
async function createTransaction2(options) {
  try {
    return await createTransaction(options, newContext(TIMEOUTS.createTransaction));
  } catch (error) {
    return failed(error);
  }
}
async function refundTransaction() {
  return {
    reasonCode: REASON.refund,
    errorCode: "REFUND_NOT_ALLOWED",
    errorMessage: "Refund from your sattle.me app: this plugin cannot send funds."
  };
}

// plugin/site/backend/sattleme-checkout/privacy.js
var privacy_exports = {};
__export(privacy_exports, {
  contactDeleted: () => contactDeleted,
  contactsMerged: () => contactsMerged
});
var TRIES2 = 3;
async function changeEach(ids, change) {
  const counts = { changed: 0, erased: 0, busy: 0, failed: 0 };
  for (const id of ids) {
    let result = { busy: true };
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < TRIES2 && result.busy; attempt += 1) {
      try {
        result = await changePayment(id, change);
      } catch (error) {
        logError("could not change a payment of a deleted or merged contact", error);
        result = { failed: true };
      }
    }
    if (result.changed) counts.changed += 1;
    if (result.erased) counts.erased += 1;
    if (result.busy) counts.busy += 1;
    if (result.failed) counts.failed += 1;
  }
  return counts;
}
async function contactDeleted(event) {
  const metadata = event && event.metadata || {};
  const contactId = typeof metadata.entityId === "string" ? metadata.entityId : "";
  if (contactId === "" || metadata.originatedFrom === "merge") return null;
  const anonymize = metadata.triggeredByAnonymizeRequest === true;
  await ensureCollection();
  await remember("erasure", contactId, { at: Date.now(), anonymize });
  const ids = await paymentIdsOfContact(contactId);
  const counts = await changeEach(ids, () => false);
  logInfo(
    `a contact was deleted: ${counts.erased} of its ${ids.length} payment(s) erased, the others once settled${counts.busy + counts.failed ? ` (${counts.busy + counts.failed} not reached)` : ""}`
  );
  return Object.assign({ payments: ids.length }, counts);
}
async function contactsMerged(event) {
  const merged = event && event.data || {};
  const target = typeof merged.targetContactId === "string" ? merged.targetContactId : "";
  const sources = Array.isArray(merged.sourceContactIds) ? merged.sourceContactIds.filter((id) => typeof id === "string" && id !== "" && id !== target) : [];
  if (target === "" || sources.length === 0) return null;
  await ensureCollection();
  let payments = 0;
  const counts = { changed: 0, erased: 0, busy: 0, failed: 0 };
  for (const source of sources) {
    const ids = await paymentIdsOfContact(source);
    payments += ids.length;
    const result = await changeEach(ids, (data2) => {
      if (data2.contactId !== source) return false;
      data2.contactId = target;
      return true;
    });
    for (const key of Object.keys(counts)) counts[key] += result[key];
  }
  if (payments) {
    logInfo(`contacts were merged: ${counts.changed} payment(s) follow the contact kept`);
  }
  return Object.assign({ payments }, counts);
}
